# Spirit Island FAQ → Turn test scenarios

One unit-test scenario per entry of the official Spirit Island FAQ on Querki (853 entries, fetched 2026-10-11 from <https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Question>). Entry data, with full answers, is in `faq.json`; this file is generated by `faq/build-todo.py`, which keeps the status marks below when re-run.

## Plan

- **One scenario per entry**: `faq/tests/<id>.t`, where `<id>` is the anchor below (e.g. `q-blight-card-flip-order`). It sets up the situation the question describes and ends with `-- expect:` lines stating the answer as tuples that must (or `-- expect-not:` must not) appear.
- **Misconceptions**: `misconceptions/<id>.json`, a wrong reading of the rules as find/replace edits to a rule file; the runner checks that each makes the scenarios in its `caught_by` fail. Design and conventions: `DESIGN.md`.
- **Base files** are named in a header line, e.g. `-- uses: core.t ext-actions.t`; the scenario is concatenated after them, as `run.sh` does.
- **Runner**: `faq/faq.test.ts`, run from `si/` as `node --import tsx faq/faq.test.ts`, runs every scenario and checks its expectations, so each entry is a regression test for the engine and for `core.t`. A tuple matches when its rendered atom (interval dropped) equals the line, at any time in the run. `FAQ=<id>` runs one; `FAQ_DIR=<dir>` runs another folder (e.g. copies pointed at a deliberately broken `core.t`, to check a scenario can fail).
- Many entries need rules that aren't formalized yet (most cards, Adversaries, Scenarios, Aspects). Writing the scenario is also the prompt to add that rule, as a pure addition where possible; an entry that forces a base rewrite is a finding for the report.

Status marks: `[ ]` todo · `[x]` scenario passes · `[~]` partial (some clause untested) · `[!]` scenario written, fails · `[-]` not a rules question, no test (say why).

**Progress:** 3 pass · 0 partial · 0 failing · 0 skipped · 850 todo, of 853.

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g9jv"></a>**Can Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares do real damage (destroying cities, etc) with Power X?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g9jv))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8rd"></a>**Can Spirits May Yet Dream be used to flip any Fear card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8rd))

  > Yes, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares can use the first effect of Spirits May Yet Dream to flip any Fear card. The Fear card could be already earned, on top of the Fear deck, or further down in the Fear deck.

  <br><sub>Core game · Dahan, Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Drowning (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > No. If Dahan are Drowned they are destroyed with no benefit. Thematically, the Energy gained is canceled out by the loss of propitiation from other Dahan.

  > Yes. No ability to move other Spirits' presence existed until Jagged Earth, so the wording doesn't handle that contingency. Apologies! More recent printings of these Spirits have adjusted the wording to reflect the intent.

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pb"></a>**How much energy can Ocean's Hungry Grasp gain for Drowned invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pb))
  <br><sub>Core game · Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Drowning (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > For every X health worth of invaders, Ocean gains 1 energy, where X is the number of players. For instance, in a 4-player game, Ocean can trade 1 Town and 2 Explorers for 1 energy. You can make change as needed. For instance, in a 2-player game, Ocean can trade 1 City for 1 energy and 1 Explorer.

Thematically, the Dahan are actually doing a raid in the real world, it is not just a …

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfh3"></a>**If Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares does shadow damage to a damaged Town, causing Fear and Pushing it, does the damage get healed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfh3))
  <br><sub>Core game · Damage, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Terror Stalks the Land (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > No, Towns and Cities remain at whatever Damage they had taken before Bringer of Dreams and Nightmare used its power.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pj"></a>**If Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares Repeats a Power, can the Repeat "destroy" Invaders that were "destroyed" by the original Power use?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pj))
  <br><sub>Core game · Repeat, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares</sub>
  > Yes. The Repeat is considered a separate action from the original. This also means Bringer's Repeat cannot usefully combine Damage with its original – for instance a Repeated "2 Damage" Power couldn't do anything to an undamaged City. The original action would resolve, and 2 Damage isn't enough to destroy the City, …

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oc6g"></a>**If Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares uses a Power that Damages/Destroys Dahan, what happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oc6g))
  <br><sub>Core game · Dahan, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares)</sub>
  > Nothing happens to the Dahan. Bringer's Powers cannot Destroy anything other than its own Presence, so you ignore that instruction. The rest of the Power takes effect normally. ("General Principle: Do as much as you can", p. 17)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebn"></a>**If Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares uses Infestation of Venomous Spiders and meets the threshold, does it deal real Damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebn))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Core game · Triggered Actions, Infestation of Venomous Spiders, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares)</sub>
  > No. The threshold is a triggered action from Bringer's Power, so obeys To Dream a Thousand Deaths.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geam"></a>**If Finder of Paths Unseen gets Presence in the Ocean, can it make lands Coastal?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geam))

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oc73"></a>**If I Gather/Push/otherwise move 2 Dahan at once, can I move 2 of Thunderspeaker's Presence along with them?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oc73))
  <br><sub>Core game · Move, Dahan, Gather, Push, Ally of the Dahan (Thunderspeaker)</sub>
  > Yes. The special rule says "whenever a Dahan moves ... you may move 1 Presence ...". Had you been limited to a single Presence, the rule would have been phrased, "whenever 1 or more Dahan move ... you may move 1 Presence ...".
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqb"></a>**If Ocean's Presence is moved/destroyed entirely off a board in the middle of an action, when does "treat the Ocean as Coastal Wetlands" expire?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqb))
  <br><sub>Core game · Presence, Actions, Action tree, Ocean In Play (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Ocean's Hungry Grasp</sub>
  > It stays in effect until the end of the action tree (you're not in the middle of an action, and have no triggered actions about to happen).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8r6"></a>**If Shadows Repeats a Power that it range-boosted with Shadows of the Dahan, does it have to pay Energy again?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8r6))

So if a Power Card says "Repeat this Power in an adjacent land", you may (and must) target a land adjacent to the original, rather than choosing a new …

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pf"></a>**What happens if Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares uses its special rule to push a Town into the Ocean on a board where Ocean's Hungry Grasp has presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pf))

  > The Invader is Drowned, by Ocean's Hungry Grasp's special rule. It is Ocean's Hungry Grasp doing the destroying, so that works as normal. - [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefg"></a>**What land does Harbingers of the Lightning generate Fear in?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefg))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear, Harbingers of the Lightning (Lightning's Swift Strike), Dread Apparitions (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Foreboding Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > The target land. Thematically, it should be "one land pushed to which has Town/City", but that's not how the card's written. Feel free to play it the thematic way if your playgroup prefers.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfd7"></a>**When a Spirit uses a special rule to make a sacred site with only one Presence (e.g., River Surges in Sunlight using River's Domain in a Wetland), do they count as having two Presence there?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfd7))
  <br><sub>Core game, Nature Incarnate · Sacred Sites, Counts As, Incarna, River's Domain (River Surges in Sunlight), River Surges in Sunlight, Lair Aspect (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > No, they still just count as having as many Presence as they have disks in the land. Similarly, when an Incarna counts as a Sacred Site, it's still just counts as one Presence. While two Presence is enough to make a land a Sacred Site (as suggested by the iconography), the reverse is not true.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kf34"></a>**When Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares adds Blight, does that destroy Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kf34))
  <br><sub>Core game · Blight, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares)</sub>
  > Yes. Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares can add real Blight, and it's the Blight that destroys the Presence.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pt"></a>**When do you pay the extra Energy for Shadows of the Dahan? ("Whenever you use a Power, you may pay 1 Energy to target a land with Dahan...")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pt))
  <br><sub>Core game · Energy, Targeting, Shadows of the Dahan (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > When you use the Power - i.e., resolve its text effects. You don't have to choose a Power's target until you actually use it.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qy"></a>**When does Ocean's Hungry Grasp get to Drown invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qy))

They do not get drowned when moved in to any other land, and Ocean's Hungry Grasp does not need to have Presence that is actually in the ocean, just on the same board. They …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9g"></a>**When Invaders have increased health, does Ocean's Hungry Grasp get a bonus when exchanging them for Energy?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9g))
  <br><sub>Core game · Health, Kingdom of England, Tight-Knit Communities, Drowning (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Well-Prepared Explorers</sub>
  > No. As the Special Rule says, ignore modifiers to Invader Health when trading Invaders for Energy. This applies to all modifiers, whether permanent (e.g., England Level 5) or temporary (e.g., the Event card Tight-Knit Communities).
- [ ] <a id="q-when-does-swiftness-of-lightning-happen"></a>**When is Swiftness of Lightning used to make Powers Fast?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/when-does-swiftness-of-lightning-happen))
  <br><sub>Core game · Speed (Fast/Slow), Swiftness of Lightning (Lightning's Swift Strike), Blitz</sub>
  > During the Fast phase. This is rarely relevant, but can matter for the Blitz Scenario: you can't "make a Power Fast" during the Spirit Phase to lower its cost by 1, only "make a Power Fast" during the Fast Phase to gain a 1-Energy rebate.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8rn"></a>**When Ocean's Hungry Grasp is in play against Brandenburg-Prussia, can the extra Towns from the Stage II Escalation be placed in the Ocean (and drowned immediately)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8rn))

  > No. The Ocean is only a land for the purpose of Powers and Blight, and not, for instance, for Events, Fear cards, or Stage II escalation effects.
- [ ] <a id="q-When-OHG-is-in-play-can-other-pieces-end-up-in-the-Ocean"></a>**When Ocean's Hungry Grasp is in play, can Blight, other Spirit Presence, Beasts, or other tokens end up in the Ocean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/When-OHG-is-in-play-can-other-pieces-end-up-in-the-Ocean))

Spirit Presence from other spirits cannot be placed there during the Spirit Phase, but can end up there as a result of cards like "Indomitable Claim" or "Flow Like Water, Reach Like Air." So …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kbka"></a>**When Ocean's Hungry Grasps drowns a Town or a City, is the Fear earned immediately?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kbka))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gdtk"></a>**When Oceans are in play, can Invader or Dahan pieces remain in the Ocean if they avoid Drowning?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gdtk))

In the base game, the edge-case where this can happen is if Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares uses …
- [ ] <a id="q-range-through-ocean"></a>**When Ocean’s Hungry Grasp is on a board, can Powers and Special Rules measure Range through that board’s Ocean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/range-through-ocean))

  > Yes, Powers and Special Rules can treat the Ocean as a land via Ocean in Play, so they can measure Range through it. - [ ] <a id="q-9v5kbmn"></a>**When playing against England, can A Spread of Rampant Green prevent the escalation effect or High Immigration Builds?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kbmn))

  > Yes. Those are both Builds, so A Spread of Rampant Green's special ability Choke the Land with Green can prevent them. England can build multiple times in the same land, and A Spread of Rampant Green would need to decide each time whether to sacrifice a Presence to prevent it.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odcf"></a>**With Ocean's Hungry Grasp, what does it mean that "Oceans are treated as Coastal Wetlands for Blight" *(in addition to powers)*?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odcf))

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6offr"></a>**Can Fear cards move Dahan to lands where Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds has a Sacred Site?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6offr))

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka97"></a>**Can the Event card "Outpaced" be used to resolve a power that has been made fast by Lightning's Swift Strike's special rule?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka97))

- [ ] <a id="q-offer-no-firm-foundations-against-kingdom-of-france"></a>**Does Mud’s Offer No Firm Foundations special rule do anything useful against The Kingdom of France Level 2+?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/offer-no-firm-foundations-against-kingdom-of-france))

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfrq"></a>**How does Mists Shift and Flow interact with a Power targeting two lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfrq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Targeting, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist, Fire and Flood</sub>

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g9og"></a>**If a Spirit reveals two 'Reclaim 1' spaces on their Presence tracks, can they Reclaim two cards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g9og))

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2o"></a>**What counts as "creating a Sacred Site" for the special rule of Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2o))

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odee"></a>**What does "-X Cost" mean on a Growth option or Power threshold?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odee))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8oj"></a>**What happens if Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares uses the threshold effect of Cast Down into the Briny Deep?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8oj))

- [ ] <a id="q-impending-cards-with-unlock-the-gates-of-deepest-power"></a>**When Dances Up Earthquakes plays a Major Power via Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, what happens if you make that Power Card impending?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/impending-cards-with-unlock-the-gates-of-deepest-power))

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka93"></a>**With Sweep into the Sea, can you push Invaders into the Ocean on a board where Ocean's Hungry Grasp has presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka93))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef4"></a>**Can a Spirit like Finder get its Presence in an in-play Ocean using "Move 1 Presence" on a growth track?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef4))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8nj"></a>**How does Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island work in a solo game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nj))

- [ ] <a id="q-computing-energy-and-plays-with-unusual-tracks"></a>**If a Spirit has an unusually-shaped Presence Track, how does it determine the number of Energy/Plays it has access to each turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/computing-energy-and-plays-with-unusual-tracks))

- [ ] <a id="q-incarna-going-to-presence-tracks-or-deep-slumber-track"></a>**What happens when an Incarna would go to place off the island that only Presence goes, like being returned to a Presence Track by "Numinious Crisis" or moved to Serpent's "Deep Slumber" track by "Absorb Essence"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/incarna-going-to-presence-tracks-or-deep-slumber-track))

- [ ] <a id="q-abducting-presence-and-blazing-presence"></a>**What happens when Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine abducts Presence belonging to Heart of the Wildfire?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/abducting-presence-and-blazing-presence))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g9ki"></a>**What is the strange icon on the special rule for Heart of the Wildfire?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g9ki))

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ocxw"></a>**When Heart of the Wildfire places Presence, does it immediately get the effect of any newly-revealed Fire element?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ocxw))

- [ ] <a id="q-Powers-ineligible-for-Gift-of-Wind-Sped-Steps"></a>**Are there any Powers that use the words "Push" or "Gather" that Gift of Wind-Sped Steps can't make Fast?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Powers-ineligible-for-Gift-of-Wind-Sped-Steps))

In the Jagged Earth expansion, it can't affect Gather the Scattered Light of Stars — while that Power Card has "Gather" in its name, it doesn't have a Push/Gather instruction as part of its effect. (Other expansions have similar effects that check for specific instructions.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee8"></a>**As Volcano, when destroying your own Presence for "Explosive Eruption," can that Presence be saved (by, e.g., "Flowing and Silent Forms Dart By")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee8))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Saved From Destruction, Action Modifiers, Presence, Destroy, Changed rulings, Explosive Eruption (Volcano Looming High)</sub>
  > Yes, but not usefully. It doesn't count towards the eruption threshold requirements / effects (because they check the # of Presence actually destroyed) or for "Collapse In a Blast of Lava and Steam" (because "destroy" gets replaced with "push" before the rule "when your Presence is destroyed, deal damage" kicks in). …
- [ ] <a id="q-can-bringer-of-dreams-and-nightmares-benefit-from-badlands"></a>**Can Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares' Powers benefit from Badlands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-bringer-of-dreams-and-nightmares-benefit-from-badlands))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Flame's Fury (Heart of the Wildfire), Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot)</sub>
  > Yes. This isn't necessarily obvious: To Dream a Thousand Deaths affects the instructions of Bringer's powers (see FAQ), and unlike most damage modifiers, Badlands applies when the results of an action deal Damage (otherwise "Badlands don't apply if Damage is reduced to 0" makes no sense). So on first blush, Badlands …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geas"></a>**Can Dahan be moved into a land affected by Stubborn Solidity?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geas))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Dahan, Stubborn Solidity (Stone's Unyielding Defiance)</sub>
  > Yes. Once there, they are affected by Stubborn Solidity and cannot be moved out. This will not change the amount of Defense provided.
- [ ] <a id="q-dahan-trust-the-watchers-with-encompassing-ward"></a>**Can Eyes use its Dahan Trust the Watchers special rule to Gather Dahan when targeting a Spirit with Encompassing Ward?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dahan-trust-the-watchers-with-encompassing-ward))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Defend, Encompassing Ward, Dahan Trust the Watchers (Eyes Watch From the Trees), Eyes Watch From the Trees, Travel Aspect (River Surges in Sunlight), Ward the Shores</sub>
  > No. Even if all of target Spirit's Presence is in one land, the Power doesn't add Defend to the land, but rather grants the Spirit a new effect.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef7"></a>**Can Finder of Paths Unseen make two lands adjacent for **all** purposes?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef7))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Finder of Paths Unseen, Open the Ways (Finder of Paths Unseen)</sub>
  > Yes. In particular, any Spirit can target range 2 or range 3 Powers bridging over Finder's "adjacent" lands, even if neither land is the origin or destination of the Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebz"></a>**Can Lure of the Deep Wilderness win the game in Terror Level 1 by using Enthrall the Foreign Explorers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebz))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Victory and Defeat, Changed rulings, Enthrall the Foreign Explorers (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > Changed answer: No. Lure's ability effectively means "do not participate in Ravage", and pieces not participating in Ravage aren't ignored for Victory / Defeat. It would be better phrased as, "For each of your Presence in a land, up to 2 Explorer do not participate in Ravage." See also Pieces that "do not participate …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geaq"></a>**Can Mists Shift and Flow let you meet origin land targeting requirements? (E.g., targeting from a Jungle, or from a Sacred Site)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geaq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Targeting, Sacred Sites, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist</sub>
  > Yes. (JE, p. 31)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefh"></a>**Can Shifting Memory of Ages spend element markers to give other Spirits elements?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefh))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Elements, Element markers, Prepare, Shifting Memory of Ages, Insights Into the World's Nature (Shifting Memory of Ages), Elemental Teachings (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Only by using their Unique Power "Elemental Teachings." The normal "gain an Element for 1 action" benefit is for Shifting Memory only.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebh"></a>**Can Stone's Unyielding Defiance use Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will (to take Blight from the box) even if the Blight being added will destroy their Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebh))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Blight, Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will (Stone's Unyielding Defiance)</sub>
  > Yes.
- [ ] <a id="q-do-healing-markers-grant-elements"></a>**Can the Healing Markers claimed by Wounded Waters Bleeding be used for anything other than checking which Healing Markers you have? (e.g., do they grant Elements?)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/do-healing-markers-grant-elements))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Prepare, Element markers, Elements, Claim, Starlight Seeks Its Form, Shifting Memory of Ages</sub>
  > No, Healing Markers are just Healing Markers; they don't do anything else associated with Elements or Element Markers. Element Markers don't inherently do anything; they're just used to track effects that are associated with Elements in some way. While Starlight Seeks its Form uses them to track permanent Elements, …
- [ ] <a id="q-the-terror-of-a-slowly-unfolding-plague-and-ceaseless-mining"></a>**Can Vengeance as a Burning Plague let the Habsburg Mining Expedition Ravage in Mining lands with Disease in order to earn Fear?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/the-terror-of-a-slowly-unfolding-plague-and-ceaseless-mining))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Disease, Action Modifiers, Vengeance as a Burning Plague, The Terror of a Slowly Unfolding Plague (Vengeance as a Burning Plague), Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > Yes. The Terror of a Slowly Unfolding Plague modifies how Disease works, so Ceaseless Mining lets it apply to Ravages in Mining lands.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geaf"></a>**Can Volcano just decide to destroy Presence with Explosive Eruption without triggering any of the other effects (for the benefit from Collapse in a Blast of Lava and Steam)? Can it destroy more than it needs to?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geaf))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Resolving Powers, Explosive Eruption (Volcano Looming High), Collapse in a Blast of Lava and Steam (Volcano Looming High)</sub>
  > Yes. Formally, "Destroy X [Presence]" is just a part of the innate that doesn't require any elements. Doing so counts for the Power to have been considered "used", for things like Scream Disease Into the Wind, even if you Destroyed just 1 Presence (the minimum for that Power).
- [ ] <a id="q-observe-the-ever-changing-world-and-counts-as"></a>**Can you use an optional “counts as” effect to get an Element marker from Observe the Ever-Changing World? (e.g., by making a Presence count as a Beasts with Settle into Hunting Grounds)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/observe-the-ever-changing-world-and-counts-as))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Counts As, Incarna, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Shifting Memory of Ages, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds</sub>
  > No, "counts as" effects don't change which pieces are in a land, just which traits those pieces have. A Presence turned into a Presence-Beasts-Badlands by Settle into Hunting Grounds is still the same Presence it always was; it just also counts towards effects that check Badlands and Beasts.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geh1"></a>**Do you pick the target for Let's See What Happens before or after seeing what card you draw?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geh1))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Let's See What Happens (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble</sub>
  > Before. It's a power like any other, so you need to pick its target before you start resolving it.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebg"></a>**Does "Reclaim half" on the first growth track of Starlight Seeks its Form mean half of the discard, or half of all your cards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebg))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Reclaim, Growth, Starlight Seeks Its Form</sub>
  > Half of the discard.
- [ ] <a id="q-what-plays-does-rightmost-trickster-growth-check"></a>**Does Grinning Trickster's rightmost Growth Option just use the Card Plays revealed on the Presence Track, or does it include modifiers (e.g., "Back Against the Wall," or Invader benefit 4 in Powers Long Forgotten)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/what-plays-does-rightmost-trickster-growth-check))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Growth, Elements, Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble, Back Against the Wall, Powers Long Forgotten, Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > The printed number on the Plays track. In general, effects that scale via the Energy/Plays/Elements on Presence tracks use the printed numbers rather than the amount of Energy, Plays, or Elements actually usable during a given Spirit Phase, whether or not they explicitly say so.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef0"></a>**Does Mists Shift and Flow make Range 0 powers effectively Range 1?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef0))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Targeting, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist</sub>
  > You can move Presence to satisfy the targeting requirement, so effectively yes, if you move into the target land. But note you cannot move your last Presence out of the target land for a Range 0 Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-does-lingering-plagues-prevent-vengeance-from-getting-fear-from-builds-where-theres-disease"></a>**Does the event Lingering Plagues *("....Ignore Disease during Builds this Invader Phase")* stop Vengeance from gaining Fear by letting Builds happen where there's Disease?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-lingering-plagues-prevent-vengeance-from-getting-fear-from-builds-where-theres-disease))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Action Modifiers, Disease, Build, Lingering Plagues, Vengeance as a Burning Plague, The Terror of a Slowly Unfolding Plague (Vengeance as a Burning Plague)</sub>
  > No, Vengeance may still gain the Fear. Lingering Plagues should be read as "Disease doesn't prevent Builds this Invader Phase" - it modifies the same thing ("When Disease would prevent a Build") as The Terror of a Slowly Unfolding Plague, so you can choose to apply the latter first.
- [ ] <a id="q-growth-order-for-pick-one-of-and-one-of"></a>**During Growth, can Lure of the Deep Wilderness see which Power Card its gains before it decides whether to Reclaim or Add Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/growth-order-for-pick-one-of-and-one-of))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Ordering, Growth, Lure of the Deep Wilderness</sub>
  > Yes. The only difference between Lure and other Spirits that freely choose 2 or 3 Growth Options is that Lure can't pick both from the left pair or both from the right pair.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gejx"></a>**For Shifting Memory of Ages' innate which lets them prepare element markers, if the pieces in the land change during an Action but end up the same as they were, do you get an Element marker?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gejx))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Triggered Actions, Element markers, Prepare, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Shifting Memory of Ages</sub>
  > Yes. The pieces in the land change, so the effect triggers. The word "After" just signals that you only get the Elements after the first power finishes.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedy"></a>**For Shifting Memory of Ages' innate which lets them prepare element markers: does damaging pieces count? How about adding Strife? Scenario markers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedy))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Strife, Element markers, Prepare, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Shifting Memory of Ages</sub>
  > Damaging a piece doesn't change which pieces are in the land, so no. Adding Strife adds a new piece, so yes. Scenario markers aren't generally considered pieces, so no.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedz"></a>**For Shifting Memory of Ages' innate which lets them prepare element markers: if a single power changes a bunch of things in the target land, can I get multiple element markers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedz))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Triggered Actions, Actions, Element markers, Prepare, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Shifting Memory of Ages</sub>
  > No. You can only earn 1 Element Marker per Action, no matter how many changes that Action makes to the land. (Because the rule is phrased as “After X do Y “, it triggers exactly once after each qualifying Action.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef9"></a>**For the "Relentless Optimism" Event (Destroying Towns/Cities generates no fear), can Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares still gain Fear and Push?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef9))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Relentless Optimism, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Transforming Aspect (Heart of the Wildfire)</sub>
  > Yes, because Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares doesn't generate Fear for destroying Towns/Cities, it generates Fear via "To Dream a Thousand Deaths" instead of destroying Towns/Cities.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geap"></a>**For the Fire/Sun level of Why Don't You and Them fight, do the Dahan and Invader damage each other simultaneously? Does Defend help? Does Strife help?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geap))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Strife, Defend, Dahan, Invaders, Why Don't You and Them Fight (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble</sub>
  > Yes, yes, and yes. As usual, if Strife is used, remove 1 Strife.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geeq"></a>**For Why Don't You And Them Fight, when do you need 3 Moons to make it Slow? The Fast phase, or the Slow phase?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geeq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Speed (Fast/Slow), Why Don't You and Them Fight (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble)</sub>
  > The Slow phase. The elements you had in the Fast phase are irrelevant; you check at the moment you're using the Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee6"></a>**How do the elements placed onto Starlight's Presence tracks work?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee6))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Elements, Presence track, Element markers, Starlight Seeks Its Form, Slowly Coalescing Nature (Starlight Seeks Its Form), Numinous Crisis</sub>
  > Once set, they work exactly like Elements on other Spirits’ Presence Tracks. If Presence is returned onto an Element, leave the Element Marker in place.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged9"></a>**How does "Heavy Mining" (The Kingdom of Sweden Level 1) interact with the "Bestow the Endurance of Bedrock" special rule of Stone's Unyielding Defiance?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged9))

"Heavy Mining" stating that the second Blight cannot destroy Presence or cascade is just a reminder of the general rule that Presence destruction and cascades …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedw"></a>**How exactly does Pour Time Sideways work?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedw))

(Then similarly for both Build and Explore.) On the board you moved Presence from, each Sands with Invaders will Ravage zero times. It’s phrased as “one more …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqv"></a>**If 2 Blight is added at once (eg: Sweden), can Stone's first innate pay for just 1 of it to come from the box? Or is the choice all-or-nothing?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqv))

  > Yes, you can pay for just 1 of the 2 Blight to come from the box.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfhe"></a>**If another Spirit gets hold of "Elemental Teachings" (from Shifting Memory of Ages), can they Prepare Element markers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfhe))

  > Yes. While Shifting Memory's panel defines what "Prepare" means, other Spirits can Prepare Element markers (storing them on their own panel) if they are able to use a Power that instructs them to. They can spend those Element markers on "Elemental Teachings" or for whatever Shifting Memory's special rules say those …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebm"></a>**If Finder of Paths Unseen uses Infestation of Venomous Spiders with threshold, when does it have to pay for Responsibilities of the Dead?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebm))

  > Each time an action is skipped and 1 or more Invaders are destroyed by the Damage (from the resulting triggered action).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geer"></a>**If Fractured Days gives away its Unique Powers (e.g., with "Entwined Power"), can other Spirits play them?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geer))

  > Other Spirits can play them but not use them, since they would have to pay Time and have no way of gaining it.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedn"></a>**If Many Minds Move as One uses "Settle Into Hunting-Grounds," do the two "may count as Beasts" rules stack?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedn))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Counts As, Many Minds Move as One, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One), Spreading Hostility Aspect (Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > No. A land with 2 of their Presence could count as 1 Beasts (via their Special Rule) or 2 Beasts (via the Major Power), but not 3.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-long-a-joining-of-swarms-and-flocks-lasts"></a>**If Many Minds Moves as One gathers multiple Sacred Sites into one land with an action, do they still count as separate Beasts for the rest of that action?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-long-a-joining-of-swarms-and-flocks-lasts))

  > Yes, while Many Minds can only use A Joining of Swarms and Flocks on one pair of Presence in each of its Sacred Sites (Jagged Earth, page 29), those Presence-pairs-as-Beasts remain Beasts for the rest of the action even if they move to new lands. In particular, Many Minds can use The Teeming Host Arrives to gather …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gege"></a>**If playing with Ocean's Hungry Grasp and you Weave Together a land with an Ocean, how does that work? What if Ocean's Hungry Grasp loses all their Presence from that board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gege))

  > For all purposes, the joined land is: a land (since one of its components is a land), an Ocean (since one of its components is an Ocean), Coastal, and the terrain(s) of its land component(s). For Powers and Blight only, "Ocean in Play" makes it a Wetland if it wasn't one already. Since it's an Ocean, every land …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gehz"></a>**If Starlight returns Presence to one of its Growth track rows, does it lose access to that Growth choice?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gehz))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Starlight Seeks Its Form, Numinous Crisis, Growth Begets Growth (Starlight Seeks its Form)</sub>
  > Yes, until that row is cleared again. (JE p. 32) When the row is cleared, the choice of which of the two Growth options to unlock cannot be revisited.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geeh"></a>**If Trickster uses Let's See What Happens, do they automatically get elemental thresholds?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geeh))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Let's See What Happens (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble)</sub>
  > No, you check as normal to see if you have the elements you need. Since you don't get the elements on the card you drew, you have to be lucky to hit the threshold.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb6"></a>**If Trickster's innate power Let’s See What Happens draws Strong and Constant Currents, can you swap 1 Dahan in the target land with 1 Dahan in a Coastal land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb6))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Move, Dahan, Let's See What Happens (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Strong and Constant Currents, Offer Passage Between Worlds (Finder of Paths Unseen)</sub>
  > Yes, this satisfies the mandate to use "up to" instructions at their maximum value. (And both Dahan are considered to have moved, even though the ending board state may appear the same.)
- [ ] <a id="q-can-ocean-push-between-adjacent-oceans"></a>**If two distinct Oceans are made adjacent somehow (e.g., via Open the Ways), can Ocean’s Hungry Grasp Push/Gather Presence between them with its Growth Options?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-ocean-push-between-adjacent-oceans))

  > Sure! It’s a bit ambiguous whether or not this works (and why) based on the exact wording of Ocean in Play, but there isn’t any harm in being permissive in this case.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged7"></a>**If Vengeance as a Burning Plague destroys Presence to prevent a penalty from a Blighted Land Event, does it get Disease from its 'Lingering Pestilence' special rule?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged7))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Events, Actions, Vengeance as a Burning Plague</sub>
  > A: Yes. Though Vengeance is making a choice both to destroy Presence and which Presence to destroy, the Event Action is what actually causes the destruction.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfoc"></a>**If Vengeance as a Burning Plague's presence is saved from destruction, does it still get to add a Disease?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfoc))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Saved From Destruction, Vengeance as a Burning Plague, Flowing and Silent Forms Dark By (Shroud of Silent Mist), Lingering Pestilence (Vengeance as a Burning Plague), Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will (Stone's Unyielding Defiance), Stone's Unyielding Defiance</sub>
  > No. Both because of the phrasing "...where each Destroyed Presence was", which is meaningless if no Presence is actually destroyed; and because the replacement must be applied before other modifications. The phrasing on this is pretty unclear – "...where each Destroyed Presence was" is a check that should happen …
- [ ] <a id="q-terror-of-a-slowly-unfolding-plague-with-multiple-disease"></a>**If Vengeance uses its 'The Terror of a Slowly Unfolding Plague' special rule to let Invaders Build in a land with multiple Disease, how much Fear does it generate?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/terror-of-a-slowly-unfolding-plague-with-multiple-disease))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Fear, Disease, Vengeance as a Burning Plague, The Terror of a Slowly Unfolding Plague (Vengeance as a Burning Plague)</sub>
  > 1 Fear total. The special rule checks if Disease would prevent a Build, not how much Disease is present.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-many-powers-is-close-the-ways"></a>**Is "Close the Ways" one Power that targets three lands or three Powers that share a name?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-many-powers-is-close-the-ways))
  <br><sub>Promo 2, Jagged Earth · Resolving Powers, Close the Ways (Finder of Paths Unseen), Finder of Paths Unseen, Elemental Invocation, Elemental Teachings (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Three Powers that share a name. This occasionally matters in the Elemental Invocation scenario or if Finder of Paths Unseen is gifted "Elemental Teachings" from Shifting Memory of Ages.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geea"></a>**Is Lure of the Deep Wilderness' ability to ignore Explorers the same as saying those Explorers do not participate in Ravage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geea))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Ignore, Do not participate in Ravage, Changed rulings, Enthrall the Foreign Explorers (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > Yes. You may ignore Explorers for determining which lands Ravage and for individual Ravage actions, as per "does not participate in Ravage" (JE rulebook, p. 14; NI rulebook, p. 6).
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqi"></a>**Many Minds Move as One says "when *you* Gather or Push Beasts" - is that identical to "when *your actions* Gather or Push Beasts"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqi))

("Each player may Push a Beasts" would not be sufficient - cases like that don't represent Spirits …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedx"></a>**Playing Many Minds Move as One: if I treat my Sacred Site as Beasts Token in order to move it, do I have to move both Presence to the same land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedx))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Sacred Sites, Counts As, Many Minds Move as One, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > Yes. If the Sacred Site consists of more than two Presence, all but two of those Presence get left behind. [JE p. 29]
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedv"></a>**What do the "x2" and "x3" mean in the Growth choices for Fractured Days Split the Sky?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedv))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Growth, Fractured Days Split the Sky</sub>
  > They represent 2 (or 3) separate choices: N times, choose either (option A) or (option B). Each is a separate action, if that ever becomes relevant.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqw"></a>**What does "move 1 Beast up to two lands" mean on "Guide the Way on Feathered Wings"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqw))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Move, Archipelago, Many Minds Move as One, Wrap in Wings of Sunlight, Strong and Constant Currents, Call to Migrate</sub>
  > You may move 1 Beast to an adjacent land, then may again move that same Beast to an adjacent land. "Move" is used rather than "Push" because Many Minds' special rule about Pushing Beasts doesn't apply. Most Powers which use the word "move" move pieces directly from one land to another without going through the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee2"></a>**What does "When an Event or Blight Card directly destroys Presence" mean, for Stone's innate Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee2))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Events, Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will (Stone's Unyielding Defiance)</sub>
  > It means the Event / Blight card uses the words “Destroy (Presence)” rather than, e.g., adding Blight that then happens to Destroy Presence. This Innate’s effect can be used even if the Presence Destruction was a choice, paying a cost or otherwise optional.
- [ ] <a id="q-visions-of-a-shifting-future-with-reminder-card"></a>**What happens if Fractured Days Split the Sky uses Visions of a Shifting Future on the Invader deck, and there is a reminder card right below the top card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/visions-of-a-shifting-future-with-reminder-card))

  > Set aside the reminder card, look at the top Invader Card, and shuffle the top two Invader Cards (one of which was previously below the reminder card). Then put the cards back on top of the deck, with the reminder card below the top card. If you instead choose to put the top Invader Card on the bottom of the deck, …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2q"></a>**What is the timing of specifying a new Presence-track Element for Starlight?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2q))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Elements, Timing, Triggered Actions, Action Modifiers, Starlight Seeks Its Form, Slowly Coalescing Nature (Starlight Seeks Its Form)</sub>
  > As soon as it's revealed, even mid-action. Its panel should read "When you reveal a (star)", not "After you reveal a (star)". This rarely matters, but is also more consistent with how other Presence-track elements work. (Technically as written, if you revealed multiple stars in the same action, you would only cover …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebl"></a>**What's the point of using "Elemental Teachings" (Shifting Memory of Ages) on yourself? Don't you have those Elements available anyway?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebl))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Elements, Element markers, Prepare, Shifting Memory of Ages, Insights Into the World's Nature (Shifting Memory of Ages), Elemental Teachings (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > If you spend Element Markers via "Elemental Teachings," you get those Elements for the entire turn, not just for a single Action via "Insights Into the World's Nature." But yes, the Power Card is generally more useful targeting other Spirits.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee5"></a>**What's the timing on using new Growth options?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee5))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Growth, Starlight Seeks Its Form</sub>
  > Growth choices are made one at a time, so if you unlock a new Growth option by placing Presence, you can choose it immediately if you still have choices left. If you return Presence to a Growth row for some reason, you can’t use that Growth option until the row is unlocked again, and your selection of which Growth to …
- [ ] <a id="q-breath-of-darkness-down-your-spine-abducting-russian-explorers"></a>**When Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine faces the Tsardom of Russia Level 2+, can Breath abuct lone Explorers by doing Damage to them or directly destroying them?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/breath-of-darkness-down-your-spine-abducting-russian-explorers))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Action Modifiers, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, Terror Stalks the Land (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > Yes, it can and must, even if it'd rather let the Explorer flee to get 1 Fear. Terror Stalks the Land abducts lone Invaders if Breath would attempt to damage or destroy them, while A Sense for Impending Disaster only makes an Explorer flee if that Explorer would actually otherwise be destroyed.
- [ ] <a id="q-when-observe-the-ever-changing-world-expires"></a>**When does Observe the Ever-Changing World expire?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/when-observe-the-ever-changing-world-expires))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Time Passes, Element markers, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Shifting Memory of Ages</sub>
  > It expires when Time Passes like other effects do. The Element Markers stacked up in in the target land are a reminder of how many more times it can trigger this turn, not an actual change to the board state that persists into future turns. All effects in Time Passes expire simultaneously, so if something else causes …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee3"></a>**When does the damage from the 2nd level of Let Them Break Themselves Against the Stone happen?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee3))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Triggered Actions, Let Them Break Themselves Against the Stone (Stone's Unyielding Defiance)</sub>
  > At the same time - as part of the same triggered action - as the damage from the first level. (So, e.g., Badlands only boosts the damage once.) It's phrased the way it is because If it also used the wording, "After Invaders deal 1 or more Damage to target land...", it would set up a separate triggered action.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gecs"></a>**When playing Fractured Days Split the Sky solo, is Pour Time Sideways useless?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gecs))

Solo games are no longer strictly single-board, however, with the addition of "use an additional board" as a Play Option. In a one-board game, Fractured Days Split the Sky is apt to want at least one …
- [ ] <a id="q-adding-blight-to-a-rising-heat-sacred-site-with-damaged-livestock-colony-towns"></a>**When Rising Heat of Stone and Sand has exactly two Presence in a land with a Durable Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Town that has taken one Damage, what happens with Blight is added to the land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-blight-to-a-rising-heat-sacred-site-with-damaged-livestock-colony-towns))

  > The Town is destroyed. To lay out the steps in detail: - Heat has two Presence. The Town has 1 Damage and 3 Health. - Blight is added. The Town has 1 Damage and 1 Health. - Destroy one Presence (Blight was added) and the Town (it took Damage equal to or greater than its Health).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb4"></a>**When Stone's Unyielding Defiance places a Presence from a +1 Card Play space and gains a Minor Power, can you look at the new Power before deciding where to place a Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb4))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Timing, Adding Presence, Deep Layers Exposed to the Surface (Stone's Unyielding Defiance), Stone's Unyielding Defiance</sub>
  > No. There's no point in time when you have added the Presence but it's not already on the board. The intent is that this is a triggered action (which makes the timing more obvious) but the special rule would be clearer if it started with "After...".
- [ ] <a id="q-habsburg-monarchy-livestock-colony-building-in-a-fathomless-mud-sacred-site"></a>**When the Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Level 2+ would Build a City in an Inland Fathomless Mud Sacred Site, how many Explorers get Built?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/habsburg-monarchy-livestock-colony-building-in-a-fathomless-mud-sacred-site))

  > One or two Explorers, your choice. If you choose to apply Offer No Firm Foundations first, it switches the Build from a City to an Explorer, so More Rural Than Urban doesn’t apply. On the other hand, if you choose to apply More Rural Than Urban first, it switches the Build from a City to two Towns, which Offer No …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedf"></a>**When Volcano uses Explosive Eruption, it deals damage both via destroying its Presence and the Innate Power. Does Badlands apply twice?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedf))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Actions, Action Modifiers, Badlands, Explosive Eruption (Volcano Looming High), Collapse in a Blast of Lava and Steam (Volcano Looming High), Volcano Looming High</sub>
  > No, because the damage from destroying its Presence isn't a separate action. The Special Rule says, "When your Presence is destroyed...", which means the damage is done as part of whatever action destroyed your Presence. (If it were instead a triggered action, it would say "After your Presence is destroyed".)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gees"></a>**With Pour Time Sideways and Dream of the Untouched Land could you use "skip actions on one board" on the "extra time" board (the board that Fractured Days' moved presence to)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gees))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Actions, Skipping Actions, Rulings Under Examination, Pour Time Sideways (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Dream of the Untouched Land</sub>
  > Yes. Dream of the Untouched land skips all Build Cards and "Each land"/"Each board" Adversary actions on one board, even if that board has had Time poured to it. If this proves horribly abusive it'll be revisited, but that doesn't seem likely, it's pretty niche.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebk"></a>**With Shifting Memory of Ages and the Elemental Invocation scenario, are the Element Markers considered to be in the adjacent lands for the purposes of Observe the Ever-Changing World?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebk))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Pieces, Element markers, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Elemental Invocation</sub>
  > No - they're not in the land, they're on a corner between lands.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebj"></a>**With Unnerving Pall (from Shroud of Silent Mist), when do you choose which Invaders are participating in the Ravage? - [ ] <a id="q-7w4gear"></a>**Can you take the second growth option of Downpour Drenches the World if you have only 0 or 1 Power Cards in hand?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gear))

  > Yes. Discarding two cards is not listed as a requirement or cost, but as a Growth effect. You must perform that effect to the extent possible. (In this case, you would be left with no cards to play.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged6"></a>**Does hitting both the first and second thresholds of Downpour's 'Rain and Mud Suppress Conflict' reduce Dahan counterattack damage to 0 in Downpour's lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged6))

  > No. The total counterattack damage is reduced, not the damage per Dahan. (You can intuitively think of it as Defend that also protects the Invaders from the Dahan.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged4"></a>**Finder of Paths Unseen has three Unique Powers which move/push pieces to "a land". Does that have to be a single land for the whole Power use?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged4))

  > Yes – if you could move/push them freely it would say "to lands" or "to one or more lands" instead of "to a land". By contrast, Traveler's Boon can move pieces from different lands – though they have to all end up in the same place.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geke"></a>**For Finder of Paths Unseen growth option that lets you ignore ranges or Presence icon that gives you +1 Range, what does this effect?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geke))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebx"></a>**For Finder of Paths Unseen's special rule Open the Ways, what does "once between actions" mean? Can you only change the choice once per turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebx))

For Fear/Event cards, you get to read them prior to the first Fear/Event action they cause, because revealing the card isn't part of the action. It's …
- [ ] <a id="q-open-the-ways-for-repeating-powers-on-adjacent-lands"></a>**For Powers that Repeat themselves on an adjacent land, can Finder of Paths Unseen use "Open the Ways" before the Repeat to change which lands can be targeted by the Repeat?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/open-the-ways-for-repeating-powers-on-adjacent-lands))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gean"></a>**If Downpour Drenches the World has the elements for the first two thresholds of Rain and Mud Suppress Conflict, does it get Defend 2 per Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gean))

- [ ] <a id="q-Finder-double-jeopardy"></a>**If Finder of Paths Unseen has an action that both destroys Invaders, and *also* triggers their destruction by a second action by moving them, does it lose Presence twice from "Responsibilities to the Dead"? - [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged2"></a>**If Finder of Paths Unseen makes 2 of its lands adjacent, then uses the Moon+Air threshold of its "Lay Paths They Cannot Help But Walk" Innate Power to Push its Presence out of one of those lands, are those two lands still adjacent for the Sun+Air threshold?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged2))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged5"></a>**When are the bonuses and penalties computed for Downpour's 'Rain and Mud Suppress Conflict' innate power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged5))

Practically speaking, check Defend whenever Invaders deal damage (Ravage, usually), Dahan damage …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gect"></a>**When exactly do you have to pay for Finder's rule Responsibilities to the Dead when you move something?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gect))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb5"></a>**When Finder of Paths Unseen reveals its "+1 Range" space by placing a Presence, can it use that to extend the range at which it places that Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb5))

- [ ] <a id="q-targeting-the-origin-with-spreading-and-dreadful-mire"></a>**Can Fathomless Mud of the Swamp target the origin land of Spreading and Dreadful Mire, or does that fail because moving Presence is required?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/targeting-the-origin-with-spreading-and-dreadful-mire))

(Also, while it's rarely relevant, you can technically Move a piece from a land to that same land, so long as the wording of …
- [ ] <a id="q-can-gift-of-wind-sped-steps-affect-powers-which-might-not-gather-or-push"></a>**Can Gift of Wind-Sped Steps affect a Power which *might* Gather or Push something, like Devouring Teeth's innate power Death Approaches From Beneath the Surface?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-gift-of-wind-sped-steps-affect-powers-which-might-not-gather-or-push))

- [ ] <a id="q-targeting-a-land-where-not-every-effect-can-be-resolved"></a>**Can Powers target lands where it's impossible to resolve all of the effects (e.g., Spreading and Dreadful Mire targeting a land without Dahan)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/targeting-a-land-where-not-every-effect-can-be-resolved))

While some thresholds of the Power will push Dahan if possible, Fathomless Mud of the Swamp isn't required to target a land with …
- [ ] <a id="q-does-devouring-teeth-get-plus-one-damage-on-gift-of-searing-heat"></a>**If *Gift of Searing Heat* is used on Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot, and Devouring Teeth chooses to do 1 Damage, does it get the +1 Damage bonus from its Territorial Aggression special rule?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-devouring-teeth-get-plus-one-damage-on-gift-of-searing-heat))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Damage, Action Modifiers, Gift of Searing Heat (Rising Heat of Stone and Sand), Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot, Territorial Aggression (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot)</sub>
  > No. Territorial Aggression affects only Powers belonging to Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot. Gift of Searing Heat belongs to a different Spirit, even though it is letting Devouring Teeth do something.
- [ ] <a id="q-range-bonuses-with-powers-that-gather-presence"></a>**If Devouring Teeth Lurks Underfoot gets a Range bonus to Death Approaches from Beneath the Surface and targets a land at least Range 2 from its Presence, does it have to Gather Presence into that land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/range-bonuses-with-powers-that-gather-presence))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Targeting, Gather, Range Boosts, Death Approaches Beneath the Surface (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Spreading and Dreadful Mire (Fathomless Mud of the Swamp), Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot</sub>
  > No, "Gather" can only bring in pieces from adjacent lands. If it used "Move" — e.g., "Move 1 Presence to the target land" — then it could happen at any Range, like Fathomless Mud of the Swamp's Spreading and Dreadful Mire.
- [ ] <a id="q-eyes-watch-from-the-trees-thresholds-trees-and-stones-speak-of-war"></a>**If Eyes Watch From the Trees uses the threshold of *The Trees and Stones Speak of War*, do they get to Gather a Dahan afterwards? If so, when?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/eyes-watch-from-the-trees-thresholds-trees-and-stones-speak-of-war))

  > Yes, they may Gather 1 Dahan into the target land after the Power is 100% finished (including the threshold, even if the threshold Pushes out all of the Dahan). - [ ] <a id="q-entwined-power-rising-heat"></a>**If Rising Heat of Stone and Sand has a Sacred Site in a land for targeting purposes due to "Entwined Power," do Invaders there have -1 Health from its "Blistering Heat" Special Rule?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/entwined-power-rising-heat))

- [ ] <a id="q-spreading-and-dreadful-mire-moving-presence-from-other-spirits"></a>**When Fathomless Mud of the Swamp uses Spreading and Dreadful Mire, does the moved Presence have to be Mud’s Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/spreading-and-dreadful-mire-moving-presence-from-other-spirits))

- [ ] <a id="q-exaltation-of-tangled-growth-in-solo-games"></a>**When playing Fathomless Mud of the Swamp solo, can it gain 2 Power Cards with Exaltation of Tangled Growth?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/exaltation-of-tangled-growth-in-solo-games))

- [ ] <a id="q-spreading-and-dreadful-mire-with-entwined-power"></a>**When using Spreading and Dreadful Mire, does it matter if you don't have a Sacred Site in the origin land when you resolve the first threshold (e.g., because you targeted via Entwined Power)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/spreading-and-dreadful-mire-with-entwined-power))

- [ ] <a id="q-finder-double-responsibilities-on-self-trigger"></a>**If Finder of Paths Unseen moves Invaders to their destruction, and that destruction is also due to Finder's triggered action, does "Responsibilities to the Dead" happen once or twice? - [ ] <a id="q-are-pieces-in-the-endless-dark-on-the-island"></a>**Are pieces in The Endless Dark still on the island?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/are-pieces-in-the-endless-dark-on-the-island))

- [ ] <a id="q-abducting-immobile-pieces"></a>**Can Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine abduct pieces that can't move (e.g., because they're at the Lair Incarna)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/abducting-immobile-pieces))

- [ ] <a id="q-breath-of-darkness-down-your-spine-adding-presence-to-the-endless-dark-in-growth"></a>**Can Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine add Presence to The Endless Dark in Growth?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/breath-of-darkness-down-your-spine-adding-presence-to-the-endless-dark-in-growth))

- [ ] <a id="q-can-strife-escape-the-endless-dark"></a>**Can Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine choose Strife as one of the pieces escaping the Endless Dark?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-strife-escape-the-endless-dark))

- [ ] <a id="q-breath-of-abducting-invaders-with-badlands"></a>**Can Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine use Damage bonuses (from Badlands or other sources) to abduct Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/breath-of-abducting-invaders-with-badlands))

- [ ] <a id="q-to-dream-a-thousand-deaths-and-heart-tree-guards-the-land"></a>**Can Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares use "To Dream a Thousand Deaths" to generate Fear/Push Invaders that are protected by the Heart-Tree Incarna?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/to-dream-a-thousand-deaths-and-heart-tree-guards-the-land))

- [ ] <a id="q-forgetting-impending-powers"></a>**Can Dances Up Earthquakes Forget an impending card when it gains a Major Power? How about to pay for a Choice Event?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/forgetting-impending-powers))

- [ ] <a id="q-can-cards-be-made-impending-by-repeating-them"></a>**Can Dances Up Earthquakes make a Power Card impending when Repeating it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-cards-be-made-impending-by-repeating-them))

- [ ] <a id="q-dances-up-earthquakes-third-growth-option-delaying-a-card-with-0-energy"></a>**Can Dances Up Earthquakes use its third growth option to delay an impending card from being played even if there's no Energy on that card to remove?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dances-up-earthquakes-third-growth-option-delaying-a-card-with-0-energy))

- [ ] <a id="q-dances-up-earthquakes-using-impending-only-plays-for-0-energy-power-cards"></a>**Can Dances Up Earthquakes use one of its "+1 Play for impending only" spaces to make a 0-Energy Power Card impending and then play it immediately?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dances-up-earthquakes-using-impending-only-plays-for-0-energy-power-cards))

- [ ] <a id="q-event-and-fear-actions-destroying-invaders-in-the-endless-dark"></a>**Can Fear/Event actions destroy Invaders in The Endless Dark?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/event-and-fear-actions-destroying-invaders-in-the-endless-dark))

- [ ] <a id="q-invader-actions-in-non-land-locations"></a>**Can Invader Actions happen in a place that isn't a land (e.g., in The Endless Dark)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/invader-actions-in-non-land-locations))

- [ ] <a id="q-relentless-punishment-and-spirit-targeting-powers"></a>**Can Relentless Gaze of the Sun use Relentless Punishment to repeat Spirit-targeting Powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/relentless-punishment-and-spirit-targeting-powers))

- [ ] <a id="q-repeating-on-specific-lands-with-targeting-conditions"></a>**Can Relentless Gaze of the Sun usefully Repeat a Power Card with "Relentless Punishment" if the first use stops the land from satisfying the Range or Target restrictions? - [ ] <a id="q-replacing-presence-with-an-incarna-when-the-incarna-is-on-the-island"></a>**Can Towering Roots of the Jungle replace Presence with its Incarna even if its Incarna is already on the island?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/replacing-presence-with-an-incarna-when-the-incarna-is-on-the-island))

- [ ] <a id="q-pushing-within-a-land"></a>**Can Wandering Voice Keens Delirium use Inscrutable Journeying to Push its Incarna within a land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/pushing-within-a-land))

- [ ] <a id="q-maximum-number-of-healing-markers"></a>**Can Wounded Waters Bleeding claim more than 5 Healing Markers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/maximum-number-of-healing-markers))

- [ ] <a id="q-wounded-waters-bleeding-first-use-of-energy-growth"></a>**Can Wounded Waters Bleeding use its third Growth Option on the turn it plays its first Healing Card (usually Turn 3)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/wounded-waters-bleeding-first-use-of-energy-growth))

- [ ] <a id="q-where-impending-power-cards-are"></a>**Do Dances Up Earthquakes' Impending Power Cards count as being in hand, play, or discard?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/where-impending-power-cards-are))

- [ ] <a id="q-elements-from-impending-power-cards"></a>**Do impending cards grant Elements once they’re in play?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/elements-from-impending-power-cards))

- [ ] <a id="q-abducting-with-non-lethal-damage"></a>**Does Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine need to do lethal Damage to a lone Invader to abduct it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/abducting-with-non-lethal-damage))

- [ ] <a id="q-isolate-blocking-spread-tumult-and-delusion"></a>**Does Isolate stop Wandering Voice's Incarna from being adjacent to Dahan, thereby allowing the Dahan to participate in Ravage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/isolate-blocking-spread-tumult-and-delusion))

- [ ] <a id="q-claimed-pieces-are-not-spent"></a>**Does Wounded Waters Bleeding spend Healing Markers when they gain a Healing Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/claimed-pieces-are-not-spent))

- [ ] <a id="q-how-many-healing-cards-can-wounded-waters-claim"></a>**How many Healing Cards can Wounded Waters Bleeding claim?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-many-healing-cards-can-wounded-waters-claim))
