- [ ] <a id="q-how-many-times-can-ember-eyed-behemoth-use-unrelenting-strides"></a>**How many times can Ember-Eyed Behemoth use Unrelenting Strides if its Incarna is Empowered?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-many-times-can-ember-eyed-behemoth-use-unrelenting-strides))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Empower, Incarna, Unrelenting Strides (Ember-Eyed Behemoth), Ember-Eyed Behemoth</sub>
  > Still just once, and only if Smash, Stomp, and Flatten hasn’t been used at all. Unrelenting Strides checks if Ember-Eyed Behemoth has used an Innate Power in any way; it doesn’t replace one use of an Innate Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-once-per-power-limits-and-repeats"></a>**If a special rule is limited to once per Power (e.g., those on the Healing Cards of Wounded Waters Bleeding), how does that limit apply to Repeated Powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/once-per-power-limits-and-repeats))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Serene Waters (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Roiling Waters (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Wounded Waters Bleeding</sub>
  > The limit is once per Power use, so you are free to use the special rule again when Repeating a Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-global-health-penalties-and-the-endless-dark"></a>**If an effect changes the Health of all pieces that meet a particular condition, does that affect pieces in The Endless Dark?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/global-health-penalties-and-the-endless-dark))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Health, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Kingdom of England, Life's Balance Tilts, Well-Prepared Explorers</sub>
  > Yes, so long as it checks the traits of the piece itself (e.g., what type it is, how much Strife it has) rather than the traits of the land it’s in (e.g., if Blight is present, if at least 3+ Invaders are present).
- [ ] <a id="q-affecting-the-endless-dark-with-land-targeting-powers-that-target-elsewhere"></a>**If Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine uses a land-targeting Power that resolves an effect in a land other than “target land” (e.g., removing an Invader in "2 lands with 4 or more Invaders" with "Vanish Softly Away, Forgotten by All") can it choose The Endless Dark as one of those lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/affecting-the-endless-dark-with-land-targeting-powers-that-target-elsewhere))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Targeting, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, Vanish Softly Away, Forgotten by All, Offer Passage Between Worlds (Finder of Paths Unseen)</sub>
  > No, The Endless Dark is only a land for Breath targeting it with a Power. It isn’t “your land” or “a land with 4 or more Invaders” even if it’s still “target land.”
- [ ] <a id="q-strifed-invaders-not-participating-in-ravage-near-the-voice-incarna"></a>**If Exhale Confusion and Delirium stops Invaders with Strife from participating in Ravage, does Spread Tumult and Delusion still use that Strife to stop Dahan from participating as well?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/strifed-invaders-not-participating-in-ravage-near-the-voice-incarna))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Do not participate in Ravage, Strife, Wandering Voice Keens Delirium, Spread Tumult and Delusion (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium), Exhale Confusion and Delirium (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium)</sub>
  > Yes, which pieces are participating in Ravage is determined simultaneously. There is no point at which the Strifed Invaders are not participating, but it's still undetermined if the Dahan will participate.
- [ ] <a id="q-keep-watch-for-new-incursions-twice-on-the-same-land"></a>**If Hearth-Vigil repeats Keep Watch for New Incursions on the same land, do they have to apply the Damage from the two uses in response to the same added/moved Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/keep-watch-for-new-incursions-twice-on-the-same-land))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Keep Watch for New Incursions (Hearth-Vigil), Hearth-Vigil</sub>
  > No. It can use both instances of Damage at the same time or at different times, depending on what's more helpful that turn.
- [ ] <a id="q-Relentless-Gaze-of-the-Sun-affecting-fewer-than-3-Presence-in-Growth"></a>**In Growth, can Relentless Gaze of the Sun add fewer than 3 Destroyed Presence or move fewer than 3 Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Relentless-Gaze-of-the-Sun-affecting-fewer-than-3-Presence-in-Growth))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Growth, Destroyed Presence, Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > Yes. Thematically, Relentless Gaze of the Sun should have to restore as many Destroyed Presence as possible (max. 3), but enforcing this in the few cases it matters (e.g., preventing "Blazing Renewal" combos) wasn’t worth the trouble of players thinking that they could only take that Growth Action if they could …
- [ ] <a id="q-the-endless-dark-and-spirit-targeting-powers"></a>**Is The Endless Dark a land for Spirit-targeting Powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/the-endless-dark-and-spirit-targeting-powers))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Targeting, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine)</sub>
  > No, no matter which Spirit uses the Power or is the target of the Power, the Power isn’t targeting The Endless Dark.
- [ ] <a id="q-Relentless-Gaze-of-the-Sun-Energy-Growth-Option-timing"></a>**On the last Growth option of Relentless Gaze of the Sun, when do you gain the extra Energy?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Relentless-Gaze-of-the-Sun-Energy-Growth-Option-timing))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Growth, Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > In the rare case that this matters, gain this Energy when you gain Energy normally during the second part of the Spirit Phase. Doing it this way, you are not penalized if you need to move Presence with this Growth Option before you add Presence off the top Presence track with the first Growth Option.
- [ ] <a id="q-Dances-Up-Earthquakes-penultimate-top-track-icon"></a>**What is the penultimate space on Dance Up Earthquakes' top track? Is it a track action that lets it gain 2 extra Energy onto each impending card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Dances-Up-Earthquakes-penultimate-top-track-icon))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Presence track, Energy, Rhythmic Power Builds to a Cataclysmic Crescendo (Dances Up Earthquakes), Begin a Dance of Decades (Dances Up Earthquakes), Dances Up Earthquakes</sub>
  > No, just gives 2 total Energy onto each card, and not as a track action. (For instance, it cannot be skipped.) Dances Up Earthquakes has two kinds of Energy icons on its top track. The highest normal Energy icon determines how much Energy Dances gains into its stockpile, and the highest Energy-on-an-impending-card …
- [ ] <a id="q-effects-that-do-not-work-in-the-endless-dark"></a>**When Breath of Darkness Down your Spine targets The Endless Dark with a Power, which effects don't work there?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/effects-that-do-not-work-in-the-endless-dark))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Blight, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine)</sub>
  > Effects that can't resolve: - Anything that interacts with adjacent lands, including Push and Gather (since there are no adjacent lands); - Invader Actions; - Anything conditional on having a particular terrain or being Coastal; - Anything that references the board that target land is on. Effects that do resolve, but …
- [ ] <a id="q-which-powers-can-target-the-endless-dark"></a>**When Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine targets The Endless Dark, which targeting restrictions can The Endless Dark meet?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/which-powers-can-target-the-endless-dark))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Targeting, Terrain, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Lost in the Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine)</sub>
  > Breath's Power can target The Endless Dark if they do any of the following: - Have no targeting restrictions; - Only target Inland lands (e.g., "Here There Be Monsters"), since the Endless Dark is not adjacent to the Ocean; - Targets all lands except a specific terrain (e.g., "Dry Wood Explodes in Smoldering …
- [ ] <a id="q-the-endless-dark-and-range-arrows-in-effect-text"></a>**When Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine uses a Power that has a Range arrow in its effect text (e.g., "Unleash a Torrent of the Self's Own Essence"), can it ignore Range to choose The Endless Dark?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/the-endless-dark-and-range-arrows-in-effect-text))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Range, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone</sub>
  > No, Breath can only ignore Range or the Range arrow in the “Range” box of a Power. However, Range arrows in the effect text of land-targeting Powers can measure Range from The Endless Dark to itself (e.g., to add extra tokens with the threshold of "Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone").
- [ ] <a id="q-putting-energy-on-impending-cards"></a>**When can Dances Up Earthquakes put Energy on impending cards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/putting-energy-on-impending-cards))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Energy, Rhythmic Power Builds to a Cataclysmic Crescendo (Dances Up Earthquakes), Begin a Dance of Decades (Dances Up Earthquakes), Dances Up Earthquakes</sub>
  > Dances Up Earthquakes can only put Energy on impending Power Cards in two ways: - When making a Power Card impending, they can put any amount of Energy on it. - When gaining Energy from its Presence Track in the middle of the Spirit Phase, they gain some amount of Energy (usually 1) directly onto each impending Power …
- [ ] <a id="q-reimpending-an-impending-card"></a>**When Dances Up Earthquakes puts an impending card into play, can it immediately make the card impending again?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/reimpending-an-impending-card))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Dances Up Earthquakes, Begin a Dance of Decades (Dances Up Earthquakes), Rhythmic Power Builds to a Cataclysmic Crescendo (Dances Up Earthquakes)</sub>
  > No. Dances Up Earthquakes can only make a Power Card impending when it wasn't already impending.
- [ ] <a id="q-breath-of-darkness-down-your-spine-checking-when-a-piece-is-alone"></a>**When does Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine check if a piece is alone for the purpose of abducting it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/breath-of-darkness-down-your-spine-checking-when-a-piece-is-alone))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Damage, Destroy, Timing, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, Terror Stalks the Land (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Accelerated Rot</sub>
  > Just before resolving the sentence with the Damage/Destroy instruction. The "+" before Damage in Power thresholds (e.g., on "Accelerated Rot") is non-functional; that's a separate Damage instruction from the one in the main Power effect.
- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-presence-check-for-relentless-punishment"></a>**When does Relentless Punishment check how many of your Presence are in the origin land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-presence-check-for-relentless-punishment))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Timing, Focus the Sun’s Rays (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Punishment (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > When you target the land with the original use of the Power, before resolving any of its effects. This most commonly matters for Focus the Sun’s Rays, but it can also matter if Presence is destroyed/removed/replaced as part of Power resolution.
- [ ] <a id="q-when-to-determine-the-range-bonus-from-reach-from-the-infinite-darkness"></a>**When is the Range bonus of Reach from the Infinite Darkness determined?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/when-to-determine-the-range-bonus-from-reach-from-the-infinite-darkness))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Timing, Range Boosts, Reach from the Infinite Darkness (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > When measuring Range for a Power. Count up your Presence in The Endless Dark to determine your bonus Range at that moment. This could be different than the amount of Presence that was in The Endless Dark when Reach from the Infinite Darkness was used earlier in the turn!
- [ ] <a id="q-presence-in-the-endless-dark-on-an-archipelago"></a>**When playing on an archipelago, do two Spirits with Presence in The Endless Dark count as being on the same islet?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/presence-in-the-endless-dark-on-an-archipelago))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Archipelago, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > Not unless they were already sharing an islet anyway. The Endless Dark isn't on any island board, so it's not part of any islet.
- [ ] <a id="q-Choosing-a-Spirit-for-Consider-a-Harmonious-Nature"></a>**When Relentless Gaze of the Sun chooses another Spirit with "Consider a Harmonious Nature," must that Spirit have Destroyed Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Choosing-a-Spirit-for-Consider-a-Harmonious-Nature))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Destroyed Presence, Consider a Harmonious Nature (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > No, Relentless Gaze of the Sun is free to choose any Spirit other than itself; the 3 Sun 1 Plant threshold just doesn’t do anything other than choosing a Spirit if the chosen Spirit has no Destroyed Presence. The chosen Spirit can still receive Energy from the final threshold.
- [ ] <a id="q-Consider-a-Harmonious-Nature-in-solo-games"></a>**When Relentless Gaze of the Sun is the only Spirit in the game, can it choose itself for the Plant thresholds of Consider a Harmonious Nature?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Consider-a-Harmonious-Nature-in-solo-games))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Solo games, Consider a Harmonious Nature (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > No, the "another Spirit" exception in solo play only applies to the "Target" box, not the effects of the Power. The last two thresholds will simply fail to do anything, since choosing another Spirit is impossible.
- [ ] <a id="q-where-can-relentless-gaze-of-the-sun-destroy-presence-with-consider-a-harmonious-nature"></a>**When Relentless Gaze of the Sun uses Consider a Harmonious Nature to destroy Presence instead of adding Blight, which Presence can it pick?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/where-can-relentless-gaze-of-the-sun-destroy-presence-with-consider-a-harmonious-nature))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Destroy, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Deep Slumber (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Consider a Harmonious Nature (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun, The Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine)</sub>
  > It can destroy any of its own Presence anywhere on the island, which includes Presence in The Endless Dark. Gaze can't destroy Presence that's not on the island, including: - On its Presence Tracks - Removed from the game, e.g., by a Bargain - Under the effects of Absolute Stasis - On Serpent's Deep Slumber Track
- [ ] <a id="q-can-relentless-gaze-of-the-sun-double-elements-in-growth"></a>**When Relentless Gaze of the Sun uses its final Growth option, does it also double the Elements it gains from its top Presence Track?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-relentless-gaze-of-the-sun-double-elements-in-growth))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Elements, Energy, Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > No. That Growth option only doubles Energy gained, not all benefits from the Energy/turn track.
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-together"></a>**When Relentless Gaze of the Sun uses its Growth Action to move up to 3 Presence, must those Presence come from the same land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-together))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Move, Growth, Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > Yes. "Move X Together" means "X must be together for the entire movement," not "X must move in a way that they are all together at the end of the movement."
- [ ] <a id="q-roiling-waters-adding-pieces-that-count-as-beasts"></a>**When Roiling Waters Bleeding adds a non-Beasts piece that counts as a Beasts token (e.g., the Presence of a Spirit under "Settle Into Hunting-Grounds"), does it deal Damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/roiling-waters-adding-pieces-that-count-as-beasts))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Counts As, Repositioning Presence, Roiling Waters (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Wounded Waters Bleeding, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > Generally, no. "Lands of Blood and Savagery" checks if the piece that's being added is already a Beasts, not if it will be a Beasts once it arrives. Nevertheless, Roiling Waters Bleeding deals Damage if repositioning a piece on the island that already counts as a Beasts, whether that's the Incarna of Breath of …
- [ ] <a id="q-dances-up-earthquakes-playing-cards-outside-of-the-spirit-phase"></a>**When something lets Dances Up Earthquakes play a Power Card during the Fast, Invader, or Slow Phases, can they make that Power Card impending?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dances-up-earthquakes-playing-cards-outside-of-the-spirit-phase))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Playing Power Cards, Begin a Dance of Decades (Dances Up Earthquakes), Dances Up Earthquakes</sub>
  > Yes, and they may pay any amount of Energy when doing so, like when making a card impending during the Spirit Phase. They don't need to pay the full Energy required by that effect (usually the cost of the card).
- [ ] <a id="q-dances-up-earthquakes-third-growth-option-picking-cards"></a>**When using Dances Up Earthquakes' third growth option, can you pick the same impending card twice? Can you gain extra Energy onto one impending card and less Energy onto another impending card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dances-up-earthquakes-third-growth-option-picking-cards))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Growth, Action Modifiers, Rhythmic Power Builds to a Cataclysmic Crescendo (Dances Up Earthquakes), Begin a Dance of Decades (Dances Up Earthquakes), Dances Up Earthquakes</sub>
  > No and yes, respectively. You first choose which 0-2 cards you're going to affect and then separately decide which of the chosen cards (if any) will gain +1 Energy and which (if any) will gain -1 Energy when you gain Energy later that Spirit Phase.
- [ ] <a id="q-what-counts-as-a-previous-use-for-relentless-punishment"></a>**When using Relentless Punishment to repeat a Power Card, what counts as a previous use?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/what-counts-as-a-previous-use-for-relentless-punishment))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Repeat, Relentless Punishment (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > Any time you have resolved the Power, including: - The original use of the Power; - Any time Relentless Punishment repeated that Power; - Any time that Power repeated itself (e.g., Winds of Rust and Atrophy repeating itself with its threshold); and - Any time something else repeated that Power (e.g., Powerstorm) …
- [ ] <a id="q-where-claimed-cards-go"></a>**When Wounded Waters Bleeding claims a Healing Card, does it go to their hand?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/where-claimed-cards-go))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Claim, Seeking a Path Towards Healing (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Wounded Waters Bleeding</sub>
  > No, it goes straight to the Spirit Panel, as though you added an Aspect in the middle of the game.

## Power Cards

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8op"></a>**Can Gift of Strength or Powerstorm (both Fast powers) be used on a Slow power card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8op))
  <br><sub>Core game · Repeat, Speed (Fast/Slow), Gift of Strength (Vital Strength of the Earth), Powerstorm</sub>
  > Yes. Gift of Strength targets a Spirit, not a Power Card. At any point during the turn, that Spirit may choose to Repeat a power card (of the specified Energy cost or lower), whether it is a fast power or a slow power. They need not choose which power card to Repeat until they actually use the ability. The repeated …
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28aex"></a>**Can Powerstorm or Gift of Strength let someone Repeat Innate Powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28aex))
  <br><sub>Core game · Repeat, Powerstorm, Gift of Strength (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > No. Powerstorm (Major Power) and Gift of Strength (Innate for Vital Strength of the Earth) both specifically say "Repeat a Power Card". (If they said "Repeat a Power", the answer would be yes.)
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfhb"></a>**Can Spirit-targeting Powers let a Spirit pick "one of their lands" where nothing happens - eg, use Pact of the Joined Hunt to do damage in a land where you cannot gather a Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfhb))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Core game · Pact of the Joined Hunt, Tidal Boon (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Mantle of Dread (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > Yes. While not supported by a strict reading of the rules, the intent is that when a Spirit does something in one of their lands, they get to choose which of their lands. So for Pact of the Joined Hunt, the target spirit chooses one of their lands, gathers a Dahan if possible, and then does damage. Similarly, you can …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gdtj"></a>**Can Vengeance of the Dead trigger itself? How about if it's Repeated?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gdtj))
  <br><sub>Core game · Repeat, Triggered Actions, Vengeance of the Dead</sub>
  > It can't trigger itself. But if it's Repeated, the two different triggers can set each other off. Example 1: You use Vengeance of the Dead on a land with many Towns which have each taken 1 Damage. If a Dahan dies there, Vengeance of the Dead will trigger and deal 1 damage (destroying a Town), but will not trigger …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqd"></a>**Can Wrap in Wings of Sunlight grant Defend if you Move 0 Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqd))
  <br><sub>Core game · Move, Wrap in Wings of Sunlight, Rally to the Defense of Distant Kin</sub>
  > No. "That land" means "the land moved to". You can choose to move 0 pieces, but then nothing moves, and no land was moved to.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8q2"></a>**Can you choose a land protected by Infinite Vitality as a place to add Blight? (Due to cascade, Power, Event Card, etc.)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8q2))
  <br><sub>Core game · Blight, Infinite Vitality</sub>
  > Yes, you can. Infinite Vitality only changes what happens when you would add Blight to a land. It doesn't restrict in any way your ability to choose the land as a place Blight is added to.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oc8n"></a>**Can you use Blazing Renewal on a Spirit with no destroyed Presence just to do 4 Damage somewhere if you have the threshold elements?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oc8n))
  <br><sub>Core game · Destroyed Presence, Blazing Renewal</sub>
  > No. The threshold effect should specify "4 Damage in that land." (The Power doesn't target a land, so there's not any other place one could deal the Damage anyhow.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gani"></a>**Do I need to forget two cards to trigger the conditional on Call on Midnight's Dreams?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gani))
  <br><sub>Core game · Forgetting, Call on Midnight's Dreams (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares)</sub>
  > No. If you choose Call on Midnight's Dreams as the usual card to forget when you gain the Major Power, then you may follow the conditional and put the card in play.
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28auq"></a>**Does "Entwined Power" (you and target Spirit may use each others’ Presence to target Powers) also allow you to count the other Spirit's Presence for the text effects of Powers (e.g., getting 1 Fear and dealing 1 Damage with "Pull Beneath the Hungry Earth")? How about for determining which lands are "your lands" for the purpose of a Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28auq))
  <br><sub>Core game · Targeting, Entwined Power, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Gift of Living Energy, Growth Through Sacrifice, Threatening Flames (Heart of the Wildfire)</sub>
  > No. Those are effects of Powers; Entwined Power only helps targeting – i.e., meeting the requirements in the "Range" and "Target" boxes.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kavn"></a>**Does an "or" line on a power divide the entire card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kavn))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Promo · Crops Wither and Fade (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Growth Through Sacrifice, Sacrosanct Wilderness (Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds), Gift of the Primordial Deeps (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Gift of Flowing Power (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Tormenting Rotflies</sub>
  > No. The "or" line only applies to the clauses immediately above and below the line, without extra whitespace. For example, if you use Crops Wither and Fade, you always gain 2 fear and then you may choose whether you are replacing a City or a Town. On the other hand, on Tormenting Rotflies, you either choose …
- [ ] <a id="q-repeating-power-cards-that-grant-repeats"></a>**Does repeating a Power Card that grants repeats (e.g., "Powerstorm") create an infinite combo?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/repeating-power-cards-that-grant-repeats))
  <br><sub>Core game · Repeat, Powerstorm, Gift of Twinned Days, Gift of Abundance (Downpour Drenches the World), Downpour Drenches the World</sub>
  > No, since "Repeat" instructions are ignored when repeating a Power. [Core game p. 19] Some of these Power Cards can be usefully repeated due to having non-Repeat instructions: - "Powerstorm" grants target Spirit 3 Energy. Repeating it for free gets the Spirits more Energy, and even repeating it by paying its cost can …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oebp"></a>**Does the second effect of Tidal Boon *(“if Dahan are pushed...”)* last for the whole turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oebp))
  <br><sub>Core game · Tidal Boon (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > No, that clause only applies to Pushes that happen from the Push on the power itself.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kc3z"></a>**For the unique power Call On Midnight's Dreams, does "this Power" refer to Call On Midnight's Dreams, or to the just-gained Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kc3z))
  <br><sub>Core game · Call on Midnight's Dreams (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares)</sub>
  > Call On Midnight's Dreams.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gdth"></a>**For Vengeance of the Dead, what counts as an "effect"? If a Power has multiple instructions that damage/destroy things, are those separate "effects"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gdth))
  <br><sub>Core game · Actions, Triggered Actions, Vengeance of the Dead</sub>
  > No. Using a Power is a single action ("effect" means "action"; terminology introduced in Jagged Earth), so Vengeance of the Dead is triggered (or not) after the whole thing resolves. See the Summary of Action rules for more details.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odei"></a>**How does Mists of Oblivion destroy invaders, if it only does one damage to each Town/City?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odei))
  <br><sub>Core game · Mists of Oblivion</sub>
  > Usually, only if the Invaders are already damaged by some other effect, or if you hit the threshold. The instruction "1 Fear per Town/City this Power destroys" means that you generate some extra Fear if the power happens to destroy things. It's up to you to arrange how this happens, using the rest of the card.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pc"></a>**How much Fear does Tsunami's threshold effect generate?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pc))
  <br><sub>Core game · Tsunami</sub>
  > If you have the elements for Tsunami, you will get an extra fear for each other coastal land, typically +2 Fear.
- [ ] <a id="q-entwined-downpour-are-joint-sacred-sites-wetlands-for-the-other-spirit"></a>**If "Entwined Power" is used on Downpour Drenches the World and another Spirit: can lands with 2 of the other Spirit's Presence be treated as Downpour's Sacred Sites (and thus Wetlands) while the other Spirit is targeting?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/entwined-downpour-are-joint-sacred-sites-wetlands-for-the-other-spirit))
  <br><sub>Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth, Core game · Targeting, Sacred Sites, Downpour Drenches the World, Entwined Power, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Volcanic Peaks Tower Over the Landscape (Volcano Looming High)</sub>
  > No, only while Downpour is targeting. "Entwined Power" is "each Spirit may use the other's Presence while it is targeting", not "both Spirits' Presence belongs to both of them while either is targeting". There is no formal definition of "using another Spirit's Presence"; informally consider it "treat it as yours for …
- [ ] <a id="q-where-do-spirit-targeting-powers-add-their-fear"></a>**If a Spirit-targeting Power adds Fear, which land does it add Fear in (e.g., for the purpose of Dread Apparitions)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/where-do-spirit-targeting-powers-add-their-fear))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Fear, Dread Apparitions (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Foreboding Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Mantle of Dread (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Exaltation of the Incandescent Sky</sub>
  > No specific land, unless the Power explicitly says which land to affect. Mantle of Dread and Exaltation of the Incandescent Sky notably don't specify where the Fear is earned, and thus can't add Defend with Dread Apparitions or trigger a Push from Stretch Out Coils of Foreboding Dread.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p8"></a>**If A Year of Perfect Stillness or Paralyzing Fright is used on a land, can that land still be a source for Exploration?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p8))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explore, Paralyzing Fright, A Year of Perfect Stillness (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Yes. (If it has a Town or City, of course.) The cards say to "Skip all actions in target land". The Explore Action is performed only in lands of the type shown on the Invader Card. The process of resolving an Explore Action checks to see what's in adjacent lands - but the Explore Action isn't happening there, so …
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28aeu"></a>**If I have one Presence in a land, and I play Entwined Power on another Spirit who has one Presence there, do we have a Sacred Site?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28aeu))
  <br><sub>Core game · Sacred Sites, Presence, Entwined Power, River's Domain (River Surges in Sunlight), River Surges in Sunlight, Travel Aspect (River Surges in Sunlight)</sub>
  > You may target Powers as if you had a Sacred Site there, and they may target Powers as if they had a Sacred Site there. But for all other purposes, no, because Entwined Power only lets you use each others' Presence for targeting Powers - not for anything else, including resolving Powers. For instance, Gift of Living …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka8w"></a>**If I use the "forget this Power" option on "Call on Midnight's Dreams," can I still Repeat the Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka8w))

  > In general, no – the Power Card is leaves play when you Forget it, so you can't Repeat it later in the Fast Phase with a "once this turn" Repeat option, like "Powerstorm" of "Gift of Strength." Changed Portion of Ruling: If using the Power Card triggers the option to Repeat it immediately (e.g., due to the Locus …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pe"></a>**If you gain a Major Power by non-standard means or from a place other than the Major Power deck, do you have to Forget a card? How about the "gifting" of Entwined Power's threshold or "taking" a Major Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pe))

  > "Gifting" is a transfer and does not require Forgetting, and "taking" explicitly doesn't require Forgetting. But for any Gain of a Major Power, you must Forget unless the effect specifically exempts you. As usual, you may Forget the Major Power you just gained, if you'd prefer that to losing any of your other Power …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8q9"></a>**If you meet the elemental threshold for "Powerstorm," can the target spirit pay for and repeat the same power multiple times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8q9))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth · Repeat, Powerstorm, Downpour Drenches the World, Pour Down Power across the Island (Downpour Drenches the World), Gift of Twinned Days</sub>
  > No, they can't. The elemental threshold on "Powerstorm" allows the target spirit to repeat up to three different power cards. The spirit Downpour Drenches the World is worded differently, and may repeat the same Power card. Similarly, when used in solo, "Gift of Twinned Days" may (and often must) Repeat the same …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8q3"></a>**If you meet the threshold bonuses on Poisoned Land (Per Blight, +1 Fear and +4 Damage), do they count the Blight added by Poisoned Land itself?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8q3))
  <br><sub>Core game · Poisoned Land</sub>
  > Yes. You perform a Power's instructions in order, so the Blight is added before the extra Fear/Damage are done.
- [ ] <a id="q-this-power-in-repeats"></a>**If you Repeat "Infestation of Venomous Spiders" with threshold, does the "this Power" in the threshold refer to all uses of the Power that turn, granting Damage twice from each skip?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/this-power-in-repeats))

Any Action skipped by one use of "Infestation of Venomous Spiders" will only cause 4 Damage from that use's threshold, not any Damage from Repeating the Power. Similarly, "Never Heard From …
- [ ] <a id="q-Repeating-internal-range-boosts"></a>**If you Repeat a card that boosts its own Range via an elemental threshold (eg: Sap the Strength of Multitudes), but don't have the threshold when you Repeat it, is the Repeat's range still boosted?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Repeating-internal-range-boosts))

  > Yes. Changes to Range/Speed persist across Repeats. If you do have the threshold, you don't get the +1 Range again. (This is counter to a strict reading of the rules; the threshold would be better written as "This Power's Range is 1", the way Talons of Lightning's is.) This does not apply to Carapaced Land, which has …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9b"></a>**If you Repeat a Power card with "Powerstorm" or "Gift of Strength," do you get double the elements?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9b))
  <br><sub>Core game · Repeat, Elements, Powerstorm, Gift of Strength (Vital Strength of the Earth), Gift of Twinned Days, Gift of Abundance (Downpour Drenches the World)</sub>
  > No, you only get the elements once, when you play the card (p. 19, under Repeat). If you Repeat a Power card that explicitly grants elements (like "Elemental Boon") then you do get the benefit twice.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ganu"></a>**If you use Encompassing Ward ("Defend 2 in every land where Target Spirit has Presence"), then the target Spirit moves some of their Presence, does the Defend move/change?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ganu))

  > Changed answer: Yes. The Power modifies the target Spirit so it provides Defend 2 in any lands where it happens to have Presence at that moment. This is for consistency with other effects that alter an entire class of things, mostly on Fear Cards and Event Cards. Powers that affect a single land, or a land and its …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qc"></a>**In "Veil the Night's Hunt," if there are fewer Invaders than Dahan, how much damage is done?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qc))
  <br><sub>Core game, Horizons of Spirit Island, Nature Incarnate · Veil the Night's Hunt, Suffocating Shroud (Shroud of Silent Mists), The Fog Closes In (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist, Flocking Red-Talons</sub>
  > If there are fewer Invaders than Dahan, each Invader takes 1 damage and the remaining damage is wasted. That is, the Damage must be dealt to different Invaders or it is wasted, rather than the Damage merely being spread as evenly as possible.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og25"></a>**In a solo game, do you get bonuses on powers like Boon of Vigor that usually only happen if you target another player?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og25))
  <br><sub>Core game · Solo games, Boon of Vigor (River Surges in Sunlight), Gift of Constancy, Elemental Boon, Spur on with Words of Fire, Boon of Growing Power (Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds)</sub>
  > No. You can target yourself with powers that say 'Another Player', but any effects that are conditional on targeting someone else do not activate. (Rulebook p. 22)
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ocxq"></a>**On the threshold effect of "The Trees and Stones Speak of War", do the Pushed Dahan each bring "Defend 2" with them, or "Defend 2 per Dahan"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ocxq))

  > Defend 2. If you have 5 Dahan in the target land, then Push 2 of them to two different adjacent lands, you'll end up with a Defend 6, a Defend 2, and a Defend 2, no matter how many/few Dahan are in the Pushed-to lands. (If you'd Pushed both Dahan to the same land, you'd end up with a Defend 6 and a Defend 4.) If the …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ocxx"></a>**What does the threshold effect of Infinite Vitality protect against?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ocxx))

If the threshold effect of Cast Down into the Briny Deep is used, the Dahan can survive. They would then be moved to the nearest legal land …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g9jw"></a>**What happens if you play Entwined Power in a solo game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g9jw))
  <br><sub>Core game · Solo games, Entwined Power</sub>
  > As usual, you can use it on yourself. As the power is worded, you can gain two powers and, if you hit the threshold, gain 6 Energy.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p9"></a>**What happens if you use Entwined Power to give another Spirit a card with Spirit-specific effects? (E.g., Absorb Essence, Swallow the Land-Dwellers)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p9))

Target Spirit gains Any Element and 1 Energy." …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g9dg"></a>**What speed is Song of Sanctity?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g9dg))
  <br><sub>Core game · Song of Sanctity</sub>

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p0"></a>**When a Power (say, Blazing Renewal) does damage to each Invader, then its threshold effect says "+4 Damage", is that +4 overall Damage, or +3 Damage to each Invader?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p0))
  <br><sub>Core game · Damage, Mists of Oblivion, Blazing Renewal</sub>
  > +4 overall Damage, done when the threshold is resolved. If it were a per-Invader improvement, the text would read "+4 Damage to each Invader". Generally, the "+" in threshold effects is just to remind players that the damage is in addition to the damage up in the normal effects. (Mists of Oblivion changed between the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pr"></a>**When a Power generates Fear directly and also destroys a Town or City, do you get Fear both times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pr))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear, Destroy, Shatter Homesteads (Lightning's Swift Strike), Rituals of Destruction (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Yes. For instance, on Lightning Swift Strike's power card Shatter Homesteads, you get 1 Fear directly, then (if you successfully destroyed a Town), get an extra Fear from that. Similarly if you trigger the more powerful effect of Rituals of Destruction (Vital Strength of the Earth).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ganc"></a>**When Invaders deal Damage to each other, is that damage dealt simultaneously?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ganc))
  <br><sub>Core game, Nature Incarnate · Damage, Dissolve the Bonds of Kinship, Waters Taste of Ruin (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Wounded Waters Bleeding</sub>
  > Yes, Invaders can destroy each other this way. Damage from a single effect is done simultaneously. For instance, with the threshold effect of Dissolve the Bonds of Kinship, a City, a Town, and 5 Explorers can wipe each other out. For the special case involving Invaders with Strife, see here.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gdab"></a>**When selecting a land with Mantle of Dread, do I have to choose a land with an Explorer and a Town if I can?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gdab))
  <br><sub>Core game · Mantle of Dread (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > No. The intent of the power is that the target Spirit may select one of their lands with only an Explorer or only a Town, even if they have another land with both Explorers and Towns.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9a"></a>**Why do some of the Power cards have colored corners?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9a))
  <br><sub>Core game · Corners of Power Cards, Learning</sub>
  > These are the cards that match the Power Progression Cards for the starting Spirits. You may ignore them after your first games.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odeb"></a>**If a Power has a conditional effect (e.g., Defend if a Sacred Site is present), does the effect persist when the condition changes?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odeb))

This …
- [ ] <a id="q-What-do-range-boosts-affect"></a>**If I get a Range boost, what Ranges does it affect?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/What-do-range-boosts-affect))

So not just the arrow under "Target Land" on most Power Cards, but also, e.g., Range arrows in the text of Powers (e.g., …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfgq"></a>**If you Forget a Power Card partway through resolution, do you continue resolving it? (E.g., if you gain a Major Power with "Entwined Power" and then Forget "Entwined Power.")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfgq))

The Power Card is no longer in play though, so you no longer gain the benefits of its Elements towards its …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gbf7"></a>**If you repeat a Power, can you use another Power in between the two uses, or do the two uses have to go right after each other?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gbf7))
  <br><sub>All · Repeat, Gift of Strength (Vital Strength of the Earth), Powerstorm, Gift of Twinned Days, Gift of Abundance (Downpour Drenches the World), Pour Down Power across the Island (Downpour Drenches the World)</sub>
  > That depends on what's letting you Repeat the Power. - If you can Repeat the Power "this turn" (e.g., from "Powestorm" or "Gift of Strength"), then you may use another Power between the two uses of the repeated Power. - If the Power repeats itself (e.g., "Creepers Tear into Mortar" or "The Land Thrashes in Furious …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ofsj"></a>**Can Sky Stretches to Shore let you, during the Slow Phase, Repeat an already-used Fast Power? (Assuming you have a Repeat from somewhere, like Powerstorm or Gift of Strength.)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ofsj))

  > Yes. Even after a Power is used, it stays in play, and can be modified - there's just usually little point in doing so. So the Fast Power can be made Slow1 with Sky Stretches to Shore, then used via the Repeat. 1 Effects reading "Use a Power as if it were (Fast/Slow)" should be interpreted as "Make a Power …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8rr"></a>**Can Strangling Firevine target at Range 0 to add two Wilds to the same land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8rr))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Targeting, Strangling Firevine</sub>
  > Yes. You add 1 Wilds to both the originating land (the land from which you're measuring Range) and the target land. At Range 0, these will be the same, so both Wilds tokens are added to the same land.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged0"></a>**Can you disconnect the island using Cast Down into the Briny Deep?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged0))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Island setup, Archipelago, Player-convenience choices, Cast Down into the Briny Deep</sub>
  > Yes. For instance, if you sink the middle board in the "Coastline" layout variant 3-player game, there are now two island boards left. By default, there is no way to get from one board to the other, but if you want (player option), you may treat lands that were Range 2 from each other before the sinking as still …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcn7"></a>**Do damage boosts affect the damage from the threshold effect of "Instruments of their Own Ruin"? Do Strife and Defend?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcn7))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Strife, Damage, Defend, Instruments of their Own Ruin</sub>
  > Damage boosts (e.g., from Sweden level 3) usually do affect it, as they are still Ravaging. Strife and Defend do not, as the Invaders are damaging other Invaders.
- [ ] <a id="q-power-cards-checking-for-invaders-participating-in-ravage-actions-they-cause"></a>**Do Powers that cause Ravage Actions (e.g., "Manifest Incarnation") check if any Invaders are participating in Ravage, just like Invader Cards do?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/power-cards-checking-for-invaders-participating-in-ravage-actions-they-cause))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Ravage, Do not participate in Ravage, Manifest Incarnation, Blur the Arc of Years (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Fractured Days Split the Sky, Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > Yes. Those Ravages only include Invaders that can participate in Ravage. Though it rarely matters, the Ravage Actions caused by Power Cards don't happen if no Invaders are able to participate.
- [ ] <a id="q-Does-Sky-Stretches-to-Shore-affect-non-targeting-Range-arrows"></a>**Does Sky Stretches to Shore boost Range arrows inside a Power's effect text?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Does-Sky-Stretches-to-Shore-affect-non-targeting-Range-arrows))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Range Boosts, Sky Stretches to Shore</sub>
  > Yes. On a strict reading, it shouldn't - Range arrows inside a Power's effect text aren't part of "targeting", and it reads "for targeting Coastal lands" - but it's much clearer to have all Range boosts work the same way. (In general, Range boosts affect all Range arrows, including those in the text of Powers.)
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9m"></a>**For the base effect of "Instruments of Their Own Ruin," if there are multiple Invaders with Strife, do they deal Damage simultaneously? Can they deal Damage to each other?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9m))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Nature Incarnate · Strife, Instruments of their Own Ruin, Discord, Theological Strife, Waters Taste of Ruin (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Wounded Waters Bleeding</sub>
  > Yes and yes. So if you had a City, a Town, and an Explorer all with Strife, the Town + Explorer could destroy the City while the City destroyed the Town + Explorer. The same applies to the Terror Level III effects of the Fear cards "Discord" and "Theological Strife," and to the "Afflict with Bloodthirst" Innate Power …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8r9"></a>**How much damage is done with the threshold effect of Instruments of their Own Ruin? Is full damage done in each adjacent land, or is it split up among the adjacent lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8r9))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Damage, Instruments of their Own Ruin</sub>
  > The damage the Invaders do is split up however you like between Invaders in adjacent lands. As a general rule, "X deals damage to Y" merely specifies what you're allowed assign the damage to. Effects which damage every-one-of-a-thing will explicitly use the word "each", "every", or "all" - e.g., "to each Invader", …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedq"></a>**If Gift of Nature's Connection comes up as an Event randomizer, does it work like Elemental Boon (we get to choose if it has the element or not)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Choice Events, Gift of Nature's Connection, Elemental Boon</sub>
  > No, treat it as it appears - it has no elements. (Unlike Elemental Boons, which has question-marks.) In hindsight, this is how Elemental Boon should have worked, since it avoids an exception case / specific rule just for it.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfo8"></a>**If Pent-Up Calamity removes a Spirit's Presence to deal damage (via that Presence counting as, e.g., Beasts): can the threshold replace that Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfo8))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Pieces, Counts As, Incarna, Pent-Up Calamity, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Many Minds Move as One</sub>
  > No, it will replace an actual token of the type you chose to remove. When a piece is removed from the board, it goes to the supply (or other destination) for that physical type of piece, it doesn't "remember" what it was while on the island.
- [ ] <a id="q-cross-board-woven-lands-and-skipping-invader-cards"></a>**If Weave Together the Fabric of Place joins lands from two different boards, what happens if an effect skips an entire Invader Card on one of those boards? (e.g., "Dream of the Untouched Land")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/cross-board-woven-lands-and-skipping-invader-cards))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Matches, Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Dream of the Untouched Land, New Cash Crops Take Hold</sub>
  > The land can still match the Invader Card due to being on the other board. In cases where multiple boards can be protected, like sacrificing Presence to "New Cash Crops Take Hold," the land is only protected if both boards are skipped.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geak"></a>**If Weave Together the Fabric of Place joins lands on two adjacent boards, what happens if you destroy one of those boards with Cast Down Into the Briny Deep?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geak))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Player-convenience choices, Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Cast Down into the Briny Deep</sub>
  > It will destroy everything in the joined land, since it's on the board being Cast Down. (The land is on both boards.) If you target the conjoined land, you choose which single board to sink. If you want (player option) and you have a convenient item handy to mark a land as "destroyed, no longer exists", then Cast …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pd"></a>**If you reach the threshold effect of Instruments of their Own Ruin, can the ravaging Invaders destroy adjacent Invaders before they ravage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pd))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Instruments of their Own Ruin</sub>
  > Yes, the Invaders in the targeted land can destroy Invaders in adjacent lands before they get a chance to Ravage. In general, you can choose in what order to resolve effects, including Invader Actions.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfpm"></a>**If you sink a board when playing against Russia Lvl 2+, does the Pushed Explorer have to leave the destroyed board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfpm))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Illegally placed pieces, Cast Down into the Briny Deep, Tsardom of Russia, Transforming Aspect (Heart of the Wildfire), Dark Fire Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > Technically, no — but if you try Pushing the Explorer to a land on the sinking board, it can't be destroyed a second time by the same action. It will end up in an illegal state and be placed in the closest legal land.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee9"></a>**If you target yourself with Growth Through Sacrifice and remove your only Presence on the island, is that land still considered "your land"? (So you could re-add a Presence to avoid losing?)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee9))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Adding Presence, Repositioning Presence, Move, Presence, Victory and Defeat, Growth Through Sacrifice</sub>
  > No - "your land" / "your lands" are checked when you reach that instruction in the text. When it comes time to add a Presence to one of your lands, you have no lands and cannot do so. This logic doesn't apply to Moving or Repositioning your only Presence, however - in those cases, there's never a point in time when …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfas"></a>**In Pent-Up Calamity, when you "return" Strife, where does it go?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfas))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Pent-Up Calamity, Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble, Roiling Waters (Wounded Waters Bleeding), Wounded Waters Bleeding, A Real Flair for Discord (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble)</sub>
  > "Return" here means to Add back into the targeted land. In the case of Strife tokens, they can go onto any Invaders, not necessarily the ones that they were removed from. This also counts as adding those tokens for the purposes of, e.g., Grinning Trickster's A Real Flair for Discord special rule.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka5d"></a>**On Call to Trade, what can I Gather if the Terror Level is 2 or lower? The icon doesn't look quite right.** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka5d))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Iconography, Call to Trade</sub>
  > You can gather a Town. The icon is an older version of the Town icon that snuck through. (It was changed because it looked too much like the City icon.)
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ofsk"></a>**Using Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power - if you play the gained Major Power by committing to Forget at end of turn, can you do other things with it? (Like Forget it for a Choice Event / another Major.)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ofsk))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Forgetting, Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power</sub>
  > Yes. Treat it as a triggered action: "At end of turn, Forget the gained Power Card."
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geal"></a>**What happens if Cast Down into the Briny Deep sinks a board where a land is under Absolute Stasis?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geal))

  > If you want and you have a convenient coaster or other object that can represent the Stasis'd land, then the Stasis'd land survives. If it is disjoint from the rest of the island, consider it Range 2 from the land(s) nearest to it, per the Archipelago rules in Jagged Earth. If you don't want to do this, or have no …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8oi"></a>**What happens if you destroy the entire island with Cast Down into the Briny Deep?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8oi))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Sacrifice victory, Cast Down into the Briny Deep</sub>
  > You win a Sacrifice Victory (p. 12). You have won (since all the Invaders are destroyed) but you have also lost (since there is no Spirit presence left on the island). Thematically, it's also something of a Pyrrhic victory - but some Spirits of water and deep earth would survive.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqe"></a>**What is the exact timing of "you may do both?" on cards like "Growth Through Sacrifice"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqe))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Timing, Growth Through Sacrifice, Unleash a Torrent of the Self's Own Essence</sub>
  > It's checked at the moment you need to know, so as soon as you must choose A ~or~ B. For "Growth Through Sacrifice," you check the threshold as soon as you face the choice between "add a Presence" and "remove a Blight" (to see if you can do both). If you don't have 2 Sun at that point, it doesn't matter if you get 2 …
- [ ] <a id="q-unlock-the-gates-of-deepest-power-forget-optional"></a>**When using *Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power*, can target Spirit voluntarily choose to Forget when gaining a Major Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/unlock-the-gates-of-deepest-power-forget-optional))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Forgetting, Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power</sub>
  > Yes, target Spirit is merely given the option not to Forget, not a requirement to take advantage of that benefit. That being said, Forgetting anyway is almost never beneficial.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gekj"></a>**When you get the threshold on Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, can you choose **not** to use the elemental threshold on the Power you gain?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gekj))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power</sub>
  > No. You must follow the instructions as written as best you can if you choose to activate the Unlock the Gates threshold.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfhc"></a>**When you use a Power outside of its normal phase (e.g., during the Invader Phase via the Rouse the Spirits event), if Repeats have been granted to you (e.g., from Gift of Strength), can you use them then?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfhc))

  > Changed answer: Not to use the Power twice at that time. Unlike "Lightning's Boon" or "Sky Stretches to Shore," you're not actually changing the Power's speed, you're just using it (once) ignoring speed. Repeating a Power lets you use it again in the phase matching the Power's actual speed. The first-pass answer was …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfbe"></a>**Whose Presence does the range in Unrelenting Growth target from?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfbe))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Unrelenting Growth</sub>
  > It measures range from the target Spirit, since it's part of instructions telling the target what to do.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfgv"></a>**Why is there a copy of Sea Monsters in Jagged Earth *and* in Branch & Claw? What's the difference?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfgv))

The new text reads: "Add 1 [Beasts]. If Invaders are present, 2 [Fear] per [Beasts] (max. 8 [Fear]). 3 Damage per [Beasts]. 1 …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfsf"></a>**Does Flame's Fury boost damage to Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfsf))
  <br><sub>Promo, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth · Damage, Twilight Fog Brings Madness, Flame's Fury (Heart of the Wildfire), Might Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth), Territorial Aggression (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > With Flame's Fury, you must boost one instance of Damage of your choice, either to Invaders or Dahan. Usually, you will choose to do that Damage to Invaders, but with a power like Twilight Fog Brings Madness, you would have to do extra Damage to Dahan. Note that this is different from Badlands, which separately …
- [ ] <a id="q-threatening-flames-and-isolate"></a>**Does Threatening Flames grant bonus Fear if it can't move Invaders due to Isolate?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/threatening-flames-and-isolate))
  <br><sub>Promo, Feather & Flame · Isolate, Threatening Flames (Heart of the Wildfire), Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > Yes. This doesn't follow from a strict reading of the card, but is both the intended interaction and thematically appropriate.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcg2"></a>**How does "+1 Damage with Damage-dealing Powers" (e.g., Flame's Fury) work? What exactly does it increase?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcg2))
  <br><sub>Promo, Jagged Earth, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Damage, Badlands, Triggered Actions, Heart of the Wildfire, Might Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth), Creepers Tear into Mortar (A Spread of Rampant Green)</sub>
  > For every power that does Damage, it increases one part of that Damage by 1. For instance, if a power does "1 damage to each Invader", Flame's Fury would let you do 2 damage to one of those Invaders. If a power does "1 Damage to each Town/City", Flame's Fury would let you do 2 damage to one of the Towns or Cities. If …
- [ ] <a id="q-threatening-flames-in-a-land-with-no-explorers-or-towns"></a>**How much Fear does Threatening Flames add when targeting a land with Cities, but no Explorers or Towns?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/threatening-flames-in-a-land-with-no-explorers-or-towns))
  <br><sub>Promo, Feather & Flame · Threatening Flames (Heart of the Wildfire), Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > 2 or 4 Fear, depending on whether or not each adjacent land has your Presence.
- [ ] <a id="q-saving-presence-from-destruction-by-moving-it"></a>**Can "Flowing and Silent Forms Dart By" save Presence from destruction even if that Presence can't be pushed? (e.g., if the only adjacent lands are under "Absolute Stasis")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/saving-presence-from-destruction-by-moving-it))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Saved From Destruction, Shroud of Silent Mist, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Mountain Home (Volcano Looming High), Volcano Looming High, Flowing and Silent Forms Dark By (Shroud of Silent Mist)</sub>
  > No, "Flowing and Silent Forms Dart By" can only save Presence if that Presence can leave the land where it's being destroyed. The Dark Fire Aspect, on the other hand, does not check if the Push is possible; it'll save 1 Presence no matter what.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfr8"></a>**Can the damage done from Blood Draws Predators be boosted above 3?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfr8))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Blood Draws Predators</sub>
  > Yes. The limit is on the damage done by the card itself, not by any outside boosts from effects like Badlands or Flame's Fury.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfou"></a>**Can Weave Together the Fabric of Place affect victory/loss condition X?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfou))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Victory and Defeat, Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Kingdom of Scotland, Dahan Insurrection, Ward the Shores</sub>
  > Yes, Weave Together the Fabric of Place makes the lands one land for all purposes, including victory/loss for conditions like in the Dahan Insurrection scenario.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefe"></a>**Can you choose a land joined by Weave Together the Fabric of Place twice for a Fear card that asks each player to choose a different land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefe))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > No, the joined land is no longer two different lands, and the two lands that went into making it don't exist independently at that time.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb2"></a>**Can you forget Draw Towards a Consuming Void to satisfy the requirements on its own threshold?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb2))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Forgetting, Non-elemental thresholds, Draw Towards a Consuming Void</sub>
  > Yes, and you continue resolving the Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefb"></a>**Do Defend effects stack normally with Weave Together the Fabric of Place?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefb))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Isolate, Defend, Health, Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > Yes, if you join a land with Defend 3 and another land with Defend 4, the combined land has Defend 7. Similarly, if one land was Isolated before the join, then the joint land will be isolated afterwards, or if Dahan got a Health bonus in one land, they would now get it in the joint land.
- [ ] <a id="q-energy-oceans-hungry-grasp-bargains"></a>**Does the Energy Ocean’s Hungry Grasp gains from Drowning count as gaining Energy for the purpose of Bargains?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/energy-oceans-hungry-grasp-bargains))

  > Yes. Ocean doesn’t get to skip out of paying for its Bargains just because its main source of Energy isn’t one of the common sources. That being said, Ocean is not required to exchanged Drowned Invaders for Energy at a particular time, so it can save up Invaders on turns when it will not gain much Energy (avoiding …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geay"></a>**Does the Presence added via the threshold of Dream of the Untouched Land come from Presence Tracks?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geay))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Presence, Dream of the Untouched Land</sub>
  > Yes. As usual, Spirits may reposition Presence already on the island.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged3"></a>**Does Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone count Blight in the target land *and* adjacent lands, or do you have to choose?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged3))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone</sub>
  > It counts both. "in or adjacent" would be better phrased as "in/adjacent".
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef2"></a>**For Dream of the Untouched Land, what orientation should the new board be placed in? Can it use Archipelago rules?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef2))
