  > It's not specified, so you choose any orientation that would be legal during setup and fully interlocks with a side of the target board. You may not use Archipelago rules, as that is not "next to". If all three interlockable edges of target board are accounted for (e.g., if target board is the "face" of the standard …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedm"></a>**For Forests of Living Obsidian, when do you check whether the origin land is your Sacred Site?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedm))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Forests of Living Obsidian, Thunderspeaker</sub>
  > At the time you reach that instruction in the card text - so, e.g., if you're Thunderspeaker and just moved out all your Presence along with the Dahan, you no longer have a Sacred Site there.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefz"></a>**For Powers that let you skip some # of Invader Actions, how/when do you decide which Invader Actions to skip?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefz))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Skipping Actions, A Dreadful Tide of Scurrying Flesh (Many Minds Move as One), Infestation of Venomous Spiders, Exaltation of the Incandescent Sky, Blinding Glare (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Many Minds Move as One</sub>
  > You decide when they come up. You can skip as many as you want, up to the limit given. You are not obliged to use all skips to which you are entitled (you could always be saving them for a future Invader Action caused by something unusual). Unused skips do not carry over to the next turn (as normal for effects that …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geaz"></a>**For the power Storm-Swath, what happens if the origin land is the same as the target land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geaz))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Targeting, Storm-Swath</sub>
  > You do 2 damage to each Invader - the "target land" and "origin land" effects each still apply, just to the same land.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedl"></a>**For the threshold of Dream of the Untouched Land: Should it respect the "don't use together" icons on the boards? What if we have 6 players?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedl))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Island Boards, Dream of the Untouched Land</sub>
  > No, randomly select from all unused boards. If you have 6 players, you're component-limited (and thus don't add the board) unless you happen to have a spare copy of the game handy. If you are unable to add an extra board, you do not get to skip Builds and "Each Board" Adversary actions.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geed"></a>**How do Badlands / Flame's Fury work with "Veil the Night's Hunt"? How about Trickster's innate "Why Don't You and Them Fight"? Or (other Power that specifies unusual conditions on what can be damaged)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geed))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Veil the Night's Hunt, Slave Rebellion, Why Don't You and Them Fight (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Suffocating Shroud (Shroud of Silent Mists), Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot)</sub>
  > For "Veil the Night's Hunt," deal 1 bonus Damage to an Invader that's already taking Damage. (You can't use it to deal damage to more Invaders than there are Dahan in the land.) For "Why Don't You and Them Fight," damage boosts can only apply to the Dahan and Invader taking damage from each other. Badlands and …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gej2"></a>**How does "Absolute Stasis" interact with victory condition X or loss condition Y?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gej2))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Victory and Defeat, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Tsardom of Russia, Kingdom of Scotland, Dahan Insurrection, Guard the Isle's Heart</sub>
  > It doesn't, per the text on the card. The land is an ordinary land for any game-end conditions. For instance: - You can't win by using Stasis on the land with the last Cities; - You can't lose by using Stasis on the last of a Spirit's Presence - Beasts in that land count for Russia's additional loss condition; and - …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedj"></a>**How does Bargains of Power and Protection work if you use it multiple times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedj))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Bargain, Bargains of Power and Protection</sub>
  > Additively. If you've set aside a total of 6 Presence, Dahan within Range 1 of your Presence provide Defend 6, and you gain 6 less Energy per turn.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-does-voice-of-command-interact-with-events-boosting-invader-damage-or-counting-Invaders"></a>**How does Voice of Command interact with Events that boost Invader damage or count Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-does-voice-of-command-interact-with-events-boosting-invader-damage-or-counting-Invaders))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Ravage, Voice of Command, Fearful Mobs, Xenophobia, Increasing Aggression, Cooperation Among Towns</sub>
  > Events usually interact with "sides" (attacker vs counterattacker): - Boosts to total Invader Damage only benefit the Invaders' side - not the Explorers fighting alongside Dahan. - Boosts to "Invader Damage against Dahan" (Xenophobia) apply to damage done to the Dahan side - it doesn't matter if the damage is …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geaj"></a>**If "Weave Together the Fabric of Place" joins lands on two adjacent boards, how does that work with "On each board" effects?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geaj))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · On Each Board, Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Eager Explorers, Mapmakers Chart the Wild, Habsburg Mining Expedition, Civic Engagement</sub>
  > The conjoined land is considered to be on both boards. An "on each board" effect could affect the conjoined land twice. In particular: - A Build Action in that land can be the "first successful Build Action" on both boards for an Event like "Eager Explorers" (and thus cause 2 Explorers to be added). - If an Invader …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ghbu"></a>**If a Power on a Power Card has been modified, do those modifiers persist if the Power Card leaves play?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ghbu))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Action Modifiers, Speed (Fast/Slow), Range Boosts, Slip the Flow of Time (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Fractured Days Split the Sky</sub>
  > No, any modifiers to a Power in play, whether modifying Speed, Range, Target, or Effect, are to the Power in play, not to the Power Card itself. If the Power Card leaves play, it does not retain those modifications, nor does it automatically re-gain them if it is played again that turn.
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-an-incarna-under-settle-into-hunting-grounds"></a>**If a Spirit with an Incarna uses Settle Into Hunting Grounds, can the Incarna still move that turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-an-incarna-under-settle-into-hunting-grounds))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Counts As, Incarna, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Leave a Trail of Deathly Silence (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > Yes, but only if you choose not to count it as Presence for that action (and therefore not count it as Badlands or Beasts with Settle Into Hunting Grounds either). For instance, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine using Leave a Trail of Deathly Silence can't get the Badlands bonus if they choose to Push the Incarna.
- [ ] <a id="q-unique-power-transferred-forgotten-regained"></a>**If a Unique Power Card is transferred to another Spirit and then Forgotten, which Spirit is eligible to get it back via an effect that re-gains Forgotten Unique Powers, like *Tell the Old Stories*?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/unique-power-transferred-forgotten-regained))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Forgetting, Transferring Power Cards, Entwined Power, Tell the Old Stories, Solidify Echoes of Majesty Past, Mentor Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Any Spirit that has had that Unique Power Card (whether in hand, in play, or in discard) at some point during play. This may mean that multiple Spirits are eligible to re-gain a particular Unique Power Card.
- [ ] <a id="q-nested-doubling-with-pour-time-sideways"></a>**If an Adversary/Event/Scenario Action causes an Invader Action, does "Pour Time Sideways" double both the the original Action and then twice double the Invader Action?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/nested-doubling-with-pour-time-sideways))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Action Modifiers, Action tree, Nested Actions, Triggered Actions, Pour Time Sideways (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Fractured Days Split the Sky</sub>
  > No, "Pour Time Sideways" doesn't double anything that's already doubled. For example, if time is poured onto a board twice, then "On each board: Choose a land with Cities. Build there." (the "Provincial Seat" Event) makes players choose a land on that board three times, but only Builds once in each chosen land for a …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2y"></a>**If an Explorer with Strife is affected by Voice of Command, does it still deal damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2y))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Strife, Voice of Command</sub>
  > Yes. The rules for Strife start “Whenever Invaders Damage the Dahan and/or the Land …” An Explorer under the sway of Voice of Command is damaging other Invaders, and so is not affected by the Strife.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefc"></a>**If an inland and Coastal land get Woven Together by the Fabric of Place, can Ocean leave their Presence inland during Time Passes?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefc))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Volcano Looming High, Mountain Home (Volcano Looming High), Lure of the Deep Wilderness, Home of the Island's Heart (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > No. The joined land is Coastal, but the split when "Weave Together the Fabric of Place" expires is considered a move, and Ocean's Presence cannot move to an inland land. (This is a special case of the general ruling around illegal movement when "Weave Together the Fabric of Place" expires.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gec6"></a>**If I meet the threshold for Draw Towards a Consuming Void, can I choose a different target land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gec6))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Draw Towards a Consuming Void</sub>
  > No. You are not Repeating the Power, you're just making a single use of the Power do more.
- [ ] <a id="q-strong-and-constant-currents-in-an-inland-land"></a>**If the text of Strong and Constant Currents is resolved in an inland land (e.g., due to Let's See What Happens), can it move Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/strong-and-constant-currents-in-an-inland-land))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Strong and Constant Currents, Let's See What Happens (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Yes. "One other Coastal land" doesn't re-check if the target land is Coastal — it just means "a Coastal land that is not target land."
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedp"></a>**If two lands are joined using Weave Together the Fabric of Place, is the conjoined land considered adjacent to itself?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedp))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Land Types, Push, Move, Gather, Changed rulings, Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > Changed ruling: No. (The old ruling was creating waaay too many weird edge cases.) The original ruling was made because the definition of "Coastal" is "adjacent to the printed Ocean", and a Coastal land Woven together with an in-play Ocean should definitely still be Coastal. Turns out it's much better to just rule …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb0"></a>**If you target yourself with Gift of Twinned Days in a solo game, can you repeat your lowest-cost power twice? If so, can you choose a different power to be ‘lowest-cost’ the second time?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb0))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Targeting, Repeat, Gift of Twinned Days, Serpent Wakes in Power (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Yes, and yes. It works differently than Serpent Wakes in Power (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island) because that innate says "Other Spirits with (condition) do likewise", rather than working via targeting particular Spirits.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geei"></a>**If you use The Past Returns Again on a Stage II card, do you evaluate the Escalation effect again?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geei))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Stage II escalation, The Past Returns Again (Fractured Days Split the Sky)</sub>
  > Yes. It still counts as being resolved "for the first time" (JE rulebook p. 27), since it was turned face-down and returned to the Invader Deck; it has no "memory" of what it did the previous time it left the Invader deck.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ged1"></a>**If you use Weave Together the Fabric of Place, what is the number of the conjoined land? Which board is it on?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ged1))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Terrain, Land Types, Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Tsunami, Cast Down into the Briny Deep, Fragments of Yesteryear</sub>
  > For land types based on contents ("a land with Blight") or geography ("a land adjacent to Mountains"), check the contents or geography of the conjoined land normally. Whether a land is Coastal bases off of its geography (whether it is adjacent to the printed Ocean area), so joining a Coastal land with an Inland land …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebf"></a>**If you've used "Settle into Hunting-Grounds" (your Presence counts as Beasts and Badlands), and draw the "Beasts Prey on the Injured" Event (Beasts count as Badlands), does your Presence now count as 2 Badlands each?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebf))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Counts As, Badlands, Beasts, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Beasts Prey on the Injured (Beasts Event), Many Minds Move as One</sub>
  > No. "Piece A counts as Piece B" changes (roughly) "what does this physical object represent"; applying it multiple times has no effect.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebb"></a>**In which land does the threshold of Sleep and Never Waken generate fear?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebb))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Fear, Targeting, Sleep and Never Waken</sub>
  > The target land. All effects of a land-targeting Power happen in the target land unless otherwise specified somehow.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebc"></a>**Is Trees Radiate Celestial Brilliance limited to targeting a Jungle without Blight?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebc))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Targeting, Trees Radiate Celestial Brilliance</sub>
  > No, it can target any Jungle OR any land without Blight. The slash works just like it does for "Remove 1 Town/City". If it were "Jungle without Blight", there would be an "AND" or "&" between them.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gec7"></a>**Wait, is the target land of Bargains of Power and Protection relevant?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gec7))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Bargains of Power and Protection</sub>
  > None of the text effects affect the target land - but you do need to have a valid target land in order to enact the bargain.
- [ ] <a id="q-Weave-and-marked-lands"></a>**What happens if a land that's been marked (e.g., by "Bargain of Coursing Paths") is merged with "Weave Together the Fabric of Place"? How about if the merged land becomes marked?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Weave-and-marked-lands))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Bargain of Coursing Paths, Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > Leave the marker where it was, marking a specific printed land on the board. Since the merged land inherits the traits of its constituent lands, it's also marked, with all of the upsides and downsides that come with that. When "Weave Together the Fabric of Place" expires, the originally-marked land remains marked. If …
- [ ] <a id="q-gift-bargain-power-protection"></a>**What happens if you gift Bargains of Power and Protection to another Spirit, who uses it? Now that the Reminder Card is in front of them, do you still get the benefit?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/gift-bargain-power-protection))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Reminder Cards, Bargain, Bargains of Power and Protection, Bargain of Coursing Paths, Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > The permanent effects of the Bargain persist and are associated with you even if you don't have the Power Card any more. It doesn't matter who the Reminder Card is in front of; it's just a reminder. If table configuration allows, it is probably most convenient to put the Reminder Card between the players who have …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedb"></a>**What happens if you give a Range boost to someone who's used Bargains of Power and Protection? Does it matter if they played Bargains on that turn or an earlier turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedb))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Range, Range Boosts, Bargains of Power and Protection, Reaching Grasp, Sky Stretches to Shore, Scream Disease Into the Wind</sub>
  > In either case, for the turn the Range boost is active, it increases the "within Range 1 of your Presence" at which that Spirit affects Dahan. The boost wears off at the end of the turn, even though Bargains of Power and Protection doesn't. This includes range bonuses to "your Power Card" (e.g., Volcano Looming …
- [ ] <a id="q-how-does-badlands-interact-with-voice-of-command"></a>**What happens when Invaders Ravage in a land with a Badlands affected by Voice of Command? Can Badlands damage that would normally be done to Dahan be assigned to Explorers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-does-badlands-interact-with-voice-of-command))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Ravage, Voice of Command</sub>
  > The Explorers are still considered Invaders - boosting Damage done to them does not count as boosting Damage to Dahan. Voice of Command changes what "side" the Explorers are fighting on during Ravage (from "attackers" to "counterattackers"), but does not change what type of pieces the Explorers are, which is what …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedk"></a>**What sources of Energy gain do Bargains affect?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedk))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Energy, Bargain, Bargains of Power and Protection, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > All sources: Growth, Gain Energy, power effects, events, etc. It never actually takes away Energy you have banked, though. If you have trouble remembering to gain less Energy, covering up numbers on your Energy track can be an easy reminder.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfpx"></a>**When exactly do you have to pay a "cost to use" on a Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfpx))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Cost To Use, Triggered Actions, Scream Disease Into the Wind, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Fiery Vengeance (Vengeance as a Burning Plague), The Past Returns Again (Fractured Days Split the Sky)</sub>
  > Before targeting it - "using" / "resolving" a Power includes choosing a target land and verifying that it's legal. This can be relevant for cards like Scream Disease Into the Wind, which set up a triggered action after a Power is used on a land.
- [ ] <a id="q-gift-of-twinned-days-being-the-lowest-cost-power-card-in-play"></a>**When using "Gift of Twinned Days," what happens if it's your lowest-cost Power Card in play?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/gift-of-twinned-days-being-the-lowest-cost-power-card-in-play))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Repeat, Gift of Twinned Days</sub>
  > If you have multiple Power Cards in play that cost 1 Energy, you can break the tie for "lowest" in favor of one of the other Power Cards in order to repeat that one. If all of your other Power Cards cost 2 or more Energy, then the only Power Card you can repeat is "Gift of Twinned Days." You should generally decline …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfq4"></a>**When Weave Together the Fabric of Place expires, what if pieces in the joined land can't be legally moved to one (or both) of the printed lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfq4))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Illegally placed pieces, Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > If a piece can moved legally, it must be. If it can't be legally moved to any of the constituent lands, then it is cleaned up to one of them (player's choice which). This applies to all pieces, not just Invaders/Dahan. (Cleaning up is not an action, not considered a move, and does not trigger actions; it is a …
- [ ] <a id="q-avoiding-archipelago-islet-cost-with-entwined-power"></a>**With an Archipelago, can Entwined Power's 'target via each others Presence' let you avoid paying 1 Energy to target a Spirit you don't otherwise share an islet with?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/avoiding-archipelago-islet-cost-with-entwined-power))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Archipelago, Entwined Power, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Yes. The check for "Do you share an islet with the Spirit you're targeting?" (and 1-Energy cost if not) is part of targeting the Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-shroud-of-silent-mist-gather-entwined-spirit-presence"></a>**With Entwined Power, can Shroud of Silent Mist Gather another Spirit's Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-shroud-of-silent-mist-gather-entwined-spirit-presence))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Targeting, Move, Shroud of Silent Mist, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Entwined Power, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Yes. When Shroud of Silent Mist targets a Power (which its Presence-Gather is part of) it treats the other Spirit's Presence as also being its Presence. If the effects of the special rule didn't have anything to do with targeting the Power, it might be more ambiguous (since Entwined Power is only supposed to affect …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebq"></a>**With Settle into Hunting Grounds, can your Presence count as both Badlands and Beasts for the same action? (e.g., if an Event does Damage per Beasts)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Beasts, Counts As, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds</sub>
  > Yes. It can also count as just one or the other.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfrp"></a>**With Terror Turns to Madness, if Fear from it gets you to the next Terror Level, do you also get the next Terror Level's effects?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfrp))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Resolving Powers, Terror Turns to Madness</sub>
  > No, it's a 3-way conditional: you only check the Terror Level at the time you reach the first of the lines.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-does-ravage-damage-work-with-voice-of-command"></a>**With Voice of Command, can Explorers take Damage from Towns/Cities? How does distributing damage work when you have a mixed group of Dahan and Explorers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-does-ravage-damage-work-with-voice-of-command))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Dahan, Explorer, Ravage, Damage, Voice of Command</sub>
  > Yes, Explorers can take Damage. You can destroy Explorers before Dahan. (And will usually want to do so.) You can also mix-and-match - destroy an Explorer or two, then deal damage to a Dahan - if for some reason you want to leave some Explorers alive.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-gift-of-furious-might-boost-gift-of-searing-heat"></a>**Can "Gift of Furious Might" be used on Rising Heat of Stone and Sand to increase the damage from "Gift of Searing Heat"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-gift-of-furious-might-boost-gift-of-searing-heat))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Damage, Action Modifiers, Gift of Searing Heat (Rising Heat of Stone and Sand), Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Rising Heat of Stone and Sand, Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot</sub>
  > Yes. "Gift of Furious Might" lets a Spirit boost the Damage of one of its Powers by +3. "Gift of Searing Heat" is one of Rising Heat's Powers (even though it's working with/through another Spirit) so it qualifies.
- [ ] <a id="q-repeats-and-gift-of-furious-might"></a>**Does the Damage bonus from "Gift of Furious Might" persist when the affected Power is Repeated?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/repeats-and-gift-of-furious-might))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Repeat, Action Modifiers, Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot</sub>
  > No, the bonus only increases one instance of dealing Damage, rather than boosting one Power for the entire turn. (This is more clear starting in the second printing.)
- [ ] <a id="q-gift-of-furious-might-with-powers-that-deal-damage-mulitple-times"></a>**If a Power deals Damage multiple times, can the Damage bonus from "Gift of Furious Might" be split across those instances of Damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/gift-of-furious-might-with-powers-that-deal-damage-mulitple-times))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Damage, Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot, Vengeance of the Dead</sub>
  > Yes, so long as all of the Damage is being done during the same use of the Power. For example, if a Spirit uses "Vigor of the Breaking Dawn" with its threshold and pushes Dahan into two different lands (dealing Damage in three lands), they could: - Deal +3 Damage in one land; - Deal +2 Damage in one land and +1 …
- [ ] <a id="q-when-damage-is-boosted-with-gift-of-furious-might-does-the-bonus-damage-obey-the-same-restrictions"></a>**When "Gift of Furious Might" boosts Damage that's limited in some way – like the first level of Rising Heat's innate, which only damages Explorers – is the bonus damage also limited?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/when-damage-is-boosted-with-gift-of-furious-might-does-the-bonus-damage-obey-the-same-restrictions))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Damage, Action Modifiers, Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Scorch With Waves of Heat (Rising Heat of Stone and Sand), Tempest of Leaves and Branches (Sun-Bright Whirlwind), Veil the Night's Hunt</sub>
  > Yes. This is true for all sorts of limitations - type of piece that can be damaged, number of pieces that can be damaged ("Veil the Night's Hunt" / "Tempest of Leaves and Branches"), etc.
- [ ] <a id="q-when-do-you-choose-to-apply-the-bonus-from-gift-of-furious-might"></a>**When does the target Spirit choose when to use the Damage bonus of Gift of Furious Might?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/when-do-you-choose-to-apply-the-bonus-from-gift-of-furious-might))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Damage, Triggered Actions, Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot</sub>
  > At the moment they do Damage. They don't have to "lock in" which Power gets the bonus in advance. This could be much later in the turn than the original Power use if the Power sets up a later condition for doing Damage (e.g., Vengeance of the Dead).
- [ ] <a id="q-choosing-a-new-destination-for-bargain-of-coursing-paths"></a>**Can "Bargain of Coursing Paths" move pieces to a different land each time it triggers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/choosing-a-new-destination-for-bargain-of-coursing-paths))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > Yes, you can choose a different destination land each time, so long as the pieces that arrive from a single action all go to the same land.
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-pieces-back-into-a-marked-land-with-bargain-of-coursing-paths"></a>**Can Bargain of Coursing Paths move pieces back into one of the marked lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-pieces-back-into-a-marked-land-with-bargain-of-coursing-paths))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Move, Triggered Actions, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > Yes. The pieces are still considered moved for the purpose of effects that check for that. This won't normally trigger Bargain of Coursing Paths again, since it can't trigger itself.
- [ ] <a id="q-skipping-multiple-actions-in-a-land-with-exaltation-of-the-incandescent-sky"></a>**Can the threshold of "Exaltation of the Incandescent Sky" skip multiple Invader actions in one land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/skipping-multiple-actions-in-a-land-with-exaltation-of-the-incandescent-sky))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Skipping Actions, Exaltation of the Incandescent Sky</sub>
  > No. You choose four total lands at once, rather than choosing a land four times.
- [ ] <a id="q-checking-for-dahan-with-the-threshold-of-inspire-the-release-of-stolen-lands"></a>**Can the threshold of Inspire the Release of Stolen Lands remove Invaders from lands without Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/checking-for-dahan-with-the-threshold-of-inspire-the-release-of-stolen-lands))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Inspire the Release of Stolen Lands</sub>
  > Yes, but only if there are Dahan in target land.
- [ ] <a id="q-once-per-turn-actions-granted-by-powers"></a>**Do the effects of the threshold of Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone count as your Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/once-per-turn-actions-granted-by-powers))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone, Responsibilities to the Dead (Finder of Paths Unseen), Finder of Paths Unseen, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, Violence Aspect (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares)</sub>
  > Yes. Whether this is good or bad for you depends on the Spirit that used "Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone."
- [ ] <a id="q-fragments-of-yesteryear-thematic-options"></a>**Does Fragments of Yesteryear restore the Disease and Beasts added as part of Branch & Claw / Jagged Earth setup on the balanced boards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/fragments-of-yesteryear-thematic-options))

However, restoring them is the more thematic option, so you're welcome to do that if your group is willing to take the extra time to figure out where the Beasts started. If you want to put in a bit more work, the most …
- [ ] <a id="q-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone-choice"></a>**Does the marked Beasts from Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone need to move in order to do Damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone-choice))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > No, the marked Beasts will do Fear and Damage in its land no matter what. The choice is just whether or not to Push it first. Resolving the second sentence is not conditional on making particular choices in the first sentence.
- [ ] <a id="q-unearth-a-beast-threshold-on-the-turn-the-marked-beasts-token-is-created"></a>**Does the threshold of Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone let the marked Beasts token do Damage on the turn it's created?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/unearth-a-beast-threshold-on-the-turn-the-marked-beasts-token-is-created))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > Yes.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-does-rumbling-earthquakes-damage-bonus-work"></a>**How does Rumbling Earthquakes' ability to ignore Health bonuses interact with already-Damaged Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-does-rumbling-earthquakes-damage-bonus-work))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Health, Damage, Rumbling Earthquakes</sub>
  > Rumbling Earthquakes ignores Health bonuses only on pieces it damages. [Nature Incarnate, p. 21] (So, e.g., destroying a Durable town with 2 Damage still requires allocating 1 Damage to it.) Note that Health penalties are not ignored.
- [ ] <a id="q-multiple-uses-of-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone"></a>**If Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone is used multiple times with threshold, how many times do Marked Beasts tokens get to do Damage in the Slow Phase?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/multiple-uses-of-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > Each Marked Beasts token gets to move and do Damage once each Slow Phase, as a separate Action.
- [ ] <a id="q-choosing-to-destroy-the-beasts-token-marked-by-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone"></a>**If you have a choice between destroying a normal Beasts token and a Beasts token marked by Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone, can you pick the marked Beasts? If so, what happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/choosing-to-destroy-the-beasts-token-marked-by-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Destroy, Remove, Replace, Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone, Tsardom of Russia, A Dreadful Tide of Scurrying Flesh (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > Yes, you can pick the marked Beasts, and it's generally advantageous to do so: if it would be destroyed, removed, or replaced, skip those effects. It won't count as destroyed, removed, or replaced for effects that scale off the number of Beasts that leave the board, like A Dreadful Tide of Scurrying Flesh or Pent-Up …
- [ ] <a id="q-instead-in-the-blinding-glare-threshold"></a>**If you hit the threshold of Blinding Glare, does the "Instead" apply to just the Fear option or to the entire threshold?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/instead-in-the-blinding-glare-threshold))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Blinding Glare (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > The entire threshold. To use either of the threshold options, you must give up the base effect.
- [ ] <a id="q-Transformative-Sacrifice-threshold-timing"></a>**If you target yourself with Transformative Sacrifice, do your new Powers help you hit the threshold?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Transformative-Sacrifice-threshold-timing))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Elements, Timing, Transformative Sacrifice</sub>
  > No, the threshold is checked before taking any cards, much less playing them and getting their Elements.
- [ ] <a id="q-looping-bargain-of-coursing-paths"></a>**If you use Bargain of Coursing Paths twice, can you move pieces back and forth between the marked lands an unlimited number of times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/looping-bargain-of-coursing-paths))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Move, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > No, the first bargain can't trigger itself, even indirectly. In almost all cases, the pieces can move once from the first bargain and once from the second bargain, but won't move a third time. Cleverly overlapping multiple bargains in the same land(s) can squeeze out more than one move per bargain, but this is …
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-pieces-into-multiple-lands-marked-by-bargain-of-coursing-paths"></a>**What happens if one Action adds or moves pieces into two lands that were marked by "Bargain of Coursing Paths"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-pieces-into-multiple-lands-marked-by-bargain-of-coursing-paths))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Move, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > They all move together to the same land, since that Action only triggers "Bargain of Coursing Paths" once. In the less common case that you move pieces into lands marked by different uses of "Bargain of Coursing Paths," it'll trigger each of those bargains separately. Each bargain can then move pieces to a different …
- [ ] <a id="q-card-type-of-minor-powers-that-start-the-game-in-hand"></a>**When an Aspect starts the game with a Minor Power in its hand, does that Minor Power count as a Unique Power (e.g., for Tell the Old Stories)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/card-type-of-minor-powers-that-start-the-game-in-hand))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Tell the Old Stories, Violence Aspect (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Warrior Aspect (Thunderspeaker), Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Nourishing Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth), Solidify Echoes of Majesty Past</sub>
  > No, it's still a Minor Power. The type of a card never changes, no matter where it starts the game. If you're ever unsure which type a Power Card is, check the back of the card.
- [ ] <a id="q-origin-land-for-powers-with-unlimited-range"></a>**When using a land-targeting Power with Range "—," which land is the origin land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/origin-land-for-powers-with-unlimited-range))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Range, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Emerge from the Dread Night Wind (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Frightful Keening (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium), Radiant and Hallowed Grove (Towering Roots of the Jungle), Lost in the Endless Dark (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine)</sub>
  > In the rather rare case that it matters, you can choose any land with your Presence to be the origin of the Power. If you don't have any Presence to use as the origin (e.g., because all of your Presence is under Absolute Stasis or in the Endless Dark), you can't use the Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-adding-disease-with-plague-ships-threshold"></a>**When using the threshold of Plague Ships Sail to Distant Ports, must Energy be spent to add the final Disease?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-disease-with-plague-ships-threshold))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Plague Ships Sail to Distant Ports</sub>
  > No, the energy cost is only to remove the top card of the Fear Deck from the game. It's possible to resolve the threshold as just "1 Fear. 3 Damage. Add 1 Disease." if you don't want to pay.
- [ ] <a id="q-number-of-cards-transformative-sacrifice-can-take-and-play"></a>**When using Transformative Sacrifice with threshold, what's the maximum number of Minor Powers that Target Spirit can Take and Play?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/number-of-cards-transformative-sacrifice-can-take-and-play))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Transformative Sacrifice</sub>
  > Four: three from the base effect and one from the threshold. The Presence removed from the Presence track doesn't double-count.

## Effect Rules

- [ ] <a id="q-adding-or-moving-pieces-to-locations-not-in-play"></a>**Can Blight/Event/Fear Card effects add or move pieces to locations that aren't lands if those locations are in play (e.g., adding pieces to the Ocean if Ocean's Hungry Grasp is in play)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-or-moving-pieces-to-locations-not-in-play))

  > Generally no, pieces can only be added to a non-land location if explicitly allowed by whatever puts that location into play or temporarily lets that location be treated as a land. So, for example, an Event can add Blight to an Ocean if Ocean's Hungry Grasp has Presence on that board or the Kingdom of Scotland is the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geg1"></a>**Does a cascade count as part of the action that added the original Blight (e.g., for effects that care about Blight being added during a Ravage Action)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geg1))
  <br><sub>Core game, Feather & Flame, Promo 2, Promo, Jagged Earth · Ravage, Blight, Victory and Defeat, Solo games, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony), Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > Yes. This means that a Ravage in one land can, via cascading, add Blight into another land, even one on another board. For specific examples: - The "Destructive Nature" special rule of Heart of the Wildfire protects its Presence from cascades caused by Spirit Actions. - Against the Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock …
- [ ] <a id="q-vitality-and-adding-two-or-more-blight-at-once"></a>**How does Vitality interact with effects that add two or more Blight at once (e.g., Kingdom of Sweden Level 1)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/vitality-and-adding-two-or-more-blight-at-once))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Core game · Blight, Vitality, Kingdom of Sweden, Towering Roots of the Jungle, Far-Off Wars Touch the Island, Nourishing Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Each Vitality prevents 1 Blight. Depending on the number of Vitality relative to the number of Blight added, this may or may not prevent all of the Blight. If at least 1 Blight is added after accounting for Vitality, destroy Presence and cascade like normal.
- [ ] <a id="q-if-an-invader-with-health-bonus-and-damage-is-moved-where-is-it-destroyed"></a>**If an Invader or Dahan is in a land where it has a Health bonus, takes Damage equal to its normal Health, and then gets moved into a land where it has no Health bonus, where is it actually destroyed? (E.g., for *Vengeance of the Dead*)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/if-an-invader-with-health-bonus-and-damage-is-moved-where-is-it-destroyed))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw, Core game, Nature Incarnate · Health, Damage, Triggered Actions, Durable, Vengeance of the Dead, Blood Draws Predators</sub>
  > The Invader/Dahan is destroyed in the destination land. There's no “in between” state where it loses its Health bonus for being out of the first land, but hasn’t yet arrived in the second land; it’s either in the first land and has a Health bonus or in the second land and doesn’t have a Health bonus. Moving a damaged …
- [ ] <a id="q-chaining-upgrades-and-downgrades"></a>**If multiple pieces would be upgraded or downgraded simultaneously (e.g., with the Lair Aspect), is it possible to upgrade or downgrade the same piece multiple times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/chaining-upgrades-and-downgrades))

  > No. Pick all of the pieces you want to replace first, then upgrade/downgrade them all at the same time. However, it is possible to upgrade/downgrade a piece multiple times if they are replaced as part of different instructions (e.g., downgrading the same Invader with two different thresholds of Lost in the Endless …
- [ ] <a id="q-multiple-changes-to-dahan-counterattack-timing"></a>**If two effects change when Dahan in a land do Damage (e.g., Words of Warning and Warn of Impending Conflict), which one takes precedence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/multiple-changes-to-dahan-counterattack-timing))

So in this example, the best outcome is usually applying Words of Warning first to make all Dahan do Damage at the same time as Invaders, and then using Warn of Impending Conflict to make some of those Dahan strike first. (If you applied them in the other order, then all Dahan would do Damage at the same …
- [ ] <a id="q-repeating-powers-with-changed-speeds"></a>**If you change a Power's speed, which phase can you Repeat it in?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/repeating-powers-with-changed-speeds))

  > Whatever matches the speed the Power was most recently set to, which will almost always be the speed of the original use of the Power. There are a few unusual cases: - If you use a Fast Power (or Power that was made Fast) in the Fast Phase, and then make that Power Slow with "Sky Stretches to Shore," it's now a Slow …
- [ ] <a id="q-using-powers-that-reference-an-incarna-that-is-not-yours"></a>**What happens if a Power that references an Incarna is used by a Spirit that doesn't have that Incarna (e.g., if it's transferred via "Entwined Power")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/using-powers-that-reference-an-incarna-that-is-not-yours))

  > Resolve the Power exactly as written. Depending on the Power and Incarna in question, this may be weaker than when used by the original Spirit. For example, …
- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-choosing-powers-for-once-this-turn-repeats"></a>**When a Power like "Powerstorm" grants target Spirit the option to repeat a Power Card of their choice "once this turn," do they have to immediately choose which Power Card will later be repeated?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-choosing-powers-for-once-this-turn-repeats))

This is particularly relevant if Target Spirit has multiple Slow Power Cards in play, since they can see the outcome of the Invader Phase before choosing …
- [ ] <a id="q-scaling-removal-of-health-worth-of-invaders"></a>**When an action removes N Health worth of Invaders scaling with a condition (e.g., the number of Dahan for Inspire the Release of Stolen Lands), do the Invaders have to be removed in batches of N Health?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/scaling-removal-of-health-worth-of-invaders))

This only applies if the Invaders are all being removed as part of a single action (e.g., in Inspire a Release of Stolen Lands or Wavering Resolve). If "each player" is removing Invaders (e.g., Seek Safety, …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfgr"></a>**When instructed to "Repeat this Power", do you have to obey the origin/range/target land restrictions?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfgr))

  > Only if it doesn't tell you where to Repeat it. If a Repeat specifies where to use it, you obey those instructions, ignoring the original Range and Target restrictions (p. 19) and using the same origin land (for the rare cases where the Power's effect references that land). For example, "The Land Thrashes in Furious …
- [ ] <a id="q-effects-that-check-for-gaining-power-cards-when-getting-power-cards-in-other-ways"></a>**Do effects that interact with “gaining” Power Cards apply to every time a Spirit gets a new Power Card, no matter the method?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/effects-that-check-for-gaining-power-cards-when-getting-power-cards-in-other-ways))
  <br><sub>All · Gaining Power Cards, Taking Power Cards, Transferring Power Cards, Gather Power from the Cool and Dark (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist, Days that Never Were (Fractured Days Split the Sky)</sub>
  > No, only if the Spirit explicitly “gains” that Power Card (usually by drawing four cards and picking one to keep). If they “take” a Power Card, are given a Power Card, or otherwise receive a Power Card without being told to “gain” it, then they have not “gained” a Power Card.
- [ ] <a id="q-removing-or-replacing-a-piece-that-cannot-be-destroyed"></a>**If an effect prevents a piece from being damaged or destroyed, does that also prevent the piece from being removed or replaced?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/removing-or-replacing-a-piece-that-cannot-be-destroyed))

  > No and no. While "destroying," "removing," and "replacing" all result in a component being taken off the island, they're mutually exclusive effects. Similarly, a piece that can't be removed can still be destroyed or replaced. A small number of effects separately protect against more than one of Destroy, Remove, and …
- [ ] <a id="q-splitting-on-each-board-effects"></a>**When an effect does multiple things "on each board," can each thing happen in a different land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/splitting-on-each-board-effects))

For example, "On Each Board: Remove 1 Beasts. Add 1 Disease" (part of the first choice of the "Life's Balance Tilts" Event) can remove a Beasts token from one …
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-pieces-with-on-each-board-actions"></a>**When an effect moves Invaders "on each board," is the chosen board the origin or the destination for those pieces?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-pieces-with-on-each-board-actions))
  <br><sub>All · Move, Gather, Push, On Each Board, Seek Company, Forsake the Barren Land</sub>
  > Whichever it would be if you targeted a land on that board with that effect. So, for example, Gather pieces into a land on that board (from that board or a different board) or Push pieces from a land on that board (to that board or a different board). [Jagged Earth, p. 12]
- [ ] <a id="q-sacrificing-presence-to-prevent-effects-that-do-nothing"></a>**If a "Spirits may prevent this on any/all boards" effect wouldn't do anything on a particular board it's affecting, can Spirits sacrifice Presence there anyway?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/sacrificing-presence-to-prevent-effects-that-do-nothing))

Choosing to sacrifice Presence this way isn't generally advantageous, but can be used to set up for a Power that uses destroyed Presence, like "Blazing Renewal." Some …
- [ ] <a id="q-Do-not-participate-in-Ravage-and-scaling-Defend"></a>**If a Dahan is not participating in Ravage, can it still grant Defend 1 in that Ravage from Bargains of Power and Protection or Canny Defense?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Do-not-participate-in-Ravage-and-scaling-Defend))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gbfw"></a>**What counts as "adding" a piece (e.g., adding an Invader for "Confounding Mists")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gbfw))

- [ ] <a id="q-Moving-towards-or-away"></a>**What does moving pieces towards or away from something mean? (e.g., Pushing pieces towards the nearest Ocean with "Sweep Into the Sea.") What if you move a piece Inland?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Moving-towards-or-away))

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og38"></a>**What's the timing of Strife removal? When Invaders have reduced Health per Strife, do they get it back before damage is dealt?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og38))

- [ ] <a id="q-pieces-attached-to-pieces-not-participating-in-ravage"></a>**When an Invader with Strife is not participating in Ravage, is the Strife also ignored for modifiers to Ravage Actions?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/pieces-attached-to-pieces-not-participating-in-ravage))

- [ ] <a id="q-blight-card-flip-order"></a>**When Blight is Added, what order do you perform all of the effects of Adding Blight? If you were in the middle of a Ravage, do you finish the Ravage first?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/blight-card-flip-order))

- [ ] <a id="q-is-destroy-damage-against-durable-invaders"></a>**Are “Destroy” Powers “Damage dealing” Powers when used against Durable Invaders (e.g., for Flame's Fury)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/is-destroy-damage-against-durable-invaders))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ghm5"></a>**Do Damage bonuses apply to actions that conditionally do Damage even if those conditions aren't met?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ghm5))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Promo, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth · Badlands, Territorial Aggression (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot), Might Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth), Heart of the Wildfire, Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No, it's impossible to boost an effect that isn't happening, whether because a player chose a different option (e.g., choosing to add a Badlands with Sear Anger Into the Wild Lands), the action is happening in a land that doesn't meet the conditions for the effect (e.g., Sear Anger Into the Wild Lands in a land …
- [ ] <a id="q-are-bonuses-capped-when-applied-to-effects-that-are-capped"></a>**If a Power has a cap on the amount of Damage it can do, can Damage bonuses allow it to exceed that cap?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/are-bonuses-capped-when-applied-to-effects-that-are-capped))
  <br><sub>Promo, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Badlands, Blood Draws Predators, Swallowed by the Wilderness (Lure of the Deep Wilderness), Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Gift of Furious Might (Devouring Teeth Lurk Underfoot)</sub>
  > Yes. The cap is on the "natural" amount of Damage the Power can do before any bonuses are applied. Similarly, if there is a cap on any other effect (e.g., tokens added by Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone), apply any bonuses after checking the cap.
- [ ] <a id="q-invaders-added-isolate"></a>**Can Invaders be Added to an Isolated land with no Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/invaders-added-isolate))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Isolate</sub>
  > Generally, yes. Isolate only prevents Invaders from being Added due to Explore Actions (mandatory) and due to effects that check what Invaders are adjacent to (optional).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gecr"></a>**Can Isolate apply to Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gecr))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Isolate</sub>
  > Not unless you're checking adjacency between Dahan and an Invader piece. Thematically, the Dahan both know the island and how to get around on it much better (so many things which will block or stall Invaders aren't as big an obstacle), and have good relations with enough Spirits that they can get some help in …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqh"></a>**Can triggered actions be triggered by the action that set them up?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqh))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Triggered Actions, Forsake Society to Chase After Dreams (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > Yes, you check "did this action trigger anything?" after the action is complete. Lure of the Deep Wilderness' first innate does this, for instance.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebr"></a>**Do "destroy N fewer Dahan" effects protect Dahan against all forms of destruction (including by damage)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebr))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Destroy, Share Secrets of Survival (Shifting Memory of Ages), Birds Cry Warning, Offer Passage Between Worlds (Finder of Paths Unseen)</sub>
  > Yes.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfq9"></a>**Does "you can't destroy the same Invader twice in one action" also apply to Presence destruction?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfq9))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Destroy, Saved From Destruction, Blight, Presence, Explosive Eruption (Volcano Looming High), All Things Weaken</sub>
  > Yes — a single action can't destroy the same Presence more than once. This can happen with "Flowing and Silent Forms Dart" by in concert with anything that adds Blight one after another in multiple lands, like cascades or Volcano's "Explosive Eruption".
- [ ] <a id="q-counting-up-pieces-that-count-as-two-things"></a>**Does a piece that counts as two token types (e.g., both a Badlands and a Beasts via "Settle Into Hunting-Grounds") have a double impact on effects scale with both token types (e.g., "Beset by Many Troubles")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/counting-up-pieces-that-count-as-two-things))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Counts As, Beset by Many Troubles, Beasts Prey on the Injured (Beasts Event), Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Swallowed by the Wilderness (Lure of the Deep Wilderness), Never Heard From Again (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > No. Each piece either counts for the effect or doesn't count for the effect; there's no way for a piece to count multiple times. It's akin to counting up the number of rocks that are white or red – a rock that's both white and red would only be counted once.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gecq"></a>**Does Isolate apply if you're checking adjacency between an Invader piece and something else? (Another sort of piece, or a land type.)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gecq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Isolate, Cultural Assimilation</sub>
  > Yes. So, e.g., the Cultural Assimilation event tells you to replace a Dahan that's adjacent to a City. If either the land with the City or the land with the Dahan were Isolated, the players could nullify the adjacency. If every possible City - lone Dahan pair were separated this way, the players could thwart the …
- [ ] <a id="q-isolate-and-cascading-blight"></a>**Does Isolate prevent Blight cascades?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/isolate-and-cascading-blight))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Isolate, Entrap the Forces of Corruption</sub>
  > No, Blight is not an Invader. ("Entrap the Forces of Corruption" does stop Blight from cascading, but only because it explicitly says so, not because it isolates the land.) Thematically, the conditions that stop Invaders from easily moving around the island, don't stop ecosystem damage from spreading.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gekm"></a>**If a Badlands moves to a different land mid-action, can it boost Damage in both the original land and its new land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gekm))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Beasts Prey on the Injured (Beasts Event), Vengeance as a Burning Plague, Wreak Vengeance for the Land's Corruption (Vengeance as a Burning Plague)</sub>
  > No — just once/action.
- [ ] <a id="q-adding-badlands-and-dealing-damage-in-one-action"></a>**If a Power both adds Badlands and deals Damage, does the Damage benefit from the new Badlands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-badlands-and-dealing-damage-in-one-action))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Damage, Ordering, Desiccating Winds, Dire Metamorphosis, Unquenchable Flames</sub>
  > It depends on whether the Damage is dealt before or after adding Badlands. If the Badlands Token arrives first, it increases the subsequent Damage; if the Damage happens first, the Badlands Token arrives too late to matter. Some examples: - Badlands arrive too late: "Desiccating Winds," "Dire Metamorphosis," …
- [ ] <a id="q-damage-in-multiple-lands-when-one-has-badlands"></a>**If a Power deals Damage in multiple lands, can Damage in one of those lands be increased by Badlands in one of the other lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/damage-in-multiple-lands-when-one-has-badlands))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Explosive Eruption (Volcano Looming High), Volcano Looming High, Leave a Trail of Deathly Silence (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > No, Badlands only increase Damage in the land they are in.
- [ ] <a id="q-badlands-and-dealing-damage-multiple-times-with-one-action"></a>**If an Action deals Damage multiple times, can it benefit from Badlands each time?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/badlands-and-dealing-damage-multiple-times-with-one-action))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Damage, Ordering, Leave a Trail of Deathly Silence (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, Walls of Rock and Thorn</sub>
  > Only if one time is to Invaders and the other time is to Dahan – a single Badlands token can't increase Damage more than once against a given piece type in a single action. Most of the time, it doesn't matter which instance of Damage is increased by a given Badlands, even if a single action damages Invaders more than …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gi18"></a>**If an effect happens after a particular step of the Invader Phase (e.g., "after the Build Step") and there are multiple Invader cards in the corresponding space, does it happen after resolving each card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gi18))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Ordering, Invader deck, Triggered Actions, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony), Habsburg Mining Expedition, Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > No, resolve the triggered action(s) after the entire step is over, once you've finished resolving every card in that step. If the triggered action references the corresponding cards, it refers to all of those cards. So, for example, when up against Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Level 1+ and have both a …
- [ ] <a id="q-removing-an-incarna-from-the-game"></a>**If an Incarna is removed from the game or put back into the box (e.g., by Transform to a Murderous Darkness), can it be added back into the game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/removing-an-incarna-from-the-game))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Bargain, Incarna, Bargains of Power and Protection, Transform to a Murderous Darkness, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > No, it's no longer in the game. Permanently losing an Incarna this way generally isn't advisable.
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-a-piece-twice-with-one-instruction"></a>**If an instruction moves pieces in multiple lands, (e.g., "Unbearable Gaze" pushing a piece in origin land and a piece in target land), can it move the same piece twice: once from the first land to second land, and then from the second land to its final destination?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-a-piece-twice-with-one-instruction))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Push, Move, Ordering, Unbearable Gaze (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun, Struggles of Growth</sub>
  > No. "Unbearable Gaze" pushes once and moves two different pieces; it doesn't push twice. A single push/gather/move instruction will usually not let you move a piece more than once. You are free to move a piece multiple times in so long is it's with different instructions in one action (e.g., Thunderspeaker using …
- [ ] <a id="q-pieces-saved-from-destruction-do-not-count-as-destroyed"></a>**If an Invader is saved from destruction (e.g., by the Tsardom of Russia Level 2+), does it count towards effects that check which pieces were destroyed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/pieces-saved-from-destruction-do-not-count-as-destroyed))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Saved From Destruction, Tsardom of Russia, Never Heard From Again (Lure of the Deep Wilderness), Darkness Swallows the Unwary (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Portents of Disaster, Blood Draws Predators</sub>
  > No, since it wasn't actually destroyed.
- [ ] <a id="q-invaders-with-strife-not-participating-in-ravage"></a>**If an Invader with Strife is not participating in a Ravage, does it still remove 1 Strife in that Ravage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/invaders-with-strife-not-participating-in-ravage))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Strife, Do not participate in Ravage, Ravage, Exhale Confusion and Delirium (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium), Wandering Voice Keens Delirium</sub>
  > No. Because it's not participating in Ravage, it's not doing any Damage that Strife would prevent.
- [ ] <a id="q-ordinary-piece-and-counts-as-piece-in-the-same-land"></a>**If both a Beasts and something that counts as a Beasts (e.g., a sacred site of Many Minds Move as One) are in the same land and something destroys a Beasts there, can you choose which one is destroyed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/ordinary-piece-and-counts-as-piece-in-the-same-land))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Counts As, Incarna, Many Minds Move as One, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One), Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > Yes. Even if there are two ordinary Beasts tokens in a land, players get to choose which one gets destroyed – it just doesn't usually matter which one you pick. Similarly, if a Presence and an Incarna are both in a land and something would destroy Presence there, the Spirit can choose whether to destroy the Presence …
- [ ] <a id="q-not-participating-in-ravages-in-adjacent-lands"></a>**If pieces in a land are affected by a "do not participate in Ravage" effect, do they also sit out Ravage Actions in other lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/not-participating-in-ravages-in-adjacent-lands))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Do not participate in Ravage, Ravage, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony), Instruments of their Own Ruin, Network of Support and Trade, Tread Carefully</sub>
  > Yes, if a modifier to a Ravage checks for pieces in an adjacent land, it also checks if those pieces are participating in Ravage. As of the release of Nature Incarnate, this primarily matters in three cases: - When "Far-Flung Herds" (The Habsburg Monarchy Livestock Colony Level 6) checks for adjacent Towns in Ravage, …
- [ ] <a id="q-counts-as-for-victory-and-defeat"></a>**If your Incarna is the only Presence you have left on the island, do you need to continually count it as Presence to avoid losing?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/counts-as-for-victory-and-defeat))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Victory and Defeat, Counts As, Incarna, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Many Minds Move as One, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > No. You can choose to count it as Presence for victory and defeat checks without having to count it as Presence for everything else. Similarly, pieces that can count as Beasts don't need to continuously count as such against the Tsardom of Russia, just for the victory and defeat checks.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqf"></a>**Pieces that "do not participate in Ravage" are ignored for Ravages. Does this include checking for victory?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqf))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Do not participate in Ravage, Victory and Defeat, Unnerving Pall (Shroud of Silent Mist), Temporary Truce, Dahan Trade with the Invaders, Enthrall the Foreign Explorers (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > No, that wasn't intended. Pieces not participating in an Invader Action are still counted for Victory / Defeat.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefi"></a>**What exactly does "healing damage" refer to for Unquenchable Flames and Shroud of Silent Mist's "Slow and Silent Death"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefi))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Saved From Destruction, Damage, Time Passes, Birds Cry Warning, Share Secrets of Survival (Shifting Memory of Ages), Offer Passage Between Worlds (Finder of Paths Unseen)</sub>
  > Damage healing during Time Passes (and any future effects that use the word "heal"). It does not affect damage being cleared when an Invader or Dahan is saved from destruction, nor does it affect the expiration of temporary Health penalties when Time Passes.
- [ ] <a id="q-destroying-invaders-that-were-saved-from-destruction-and-then-replaced"></a>**When a piece is saved from destruction by an action and then replaced (e.g., by the Transforming Aspect of Heart of the Wildfire replacing a Town/City with Explorers), can the replaced piece(s) be affected by any Damage/Destroy instructions remaining in the original action?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/destroying-invaders-that-were-saved-from-destruction-and-then-replaced))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Destroy, Replace, Saved From Destruction, Transforming Aspect (Heart of the Wildfire), Tsardom of Russia, Thriving Communities</sub>
  > No, the original pieces were saved from destruction by that action, so they can't be chosen for further Damage or Destroy instructions even in their new form. The Transforming Aspect can't, e.g., deal 2 Damage to a Town with Heart of the Wildfire's "Firestorm" Innate Power, Push the resulting Explorers to another one …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfow"></a>**When an effect checks if the Ravage Step added Blight to a board (e.g., "Invested Aristocracy" or Level 6 of The Tsardom of Russia), which sources of Blight count?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfow))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Ravage, Invested Aristocracy, Tsardom of Russia, Disintegrating Ecosystem, Strong Earth Shatters Slowly</sub>
  > Count Blight added during the Ravage Step, no matter the reason Blight was added at that time. While this will usually be Blight added by a Ravage Card, the condition can also be met by flipping a Blight Card that adds Blight on each board, like "Disintegrating Ecosystem".
- [ ] <a id="q-accelerating-past-reminder-cards"></a>**When do you resolve a Reminder Card in the Invader Deck when accelerating multiple cards out of the deck (e.g., during the Despicable Theft scenario)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/accelerating-past-reminder-cards))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Reminder Cards, Accelerate, Ordering, Despicable Theft, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony)</sub>
  > If a Reminder Card is revealed in the middle of accelerating the deck, set it aside and then resolve it after the action that caused the acceleration (and any actions it triggered). For Despicable Theft in particular, all Thieves in one Coastal land escape as a single action, so if multiple numbered Thieves escape …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqu"></a>**When I can count one piece as another, can I change that mid-action? (eg: counting Many Minds Move as One's sacred sites as a Beasts?)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqu))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Counts As, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Many Minds Move as One, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > Not generally, no. Unless otherwise specified, the rule for "You may count Thing X as Thing Y" is that you choose per piece, per action, and your decision applies for that whole action. You can choose differently for nested actions - eg, if you flip the Blight card and it has immediate effects. (Example: You play …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2z"></a>**When you add Fear to the Fear pool mid-game, what exactly happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2z))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Fear, Invaders Find the Land to Their Liking</sub>
  > You add the specified number of Fear markers from the box to the top part of the Fear Pool. They behave as normal, so for the rest of the game, it takes more Fear to earn each Fear Card.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebw"></a>**When you return Presence to your tracks, can you cover one of the leftmost solid-circle spaces that start the game without Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebw))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Presence track, Bonus Spaces, Volcano Looming High, Fractured Days Split the Sky, Numinous Crisis, Hearth-Vigil</sub>
  > No, the leftmost spaces with solid circles cannot be covered by Presence. Similarly, "Bonus" spaces (which have a solid circles and start uncovered) can never be covered. However, if a normal space with a dashed circle starts the game uncovered (e.g., in the Guard the Isle's Heart Scenario), that space can be …
- [ ] <a id="q-making-different-choices-when-repeating-a-power"></a>**When Repeating a Power that has choices, do you have to make the same choice the second time?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/making-different-choices-when-repeating-a-power))
  <br><sub>Horizons of Spirit Island · Repeat, Targeting, Gift of Searing Heat (Rising Heat of Stone and Sand), Exaltation of Tangled Growth (Fathomless Mud of the Swap)</sub>
  > No, when Repeating a Power, you can make different choices unless the Power explicitly says otherwise. This can include: - Choosing a different target - Choosing a different mode (e.g., gaining Energy with the first use of Gift of Searing Heat and then spending it with the second use) - Choosing not to pay for …
- [ ] <a id="q-triggered-repeats-at-unusual-times"></a>**Can a triggered Repeat (like from "Relentless Punishment") repeat Powers resolved at an unusual time, such during the Invader Phase via the "Ethereal Conjunction" Event?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/triggered-repeats-at-unusual-times))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Repeat, Timing, Resolving Powers, Relentless Punishment (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Outpaced</sub>
  > Yes. It repeats the Power Card immediately, before moving on to the rest of the Event.
- [ ] <a id="q-changing-the-speed-of-actions-that-are-not-initial-power-uses"></a>**Can effects that change a Power’s Speed also change the Speed of other Actions taken in specific Phases? (e.g., dealing Damage with the Beasts token marked by "Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/changing-the-speed-of-actions-that-are-not-initial-power-uses))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Speed (Fast/Slow), Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, Terror Stalks the Land (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Swiftness of Lightning (Lightning's Swift Strike), Wind Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike)</sub>
  > No. Speed changes apply to the original use of a Power, not to delayed effects of a Power (as in this case) nor to non-Power Actions (like Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine abducting pieces at its Incarna).
- [ ] <a id="q-can-the-violence-aspect-use-bonus-plays-on-powers-that-might-not-deal-damage-or-destroy"></a>**Can the Violence Aspect use a bonus play for a Power Card that only might deal Damage to or Destroy something, like Veil the Night's Hunt?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-the-violence-aspect-use-bonus-plays-on-powers-that-might-not-deal-damage-or-destroy))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Checking for instructions, Violence Aspect (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), An Ominous Dawn, Wavering Resolve</sub>
  > Yes. The Violence Aspect just cares if the Power card has a "Damage" or "Destroy" instruction, whether or not Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares will resolve that instruction later in the turn. An Ominous Dawn, Wavering Resolve, and Gift of Wind-Sped Steps work similarly.
- [ ] <a id="q-conditions-changing-when-not-participating-in-ravage"></a>**If a piece only conditionally doesn't participate in Ravage, and that condition changes mid-Ravage, does the piece participate for the rest of the Ravage Action?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/conditions-changing-when-not-participating-in-ravage))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Do not participate in Ravage, Ravage, Wandering Voice Keens Delirium, Senseless Roaming (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium), Lair Aspect (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > No. If a piece isn't participating in Ravage, it sits out the whole Ravage Action. This most commonly arises for Wandering Voice Keens Delirium. If its Incarna is preventing Dahan from participating in Ravage in a land with Strife, that continues to be true even once Strife is removed to prevent Invader Damage there …
- [ ] <a id="q-leaving-an-incarna-in-place-with-an-add-move-action"></a>**If a Spirit has a Growth Action that adds or moves its Incarna, can it just leave its Incarna in place (whether on the island or in the supply)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/leaving-an-incarna-in-place-with-an-add-move-action))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Move, Counts As, Incarna, Wandering Voice Keens Delirium, Spread Tumult and Delusion (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > No, the Growth Action isn't optional: if the Incarna is in the supply, it must be added; if it's on the island already, it must be moved. That being said, you can count an Incarna on the island as Presence for the purpose of checking where it moves. So, e.g., Wandering Voice Keens Delirium can count its Incarna as …
- [ ] <a id="q-protecting-pieces-that-count-as-other-pieces"></a>**If an effect skips destruction effects against a particular piece type (e.g., "Heart-Tree Guards the Land" protecting Beasts), does it also protect pieces that count as that type (e.g., Presence that counts as Beasts due to "Settle Into Hunting-Grounds")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/protecting-pieces-that-count-as-other-pieces))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Counts As, Incarna, Heart-Tree Guards the Land (Towering Roots of the Jungle), Towering Roots of the Jungle, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, A Joining of Swarms and Flocks (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > Yes, but only from being destroyed as that type, not from being destroyed as a different piece type. E.g., Presence that counts as Beasts can still be destroyed at the Heart-Tree Incarna by effects that destroy Presence – they're only safe from effects that destroy Beasts.
- [ ] <a id="q-do-an-incarna-and-a-presence-together-make-a-sacred-site"></a>**If your Incarna and 1 of your Presence are in the same land, is that land a Sacred Site for you?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/do-an-incarna-and-a-presence-together-make-a-sacred-site))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Sacred Sites, Counts As, Incarna, Ember-Eyed Behemoth, Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine, Towering Roots of the Jungle</sub>
  > Yes, if you choose to count your Incarna as Presence for that action.
- [ ] <a id="q-removing-replacing-or-destroying-an-incarna-counting-only-as-itself"></a>**Is it possible to Remove, Replace, or Destroy an Incarna that is counting only at itself?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/removing-replacing-or-destroying-an-incarna-counting-only-as-itself))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Destroy, Remove, Replace, Incarna, Cast Down into the Briny Deep, Fragments of Yesteryear</sub>
  > Yes, with either Fragments of Yesteryear or thresholded Cast Down Into the Briny Deep. If you don’t have an easy way to get your Incarna back, using either of those Powers near your Incarna is not recommended.
- [ ] <a id="q-which-powers-have-damage-and-destroy-instructions"></a>**The Violence Aspect and An Ominous Dawn / Waving Resolve both check for Power Cards with specific instructions.
