  > Yes, the ones that use those words as part of a different instruction – or as part of no instruction at all! The Violence Aspect checks for Powers that actively do Damage or Destroy things, while An Ominous Dawn and Wavering Resolve check for Powers that add Fear, not just reference Fear as a concept. The Violence …
- [ ] <a id="q-incarna-that-cannot-be-returned-to-play"></a>**Unlike for other Incarna, there is no way to bring back the Incarna of the Lair and Warrior Aspects. What happens if those Incarna get Destroyed/Removed/Replaced?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/incarna-that-cannot-be-returned-to-play))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Remove, Destroy, Replace, Incarna, Lair Aspect (Lure of the Deep Wilderness), Warrior Aspect (Thunderspeaker)</sub>
  > They usually remain in the Incarna supply for the rest of the game, since there's normally no way to bring them back[†]. Letting this happen is generally not a good idea. Fortunately, it is effectively impossible to Destroy, Remove, or Replace an Incarna without deliberately trying to do so, since the owner can …
- [ ] <a id="q-vitality-in-lands-with-blight"></a>**What does Vitality do in lands with Blight?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/vitality-in-lands-with-blight))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Blight, Vitality, Towering Roots of the Jungle, Enduring Vitality (Towering Roots of the Jungle), Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Nourishing Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Nothing, aside from existing for effects that check how many Vitality tokens are in a land, like Heart-Tree Guards the Land. If the Blight is later moved or removed, the Vitality can be used for its normal function.
- [ ] <a id="q-adding-incarna-when-adding-presence"></a>**When a Spirit with an Incarna adds Presence, may it add its Incarna?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-incarna-when-adding-presence))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Adding Presence, Repositioning Presence, Counts As, Incarna, Wandering Voice Keens Delirium, Spread Tumult and Delusion (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium)</sub>
  > Yes, but only to reposition an Incarna already on the island. If the Incarna is not on the island, it doesn't count as Presence, so it can't be added by an action that adds Presence. The Incarna is considered added even if it's repositioned back into the land it came from. (It's not considered moved.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ghli"></a>**When Blight is added to a land with Vitality, does it destroy Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ghli))

Sufficient Vitality will also prevent extra effects of Blight being added, including those from Mining Rush (The Kingdom of Sweden Level 5), Irreparable Damage (The Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Additional Loss Condition), Runoff and …

## Scenarios

- [ ] <a id="q-can-you-mix-two-or-more-scenarios"></a>**Can you mix two or more Scenarios?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-you-mix-two-or-more-scenarios))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Difficulty, Varied Terrains, Elemental Invocation, Ward the Shores</sub>
  > You can, but it's not officially supported (they're not tested in pairs), so you'll need to use your own judgment when combining them - what will play well together, how Difficult it might be, and if there are any tweaks you need to make to avoid degenerate situations. Scenarios exist on sort of a spectrum between …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gec3"></a>**For Dahan Insurrection, what types of actions are Constant Raiding and Military Reprisal?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gec3))
  <br><sub>Core game · Dahan Insurrection</sub>
  > They're Scenario actions, triggered after anything that moves Dahan or destroys Towns/Cities (respectively). If an action triggers both, Constant Raiding must come before Military Reprisal.
- [ ] <a id="q-entwined-power-in-destiny-unfolds"></a>**How does Entwined Power work in Destiny Unfolds?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/entwined-power-in-destiny-unfolds))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Core game · Gaining Power Cards, Entwined Power, Destiny Unfolds</sub>
  > If Target Spirit still has cards in their Destiny, they gain one of them and you gain another one if any remain. (If there was only one card in their Destiny, you won't get a card.) If their Destiny was already empty before resolving "Entwined Power," resolve it like normal. You don't gain a card from your own …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedr"></a>**How does Isolate interact with the Military Response rule in the Dahan Insurrection Scenario?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedr))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth · Isolate, Dahan Insurrection</sub>
  > Oddly. The simplest way to play it is "Military Response ignores Isolate". If you want a more thematic but more complex game, use "The new Invader must be added to a non-Isolated land. Ignore Isolate effects when determining which lands are closest."
- [ ] <a id="q-Blitz-Slow-Phase"></a>**If an effect specifically happens in the Slow Phase, but isn't a Slow Power, how does it work in the Blitz Scenario?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Blitz-Slow-Phase))
  <br><sub>Core game · Speed (Fast/Slow), Blitz, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), The Great River, Rituals of Terror, Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone</sub>
  > Exactly as written. Blitz still has a Slow Phase, even if nothing usually happens in it because all Slow Powers are Fast.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kavh"></a>**In the Dahan Insurrection scenario, if Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares moves Dahan do they do real damage or shadow damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kavh))
  <br><sub>Core game, Nature Incarnate · Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, Dahan Insurrection, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Transforming Aspect (Heart of the Wildfire), Terror Stalks the Land (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > They deal real damage. It is the scenario that is causing the damage, not Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, so its special rule does not apply.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ofsh"></a>**In the Dahan Insurrection Scenario, what is the timing of "Coordinated Insurrection" (the uprising instead of a terror victory)? Can the Insurrection trigger actions – e.g., "Vengeance of the Dead"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ofsh))
  <br><sub>Core game · Triggered Actions, Victory and Defeat, Timing, Dahan Insurrection, Vengeance of the Dead</sub>
  > The Insurrection replaces the normal Terror Victory, so happens after the first action tree where you reach Terror Level 4. Yes, the Insurrection can trigger actions, which can can help fulfill victory conditions (or prevent them, in the case of "Military Response"). The new wording would be "When you would win a …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gbg4"></a>**In the Dahan Insurrection scenario, what's the timing and ordering of Constant Raiding and Military Response? If a Power both moves Dahan and deals damage *(eg, Vigor of the Breaking Dawn)*, which happens first?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gbg4))
  <br><sub>Core game · Triggered Actions, Dahan Insurrection</sub>
  > Constant Raiding (Dahan that moved deal damage) triggers after each action that moves Dahan. Military Response (add Towns/Explorers for Cities/Towns destroyed) triggers after each action that destroys Towns/Cities. If both trigger, Constant Raiding happens first; this means that newly added Invaders won't be damaged …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9q"></a>**In the scenario Guard the Isle's Heart, does the extra Presence added during Setup come from your player mat?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9q))
  <br><sub>Core game · Guard the Isle's Heart</sub>
  > Yes, it comes from the player mat, not from the board.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gd1f"></a>**What does "When exploring" mean? (e.g., the Blitz scenario) Can I add the extra Explorer anywhere I want?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gd1f))

- [ ] <a id="q-when-are-markers-considered-pieces"></a>**When are Scenario Markers / Element Markers considered pieces?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/when-are-markers-considered-pieces))

- [ ] <a id="q-blitz-timing-details"></a>**When does Blitz reduce the cost of a Fast Power and when does Blitz refund Energy for a Power being Fast?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/blitz-timing-details))

- [ ] <a id="q-adjusting-scenarios-for-extra-board"></a>**When playing with an extra board, do any Scenarios need adjusting?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adjusting-scenarios-for-extra-board))

(So you have enough tokens for all the boards, if playing on balanced. ) - Blitz - Setup - "an additional Blight per player" should be "per …
- [ ] <a id="q-blitz-setup-and-invader-steps"></a>**When the Invaders do an extra set of actions during Setup in the Blitz Scenario, does that include any Adversary effects that happen during or trigger after the Build/Explore Step?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/blitz-setup-and-invader-steps))

In particular, the following effects don't apply during Blitz setup because they reference specific steps of the Invader Phase or …
- [ ] <a id="q-whose-action-in-ritual-of-terror"></a>**When the players collectively perform the Ritual of Terror, whose Action is it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/whose-action-in-ritual-of-terror))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Core game, Branch & Claw · Solo games, Actions, Rituals of Terror, Vengeance as a Burning Plague, Lingering Pestilence (Vengeance as a Burning Plague), Rituals of the Destroying Flame</sub>

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od07"></a>**With the "Blitz" scenario, can you use the Energy gained from playing an already-Fast 0-cost Power Card to help pay for another Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od07))
  <br><sub>Core game · Energy, Playing Power Cards, Blitz</sub>
  > Yes. The intent is that already-Fast 0-cost cards effectively cost "negative 1 energy".
- [ ] <a id="q-Does-second-wave-follow-the-plus-one-blight-errata-for-subsequent-games"></a>**Between waves of the Second Wave, if returning the island to Healthy, the Scenario says to put 2 Blight per player onto the Healthy side. Should that be "2 Blight per player +1"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Does-second-wave-follow-the-plus-one-blight-errata-for-subsequent-games))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Blight, Second Wave</sub>
  > Yes - it's meant to re-create a normally-set-up Healthy Island card, so it follows the setup errata for Blight. To be clear, that's +1 Blight from the Blight that was in the First Wave - you do not add +1 Blight from the box for every Wave!
- [ ] <a id="q-in-powers-long-forgotten-benefit-2-does-shifting-Presence-count-as-moving-it"></a>**For benefit #2 in Powers Long Forgotten, does "shifting" Presence count as moving it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/in-powers-long-forgotten-benefit-2-does-shifting-Presence-count-as-moving-it))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Move, Repositioning Presence, Powers Long Forgotten</sub>
  > No. (Nor does it count as repositioning it.) You may physically put the Presence in a different land, but it doesn't trigger anything that cares about moving or adding pieces, because metaphysically it hasn't moved. Nor does it check restrictions on movement, for similar reasons. The idea is that Presence in one …
- [ ] <a id="q-surges-of-colonization-and-explorers-are-reluctant"></a>**How does Surges of Colonization interact with the Terror Level 2 effect of Explorers are Reluctant?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/surges-of-colonization-and-explorers-are-reluctant))

If you resolve Explorers are Reluctant on an …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfa5"></a>**If the Invaders get Powers Long Forgotten benefit #2, can they build a City in an empty Sands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfa5))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Powers Long Forgotten</sub>
  > Yes, if there are more Towns among all Sands than Cities. All the Invaders are treated as being in the same place for Build (and Explore), so they will often Build in empty Sands, and may well build a City there. Note that each Build is a separate action, so a City built in one Sands could cause a Town to be built in …
- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-playing-carried-over-cards-in-second-wave"></a>**In a Second Wave game, when can players play the Power Cards carried over from the prior wave?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-playing-carried-over-cards-in-second-wave))

  > Any Spirit Phase after the game has first entered Stage III. If the Stage jumps around (e.g., against the Tsardom of Russia), the it does not matter if the game is currently in Stage III, so long as it has been Stage III at some point earlier in the game. (Note though that the early Stage III Card for the Kingdom of …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka99"></a>**In Powers Long Forgotten, how long do the effects of a token discovered by the Spirits or Invaders last for?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka99))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Powers Long Forgotten</sub>
  > Unless otherwise specified, the effects last for the rest of the game.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka91"></a>**In Rituals of the Destroying Flame, if you Repeat a Power (and aren't targeting near a Flame Marker), do you need to pay 2 Energy again in order to Damage/Destroy Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka91))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Repeat, Rituals of the Destroying Flame</sub>
  > Yes. The original Power use and the Repeat are considered separately. This might mean you only need to pay the 2 Energy for one or the other, if one targets a land within 1 of a Flame Marker but the other doesn't.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2w"></a>**In the scenario *Ward the Shores*, if you play a card face-down to create a Warding Pattern, does it count as a Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2w))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Playing Power Cards, Ward the Shores</sub>
  > Yes. For instance, you can boost its range with Reaching Grasp, Repeat it with Powerstorm, or Discard it for the Event Outpaced. Note that it is 0-cost and requires Energy each time you Repeat it.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kaal"></a>**In the scenario Ward the Shores, do the Warding Patterns added by the special power come from the supply of 4 Warding Patterns per player?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kaal))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Ward the Shores</sub>
  > Yes. As a result, you won't have more than 4 Wards per player on the Island at one time. Remember that you can re-use destroyed Warding Patterns or move existing ones.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gd1k"></a>**In the scenario Ward the Shores, what's a 'Fear victory'? Can you win without placing warding patterns?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gd1k))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Ward the Shores</sub>
  > A Fear victory is any victory with the usual terror-level-based victory conditions. You cannot win the game without placing warding patterns.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gd7d"></a>**In the Second Wave scenario, do you use the same Blight card as in the first game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gd7d))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Second Wave</sub>
  > Yes. This is indicated by the line "return [the Blight card] to Healthy".

- [ ] <a id="q-incarna-adding-powers-in-second-wave"></a>**Is it possible to add an Incarna that was never in the game in the first place (e.g., if a Power Card that adds an Incarna is carried over in Second Wave)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/incarna-adding-powers-in-second-wave))

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kf3e"></a>**What are the "spirit tokens" in power 7 of Powers Long Forgotten?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kf3e))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gapm"></a>**What is prevented by the Invader benefit #8 of Powers Long Forgotten?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gapm))

  > The land cannot be selected as a target for Powers (Cards or Innate). There are no other restrictions. However, you cannot use a "Repeat this on an adjacent land" threshold to target the …
- [ ] <a id="q-elements-on-face-down-cards-for-choice-events"></a>**When a Power Card is played face-down for the Ward the Shores Scenario, which Elements does it have for the purpose of Choice Events that are aided by a particular Element?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/elements-on-face-down-cards-for-choice-events))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Elements, Choice Events, Rulings Under Examination, Ward the Shores</sub>
  > While in play, it contributes no Elements to Choice Events (and you can't Discard it to help). However, it can be Forgotten as if it had the Elements on the face-down side of the card.
- [ ] <a id="q-card-type-of-face-down-power-cards"></a>**When playing a Power Card face-down in the Ward the Shores scenario, is the card still a Major/Minor/Unique Power Card for the purpose of abilities that care (e.g., the Immense Aspect)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/card-type-of-face-down-power-cards))

  > No, a Power Card that's played face-down loses its prior type. To cover specific cases: - The Immense Aspect does not gain 2 Elements for playing a Major Power face-down. - The Intensify Aspect can't spend an Air Marker to use a face-down Minor Power during the Fast Phase. - During the Ethereal Conjunction Event, all …
- [ ] <a id="q-can-i-use-a-middle-of-the-turn-card-play-as-a-warding-pattern"></a>**When playing Ward the Shores, if you play a Power Card not during the Spirit Phase, can you play it face-down to add a warding pattern?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-i-use-a-middle-of-the-turn-card-play-as-a-warding-pattern))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Playing Power Cards, Ward the Shores, Spur on with Words of Fire, Gift of the Primordial Deeps (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, Slip the Flow of Time (Fractured Days Split the Sky)</sub>
  > Yes, subject to the "once per turn" limit.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kbzg"></a>**When setting up for the later rounds in Second Wave, should you place the initial Beasts and Disease on each board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kbzg))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Second Wave</sub>
  > No. The only Tokens at the start of the game should be one of each type per player from the previous wave.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gcrb"></a>**With Powers Long Forgotten, when do you check whether a land is searched? Can a land be searched in the middle of a Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gcrb))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Powers Long Forgotten</sub>
  > Yes, since the scenario says "whenever". For instance, if Call to Migrate gathers 3 Dahan to land A, then pushes the 3 Dahan to land B, both lands A and B are searched. The Invaders will similarly search in the middle of an action.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedt"></a>**With the Second Wave Scenario, how do you handle Still-Healthy Island cards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedt))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Blighted Island, Still Healthy Island, Second Wave</sub>
  > The short version is "rewind cards, and what you learned doesn't change" (you'll use the same cards next game). The long version: If you went past a Still-Healthy Island Card, after following the Blight instructions, put it – original Healthy side up – atop the stack of Blight Cards (so you encounter it first, just …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef3"></a>**Does The Great River scenario modify the initial Explore?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef3))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · The Great River</sub>
  > No, the initial Explore happens as normal.
- [ ] <a id="q-isolate-and-the-great-river"></a>**How does Isolate interact with The Great River scenario?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/isolate-and-the-great-river))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Isolate, The Great River</sub>
  > Ignore Isolate when determining which land are more or less Inland that each other – where a Town "wants" to go (usually to a set of more-Inland lands or off the eastern edge) is unaffected by Isolate. Isolate does still (optionally) block Towns' ability to move into those lands or off the island. The Towns divide …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfog"></a>**If playing Despicable Theft vs. Russia level 2+, do the Thieves get the benefits that Russian Explorers do?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfog))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Despicable Theft, Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > No. "They are not Explorers for any other rule or purpose", including Adversary rules. They're basically a new type of piece, and Spirit Actions which can interact with Explorers are retconned to be able to affect Thieves too.
- [ ] <a id="q-Drowning-thieves"></a>**If playing Ocean's Hungry Grasp in the Despicable Theft Scenario, can you Drown Thieves if 2 Dahan are pushed into the Ocean in the same action? What if a Town is also Pushed in?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Drowning-thieves))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Destroy, Despicable Theft, Drowning (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > Yes. The Thief would return to its supply as usual, though, and Ocean's Hungry Grasp wouldn't get Energy for it. It doesn't matter if Towns/Cities are pushed into the Ocean as well, because you can check the "no Towns/Cities" condition before or after the action.
- [ ] <a id="q-using-element-markers-to-meet-thresholds-in-elemental-invocation"></a>**In Elemental Invocation, can a Spirit use Element Markers to legally target an Innate Power that would do nothing (and thus be untargetable) without those Elements?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/using-element-markers-to-meet-thresholds-in-elemental-invocation))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Elements, Elemental Invocation</sub>
  > Yes, you can use those Elements to meet the requirement that a Power have the potential to do something. (But as usual, Range or Speed changes don't count as “potential to do something”.) This was not clear in earlier printings, but is clarified on the Scenario panel in later printings.
- [ ] <a id="q-accelerate-elemental-invocation"></a>**The definition of Accelerate in the Jagged Earth rulebook says that the affected Invader Card is discarded, but the reminder text on Elemental Invocation says it gets removed from the game.   > The rulebook. Rules text trumps reminder text. This is occasionally relevant for effects that interact with the Invader Card discard.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geav"></a>**What happens if the pool of Thieves in Despicable Theft runs out?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geav))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Despicable Theft</sub>
  > There are no Thieves in the pool, so you don't add any more. (If your playgroup wants a larger challenge, you can create a secondary pool of (1x1, 1x2, 1x3, 7xblank) using the core game Scenario Markers.)
- [ ] <a id="q-losing-lands-in-elemental-invocation"></a>**When playing Elemental Invocation, what happens to Element Markers on the border of lands sunk by Deeps or Destroyed by Cast Down Into the Briny Deep?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/losing-lands-in-elemental-invocation))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Element markers, Elemental Invocation, Cast Down into the Briny Deep, Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > If the Element Marker is still on the border of a land, it interacts normally with Powers and other Element Markers. It still counts as being on a corner; it's just not necessarily at the intersection of multiple lands. If the Element Marker is completely contained within a land because all lands surrounding it have …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geds"></a>**With the Despicable Theft Scenario, can you use Isolate to stop or detour Thieves?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geds))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Isolate, Despicable Theft</sub>
  > Yes. If you use Isolate to make the shortest path to a board’s Ocean longer than usual without completely blocking a Thief in, it will attempt that longer journey, even if that puts it onto another board. (In that case, on future turns it’ll head towards that board’s Ocean.)
- [ ] <a id="q-destiny-unfolds-and-getting-power-cards-in-ways-other-than-gaining-from-a-deck"></a>**Does Destiny Unfolds have any effect on taking powers (e.g., via "Transformative Sacrifice") or gaining powers from a specific subset rather than from the deck (e.g., "Let's See What Happens")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/destiny-unfolds-and-getting-power-cards-in-ways-other-than-gaining-from-a-deck))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Gaining Power Cards, Taking Power Cards, Aid from Lesser Spirits, Let's See What Happens (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Days that Never Were (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Turmoil's Touch (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium)</sub>
  > No, those effects work normally, since they either aren't “gaining,” or are gaining from a place that is not specifically a power deck.
- [ ] <a id="q-transforming-aspect-in-dahan-insurrection"></a>**How does the Transforming Aspect interact with the Dahan Insurrection Scenario?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/transforming-aspect-in-dahan-insurrection))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Saved From Destruction, Dahan Insurrection, Transforming Aspect (Heart of the Wildfire)</sub>
  > The Transform Rather Than Consume Special Rule saves Towns/Cities from destruction and Replaces them instead, so it will not trigger Military Response. Constant Raiding is a triggered Scenario Action, so it doesn't interact with Transform Rather Than Consume at all. The Dahan will do Damage normally and potentially …
- [ ] <a id="q-gaining-powers-with-specific-traits-in-destiny-unfolds"></a>**In Destiny Unfolds, if you only have Major Powers in your Destiny, can you gain Minor Powers normally from the deck? (Or vice versa)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/gaining-powers-with-specific-traits-in-destiny-unfolds))

However, if the source of the Power Card forces you to gain a Minor Power (e.g., "Gift of Power") and you have none in your Destiny, gain one …
- [ ] <a id="q-explore-timing-in-surges-of-colonization"></a>**In the Surges of Colonization scenario, are the Explore Cards on surge turns resolved simultaneously or sequentially?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/explore-timing-in-surges-of-colonization))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Explore, Invader deck, Surges of Colonization</sub>
  > Sequentially, just like for other effects that resolve more than one Explore Card in a single turn. Explorers added in the first card will be sources of Explorers for the second card. Also, if a land matches multiple cards, it can get Explored multiple times.
- [ ] <a id="q-solo-destiny-unfolds"></a>**Is it possible to play Destiny Unfolds with only one player?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/solo-destiny-unfolds))

- [ ] <a id="q-boon-of-reimagining-in-destiny-unfolds"></a>**When "Boon of Reimagining" is used in the Destiny Unfolds scenario, what happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/boon-of-reimagining-in-destiny-unfolds))

And if they have no Minor Powers left in their Destiny, they draw six Minor Power Cards and keep two of them, like normal for "Boon of Reimagining." …
- [ ] <a id="q-days-that-never-were-in-destiny-unfolds"></a>**When Fractured Days Splits the Sky plays Destiny Unfolds and gains a card from their Destiny, do they also put a card in Days that Never Were?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/days-that-never-were-in-destiny-unfolds))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Gaining Power Cards, Days that Never Were (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Fractured Days Split the Sky, Destiny Unfolds</sub>
  > Yes, and they can usually put any card they could have picked from their Destiny into Days that Never Were; they don't have to pick a Power Card of the same type as the one they gained unless the gain effect had a restriction.
- [ ] <a id="q-mentor-aspect-playing-destiny-unfolds"></a>**When the Mentor Aspect plays in the Destiny Unfolds scenario, do they gain two cards from its Destiny when they gain a Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/mentor-aspect-playing-destiny-unfolds))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Gaining Power Cards, Forgetting, Mentor Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Destiny Unfolds</sub>
  > Yes, if there are at least two cards left. In the unlikely case that there's exactly one card left, they just gain that one. They are free to mix and match between Majors and Minors, though gaining two Majors at once has the benefit that they need to Forget a Power Card fewer times over the course of the game.
- [ ] <a id="q-ward-the-shores-with-dances-up-earthquakes-impending-face-down"></a>**With the Ward the Shores scenario, how does Dances Up Earthquakes' Impending ability interact with playing Power Cards face-down to make Warding Patterns?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/ward-the-shores-with-dances-up-earthquakes-impending-face-down))

  > Dances Up Earthquakes may Impend a Power Card face-down; this counts as its "once per turn" face-down play. (The next turn, it will come into play, but not count as its "once per turn" face-down play.) Once a card is Impended face-up, it cannot later be flipped face-down. A strict reading of the rules would likely …
- [ ] <a id="q-great-river-town-health-bonus-and-damage-migrate-off"></a>**In the Great River Scenario, if a Town with a Health bonus and 2 Damage taken moves off the island due to Relentless Migration Eastwards, is it Destroyed or does it remain on the Scenario Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/great-river-town-health-bonus-and-damage-migrate-off))
  <br><sub>Durable, The Great River, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony)</sub>
  > It remains on the Scenario Card. The Scenario checks if it successfully exited the island, not what state it would be in after the move if it ended up in a place where it didn’t have a Health bonus.

## General Play

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9d"></a>**Can I pick the target land for a Slow power during the Slow Power phase?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9d))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee7"></a>**Can you Forget one Power Card to pay for multiple Forgetting requirements? (e.g., a self-targeted "Entwined Power" in solo)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee7))

  > No. Each Major Power gained must be paid for by Forgetting one Power Card, and that card can't be something you're Forgetting for some other benefit (like a different Major Power, or 3 Energy with "Shape the Self Anew"). In an extreme case, when using the Transforming Aspect for Heart of the Wildfire, you have to …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gcs0"></a>**Do you win as soon as you earn the last Fear card and the Victory space is revealed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gcs0))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear, Victory and Defeat</sub>
  > Yes. The text on the last Fear card does not matter.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9j"></a>**Does unused energy carry over to the next turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9j))
  <br><sub>Core game · Energy</sub>
  > Yes, it does (p. 13). The only things cleared at the end of the turn are those listed in the "Time Passes" section (p. 10).
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka98"></a>**How can damage be allocated to Invaders? Do you need to destroy the Explorers before attacking Towns?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka98))

Remember that partial damage heals at the end of the turn. …

- [ ] <a id="q-actions-for-fear-and-event-cards-with-both-each-and-non-each-instructions"></a>**If an Event or Fear Card has multiple instructions, only *some* of which are "each", how many actions is that? The entire "how many actions is it?" determination is: - If it just does 1 thing, it's 1 action. - If it has only "each" …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geff"></a>**If one of Shroud of Silent Mists lands is targeted with Vengeance of the Dead and Invaders have their Health boosted by an Event, when damaged Invaders die at the end of the turn when the health boost wears off, do you get the benefit of Vengeance of the Dead?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geff))

(Health bonuses also wear off during Time Passes, so you need Shroud of Silent Mists to prevent the healing for the Invaders to …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gee1"></a>**If you skip using the text effects of a Power *(p. 17 core rules)*, are you still considered to have targeted / used that Power? (For, eg, Shroud of Silent Mist.)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gee1))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Core game · Targeting, Shroud of Silent Mist, Scream Disease Into the Wind, Blitz, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist)</sub>
  > No, if you target a Power you must actually attempt to perform its text effects. (It need not actually have any game effect.) The core rules would be better phrased as "you may forego using a Power".
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcmw"></a>**Is there a limit to how many cards you can have, or whether you can show them to people?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcmw))

  > No.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9f"></a>**Is there a stacking limit to how many Presence can be in a land? Can a large stack count as more than one Sacred Site?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9f))

- [ ] <a id="q-is-boosted-damage-from-the-same-source"></a>**When a Power cares about how many Invaders that Power destroyed (eg: Mists of Oblivion, or Shadows' innate Power), do Damage boosts (Badlands, Flame's Fury) count?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/is-boosted-damage-from-the-same-source))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo, Jagged Earth, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Badlands, Damage, Mists of Oblivion, Darkness Swallows the Unwary (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Shadows Flicker Like Flame, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Yes. Damage boosts increase the amount of damage done, but the damage is still being done by the original source.
- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-resolving-a-power-played-mid-turn"></a>**When an effect lets a Spirit play a Power Card outside of the Spirit Phase, does the Spirit immediately resolve that Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-resolving-a-power-played-mid-turn))
  <br><sub>Core game · Playing Power Cards, Resolving Powers, Timing, Spur on with Words of Fire, Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, Gift of the Primordial Deeps (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > No, except in the rare case where the effect explicitly says the Spirit also gets to resolve the Power in addition to playing it (e.g. the "Ethereal Conjunction" Event). The vast majority of the time, the new Power Card works like any other: resolvable in the Phase in its "Speed" box when not in the middle of …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ocb5"></a>**When an effect lets you play a card, does that count against your allowed number of card plays?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ocb5))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Promo, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth · Playing Power Cards, Spur on with Words of Fire, Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, Gift of the Primordial Deeps (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Call on Midnight's Dreams (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Gift of Flowing Power (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > No. The number of card plays on your Presence track only affects how many cards you can play as part of the "Play and Pay For Power Cards" action in the Spirit Phase. Cards played any other way (usually in the Fast Phase when instructed to do so by a Power) are in addition to those limits.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9e"></a>**When you gain a new Major Power, which Power Cards can you forget?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9e))
  <br><sub>Core game · Gaining Power Cards, Forgetting</sub>
  > You can Forget any power card: in your hand, in your discard, or in play. You can even Forget the Major Power you just gained (sidebar, p. 18).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ghyo"></a>**Are the Coastal lands on one board adjacent to the Ocean on adjacent boards? (It looks like Land 3 can almost touch the Ocean.)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ghyo))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geg4"></a>**Do powers have to be resolved in order, or can you choose to do the effects in the order that's most convenient for you?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geg4))

- [ ] <a id="q-moving-a-piece-multiple-times-with-an-each-X-effect"></a>**If an effect moves pieces "on each board," "in each land," or for "each Spirit," can the same piece be moved multiple times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-a-piece-multiple-times-with-an-each-X-effect))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ge9b"></a>**If you "move" a piece to where it was (Range 0), does it still trigger effects like Wildfire's Blazing Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ge9b))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8t8"></a>**If you damage an Invader/Dahan and then replace it with another Invader/Dahan, does the new piece enter the game damaged?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8t8))

- [ ] <a id="q-ordering-for-action-modifiers"></a>**Is there an ordering or priority for action modifications? The practical upshot of this …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geiz"></a>**What's the timing of ongoing effects expiring?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geiz))

- [ ] <a id="q-affecting-multiple-lands-with-on-each-board-actions"></a>**When an effect happens "on each board," can it be split among multiple lands on that board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/affecting-multiple-lands-with-on-each-board-actions))

To illustrate the difference, "On Each Board: Push 2 Dahan from a land with Blight to a land without Blight" ("Forsake the Barren Land") must Push both Dahan from the same land because they must both leave "a land with Blight." …
- [ ] <a id="q-counting-multiple-piece-types"></a>**When instructed to count multiple piece types separated by slashes (e.g., "the fewest Towns/Cities"), do players choose which piece type to count?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/counting-multiple-piece-types))
  <br><sub>All · Counts As, Swallowed by the Wilderness (Lure of the Deep Wilderness), Lure of the Deep Wilderness, Beset by Many Troubles, Rain of Blood, Surrounded by the Dahan (Hearth-Vigil)</sub>
  > No. Count all pieces that are at least one of the types on the list. So in the example, if there are 2 Towns and 1 City in the land, the total for that land is 3 Towns/Cities. In the unlikely case that a piece is on the list multiple ways, it only gets counted once.
- [ ] <a id="q-token-limits"></a>**Can a land have multiple copies of a given token (e.g., two Disease)? Can multiple Strife be attached to a single Invader?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/token-limits))

- [ ] <a id="q-adding-strife-to-lands-without-invaders"></a>**Can Strife be added to a land without Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-strife-to-lands-without-invaders))

  > No, Strife can only be added directly to an Invader. If no Invaders are present, any instruction to add Strife is skipped.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gdax"></a>**Does Damage to the land accumulate? If something does 1 damage to the land, and later on something else does another 1 damage, does it Blight?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gdax))
  <br><sub>Promo, Branch & Claw · Damage, Blight, Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > No, each instance of Damage to the land is evaluated separately; if it does at least 2 Damage, you add a Blight, and otherwise the Damage is ignored. (core rulebook, p. 9) This never happens in the base game, but some Events can cause this to happen.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-dahan-get-strife"></a>**If an Invader with Strife is replaced with a Dahan, does it keep the Strife?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-dahan-get-strife))

- [ ] <a id="q-Do-selectors-check-just-once-or-are-they-updated"></a>**If you're told do something for "Each land that..." / "Each board that..." / "Each Spirit which...", do you check which lands/boards/Spirits to affect at the beginning and then not change that list (like figuring out which lands Ravage)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Do-selectors-check-just-once-or-are-they-updated))

- [ ] <a id="q-min-1-health-limits-when-applying-multiple-modifiers"></a>**When a piece or the land has both a bonus and a malus to Health, can players benefit from the standard "min. 1" limit on the malus by applying all of the penalties before the bonuses?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/min-1-health-limits-when-applying-multiple-modifiers))

- [ ] <a id="q-can-strife-move-independently"></a>**Can Strife move independently of the Invader piece it is attached to?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-strife-move-independently))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebu"></a>**If a Spirit's Presence counts as Beasts (say, due to "Settle Into Hunting-Grounds"), and a "destroy Beasts" effect destroys their Presence, is it still considered destroyed Presence? - [ ] <a id="q-Invader-Stage-when-deck-empty"></a>**What is the Invader Stage when the Invader Deck is empty?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Invader-Stage-when-deck-empty))

- [ ] <a id="q-dividing-evenly"></a>**When dividing pieces evenly between multiple lands (e.g., when moving Towns in The Great River) or Energy evenly between Spirits, do you account for existing pieces in those lands / Energy of those Spirits?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dividing-evenly))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8q0"></a>**Which lands are affected by the Level 1 effect "Indentured Servants Earn Land" of the Kingdom of England?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8q0))

  > The lands you'd normally check for Build. (Those of the Terrain type(s) showing on the Invader Card under Build.) The only change to how Build works is that a land without Invaders might Build (if it meets the listed requirements), which normally is not the case.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gd79"></a>**Against France Level 6, can Fear cards destroy Explorers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gd79))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Remove, Destroy, Kingdom of France</sub>
  > Yes. The Persistent Explorers effect only says it prevents Explorers from being "removed", so Fear cards that "destroy" Explorers work as usual.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-you-play-against-france-without-events"></a>**Can you play against France *(level 2+)* without using the Event deck?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-you-play-against-france-without-events))

  > Yes. You just need to track the time to make the Slave Rebellion event come up at the appropriate time, on Turns 4 and 8 and, in unusual circumstances, Turn 12, either by using the Event deck only as a timer or by some other means. Since the Slave Rebellion is normally in addition to another Event, there are no …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od02"></a>**Does the Kingdom of France's "Slave Labor" effect, converting Explorers into Towns, trigger if they don't Build normally?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od02))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Build, Disease, Kingdom of France</sub>
  > No, it doesn't. The effect happens "After Invaders Build", so if they are prevented from building (by Disease, or by skipping actions for one reason or another), then it doesn't trigger. By the same token, if the Invaders get an extra Build for any reason, the effect would trigger then.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedc"></a>**How does Farmers Seek the Dahan For Aid affect Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Level 1?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedc))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Branch & Claw · Build Step, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony), Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, Immigration Slows, Kingdom of Scotland</sub>
  > It doesn't — it changes the next Ravage Card to a Build Card, but doesn't affect the Invader Steps. Likewise if the Build is entirely skipped by Immigration Slows, there is still a Build Step and Habsburg Lvl 1 still happens.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9k"></a>**On the "Slave Rebellion" Event card, what does it mean to "return this card to the Event Deck as per Setup"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9k))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Events, Slave Rebellion, Kingdom of Brandenburg-Prussia</sub>
  > If you draw this card when not playing against France at Level 2 or higher, you should not have this card in the deck--discard it and draw another. If you are playing against France, then there are instructions on the Kingdom of France Adversary card; follow those.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8r0"></a>**The Kingdom of France's "Frontier Explorers" adds +1 Explorer when Exploring into a land without a Town/City. What happens with the Promising Farmland Event? ("When Exploring, on each board, add 1 Town instead of 1 Explorer.")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8r0))

  > You add +1 Explorer as normal for France, regardless of whether a given land got a Town instead of an Explorer. You check for Towns/Cities to see whether Frontier Explorers triggers before the Explore adds new pieces.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-slave-rebellion-victory-be-subverted-by-following-event-card"></a>**When playing against France, if the Slave Rebellion event card causes a victory, can the next Event Card turn it into a sacrifice victory or otherwise undo it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-slave-rebellion-victory-be-subverted-by-following-event-card))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedu"></a>**When playing with against France, what happens to Towns that end up somewhere other than on the island (e.g., on the Great River panel)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedu))

- [ ] <a id="q-farmers-seek-the-dahan-for-aid-during-salt-deposits"></a>**When the Ravage Card is Salt Deposits, does "Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid" cause it to Build in Mining lands, Ravage in Mining lands, or Build in non-Mining lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/farmers-seek-the-dahan-for-aid-during-salt-deposits))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Action Modifiers, Ravage, Matches, Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > Build in Mining lands. Which lands the Salt Deposits card matches is determined by the space it’s in on the Invader Board, so it always matches Mining lands in the Ravage space. "Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid" then turns the Salt Deposits card into a Build Card. Since "Ceaseless Mining" only affects the Build Step, …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ofs4"></a>**With the Aid the Uprising part of the Slave Rebellion Event card against Kingdom of France, is damage calculated against each Invader with Strife separately?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ofs4))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Kingdom of France, Slave Rebellion</sub>
  > Yes, each individual Invader with Strife takes damage according to how many Dahan are present. Thus, for instance, two Dahan can destroy any number of Towns with Strife.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geee"></a>**Against The Tsardom of Russia Level 2: a damaged Explorer with a Health bonus ends the turn in a way where it won't heal (e.g., in Shroud of Silent Mists' lands).   > No. Effects wearing off in Time Passes isn't an action, so "A Sense for Impending Disaster" doesn't save the Explorer. (This is a provisional answer – if further cases like this suggest that "Effects expire" needs to be an action, it could conceivably change, but that seems the less likely case.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geep"></a>**Both Russia 1 and All Things Weaken can make Blight destroy Beasts. Which takes precedence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geep))

- [ ] <a id="q-extracting-the-salt-deposits-card-from-the-invader-deck"></a>**Can the Salt Deposits Invader Card be removed or discarded from the Invader Deck?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/extracting-the-salt-deposits-card-from-the-invader-deck))

In particular: - If you would Accelerate it out of the deck, instead discard the next Stage II card underneath it (or the top-most Stage III card …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geeo"></a>**Does a Build have to happen for Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Level 1 to Gather Towns?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geeo))

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfoe"></a>**Does making Explorers not participate in Ravage (e.g., with Lure of the Deep Wilderness) prevent "Competition Among Hunters" (The Tsardom of Russia Level 3) from causing Ravages?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfoe))

- [ ] <a id="q-how-many-actions-habsburg-escalation"></a>**How many Actions is the Habsburg Livestock Colony Escalation?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-many-actions-habsburg-escalation))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Actions, Stage II escalation, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony)</sub>
  > One Action per board with 4 or fewer Blight followed by one additional Action per board with 2 or fewer Blight, for a maximum of 2 Actions per board. If you are able to move or destroy the first added Town with a triggered Action, it is possible to choose that land again for the second added Town.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-much-damage-do-russian-explorers-do"></a>**How much damage do Russian Explorers do (level 1+)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-much-damage-do-russian-explorers-do))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gecp"></a>**What does "in board/land order" mean for Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Level 1?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gecp))

You go by …
- [ ] <a id="q-when-the-limits-of-irreprarable-damage-reset"></a>**When against The Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony), does "Irreparable Damage" (additional loss condition) check for 8+ Damage each Ravage Action, each Ravage Card, or each Ravage Step?   <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Timing, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony)</sub>
  > Each Ravage Action and the whole game, respectively. It doesn't matter how much Damage is dealt across a Ravage Card or an entire Ravage Step, just how much is dealt by one Ravage Action in one land. And the player-count-based Blight limit never resets – it tracks all Blight added that way across the entire game.
- [ ] <a id="q-fortune-seekers-during-a-salt-deposits-explore"></a>**When Fortune-Seekers modifies the Salt Deposits card against the Habsburg Mining Expedition, which lands match the Explore Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/fortune-seekers-during-a-salt-deposits-explore))

  > The ones without Towns/Cities. “Non-Mining lands” is a printed land type on the Salt Deposits card, even if it’s not a standard one, so it gets replaced by the condition from Fortune-Seekers.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gec0"></a>**When Towns are Durable (have extra Health) due to Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony) Level 4+: If Invaders blight a land during Ravage, does their Towns' extra Health wear off immediately?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gec0))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geah"></a>**Does the Kingdom of Scotland's Escalation trigger if there are no coastal Towns/Cities in any land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geah))

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geai"></a>**When Scotland Explores into Coastal Lands, what happens if there's an Isolated Coastal land? Do the other 2 Coastal lands on that board both have to add 1 Town, or can one get an Explorer?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geai))
  <br><sub>Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Explore, Isolate, Wilds, Skipping Actions, Kingdom of Scotland</sub>

- [ ] <a id="q-mining-tunnels-and-the-empire-ascendant"></a>**Against the Habsburg Mining Expedition at Level 6, can the Explore Actions from Mining Tunnels (Escalation) get extra Explorers from The Empire Ascendant (Level 6)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/mining-tunnels-and-the-empire-ascendant))

- [ ] <a id="q-disease-preventing-ravages-by-habsburg-mining-expedition"></a>**Against the Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 1+, does Disease only prevent Ravages in Mining lands during the Build Step?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/disease-preventing-ravages-by-habsburg-mining-expedition))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Disease, Build Step, Ravage, Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > No, Disease in Mining lands will prevent any Ravage, including those caused by Ravage/Build Cards (common), Events (uncommon), or Powers (rare).
- [ ] <a id="q-skipping-build-actions-in-mining-lands"></a>**Can effects that skip Build Actions prevent Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 1+ from Ravaging in a Mining land that matches the Build Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/skipping-build-actions-in-mining-lands))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Skipping Actions, Build Cards, Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > No. There is never a Build Action to skip — the Build Card directly creates Ravage Actions. Any effect that skips Ravage Actions will work though.

- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-avarice-rewarded"></a>**During a Ravage by the Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 1+, does the upgrade from Avarice Rewarded happen before or after the Dahan counterattack?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-avarice-rewarded))

- [ ] <a id="q-avarice-rewarded-with-no-ravaging-explorers-or-towns"></a>**If a Ravage caused by the Habsburg Mining Expedition (Level 1+) would add a Blight to a land with Blight and no Explorers/Towns, what happens if there are no pieces to Upgrade (e.g., because only Cities are participating in that Ravage)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/avarice-rewarded-with-no-ravaging-explorers-or-towns))

- [ ] <a id="q-ignoring-disease-in-mining-lands"></a>**If Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 1+ would Ravage in a land with Disease, then an Event ignores Disease during Builds, does the Disease still prevent that Ravage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/ignoring-disease-in-mining-lands))

  > No, the Ravage happens normally. Ceaseless Mining makes modifiers to Disease (in this case, making Disease do nothing) apply to Ravages in addition to Builds.
- [ ] <a id="q-mining-tunnels-and-the-salt-deposits-card"></a>**What does "Escalation ignores S" on Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 4 mean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/mining-tunnels-and-the-salt-deposits-card))

  > The "Mining Tunnels" Escalation checks terrains, and neither "Mining land" nor "non-Mining land" are terrains. Similarly, "Mining Tunnels" ignores the "Coastal Lands" Invader Card at Levels 0-3. The "Fortification" Event works similarly.
- [ ] <a id="q-difficulty-of-habsburg-mining-expedition-level-0"></a>**What is the Difficulty of Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 0 (just the loss condition and Escalation)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/difficulty-of-habsburg-mining-expedition-level-0))

## Aspects

- [ ] <a id="q-mentor-and-unlock-the-gates-of-deepest-power"></a>**When the Mentor Aspect is the target of Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, what happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/mentor-and-unlock-the-gates-of-deepest-power))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Nature Incarnate, Promo, Feather & Flame · Gaining Power Cards, Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, Mentor Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Gift of the Primordial Deeps (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Shifting Memory of Ages draws two Major Powers, gains them both, and forgets nothing. If they meet the threshold requirements, they can play one of the new Power Cards – since the identity of "the Major Power" is ambiguous, Shifting Memory of Ages chooses which one is being referred to. It doesn't matter who played …
- [ ] <a id="q-intensifying-a-bargain"></a>**Can the Intensify Aspect strengthen a Bargain by spending Moon to Remove more Presence from the island? How about from a Presence track?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensifying-a-bargain))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Bargain, Bargains of Power and Protection, Bargain of Coursing Paths, Transformative Sacrifice, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No and no. The efficacy of a Bargain is based on the number of times it has been made, not the number of Presence it has removed, and Intensify can't increase the number of Presence removed from a Presence track. However, Intensify can strengthen Transformative Sacrifice by removing a 4th Presence from the island (in …
- [ ] <a id="q-does-flipping-deeps-tokens-into-markers-change-the-pieces-in-a-land"></a>**Does Deeps flipping Deeps tokens into markers count as changing the pieces in a land for the purpose of Observe the Ever-Changing World?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-flipping-deeps-tokens-into-markers-change-the-pieces-in-a-land))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Pieces, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Shifting Memory of Ages, Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > Yes, since the Deeps tokens are no longer pieces in the land. However, this rarely matters. Reclaimed by the Deeps will almost certainly change the contents of the land anyway by doing at least one of: - Destroying the last Towns/Cities - Drowning all Explorers/Dahan - Pushing all but 2 Deeps - Merging into the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb8"></a>**For the third level of Exaltation of the Storm-Wind, what does "one of your respective lands" mean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb8))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Wind Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike)</sub>
  > It meant "the land of the Spirit doing the Pushing". Lightning would Push Explorers from one of its lands, then the target Spirit would Push Explorers from one of its own lands.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geau"></a>**If Shadows Flicker Like Flame, with the Reach aspect, uses "Reaching Grasp," can it let a Spirit ignore range for the entire turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geau))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Range, Range Boosts, Shadows Flicker Like Flame, Reach Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Finder of Paths Unseen</sub>
  > No. Range boosts do not modify other Range boosts, only actual Ranges that you yourself are checking. (They do affect Ranges in printed effects, per If I get a Range boost, what Ranges does it affect? .)
- [ ] <a id="q-reach-aspect-and-repeats"></a>**If Shadows with Reach ignores Range for a Power, then later Repeats that Power, do they get to ignore Range again?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/reach-aspect-and-repeats))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Range Boosts, Range, Reach Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > Yes. When used on Power, Reach is treated as a modification to the Power's Range. (And Range modifiers apply to Repeats.)
- [ ] <a id="q-immense-counting-unused-plays"></a>**When the Immense Aspect would grant an extra Element at the end of the Spirit Phase, do its Plays count as 1 Play or half a Play?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/immense-counting-unused-plays))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Elements, Immense Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike)</sub>
  > 1 Play. Immense has just as many Plays as Lightning's Swift Strike normally does; it just has to spend twice as many of them to play a card at the end of the Spirit Phase. The extra Element lets Immense still get a benefit from advancing to having an odd number of plays, despite the 3rd and 5th plays not actually …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqq"></a>**With the aspect Wind for Lightning's Swift Strike, can you choose later which Power to make fast?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqq))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Speed (Fast/Slow), Wind Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike)</sub>
  > Yes, you can choose at any point in the turn (usually during the Fast phase).
- [ ] <a id="q-targeting-lands-sunk-by-deeps"></a>**Can a land sunk by Deeps be targeted with Powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/targeting-lands-sunk-by-deeps))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Targeting, Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Ocean In Play (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > Yes, but only insofar as it's part of the entire Ocean on that board, and that Ocean as a whole can be targeted (if Ocean's Hungry Grasp is still present on the board). It's impossible to only target the part of that Ocean that was formally a particular land in the same way that it's impossible to only target the …
- [ ] <a id="q-gathering-the-locus-incarna-while-targeting-a-power"></a>**Can Shroud of Silent Mist use "Mists Shift and Flow" to gather Presence from the Locus Aspect at the Locus Incarna? Can it use that Presence for targeting? How about if it gathers the Incarna itself?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/gathering-the-locus-incarna-while-targeting-a-power))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Repeat, Targeting, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist</sub>
  > Like with "Entwined Power", Shroud of Silent Mist can gather any Presence that it can use for targeting, even if that Presence is not its own. However, that Presence must still be at the Incarna in order to help satisfy targeting requirements. That means: - If Shroud gathers a non-Incarna Locus Presence away from the …
- [ ] <a id="q-lair-gathering-an-invader-multiple-times"></a>**Can the final threshold of the Lair Aspect move an Invader more than once if at least 12 Explorers/Dahan are present?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/lair-gathering-an-invader-multiple-times))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Timing, Lair Aspect (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > Yes. Since the Gathers are resolved sequentially, you can choose the same Invader each time to be the one being gathered.
- [ ] <a id="q-intensify-and-powers-that-add-multiple-token-types"></a>**Can the Intensify Aspect add multiple token types with Hazards Spread Across the Island?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensify-and-powers-that-add-multiple-token-types))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Spirit tokens, Action Modifiers, Hazards Spread Across the Island, Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone, Spill Bitterness into the Earth, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No. Hazards Spread Across the Island can only add more of the chosen token you are adding, even if other tokens could have been chosen instead. Similarly, if a Power allows adding multiple token types in combinations you choose (e.g., Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone), Intensify can only add extras of token types that …
- [ ] <a id="q-intensifying-beasts-with-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone"></a>**Can the Intensify Aspect create multiple marked Beasts with one use of Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensifying-beasts-with-unearth-a-beast-of-wrathful-stone))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Action Modifiers, Element markers, Unearth a Beast of Wrathful Stone, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No. While it can spend an Animal marker to add an additional Beasts token, the second token won't get marked.
- [ ] <a id="q-intensify-extra-uses-with-air-marker"></a>**Can the Intensify Aspect spend an Air marker to get an extra use of a Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensify-extra-uses-with-air-marker))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Element markers, Action Modifiers, Speed (Fast/Slow), Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No, the Air marker lets Intensify use a Slow Power in the Fast Phase instead of the Slow Phase.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-intensify-spend-markers-on-other-keywords"></a>**Can the Intensify Aspect spend any Element markers on Push, Gather, Bring, Upgrade, or Downgrade instructions?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-intensify-spend-markers-on-other-keywords))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Element markers, Upgrade, Downgrade, Push, Gather, Bring</sub>
  > Yes. Push, Gather, and Bring are all special forms of Move, so they can be boosted with Water markers. Upgrade and Downgrade are special forms of Replace (or Remove, in the case of downgrading Explorers), so they can be boosted with Moon markers.
- [ ] <a id="q-intensify-preparing-without-insights-into-the-worlds-nature"></a>**Can the Intensify Aspect still prepare Element markers without the Insights Into the World's Nature special rule?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensify-preparing-without-insights-into-the-worlds-nature))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Prepare, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Insights Into the World's Nature (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Yes. Prepare is defined in the game rules as of Nature Incarnate, so Shifting Memory of Ages no longer needs to define what "Prepare" means. (Prepare works the same as described in Insights Into the World's Nature, so it can be helpful to leave the first paragraph uncovered for easy reference.)
- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-explorer-and-dahan-check-in-the-final-threshold-of-the-lair-aspect"></a>**Do Explorers gathered by the Lair Aspect's final threshold enable that threshold to gather more Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-explorer-and-dahan-check-in-the-final-threshold-of-the-lair-aspect))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Timing, Lair Aspect (Lure of the Deep Wilderness)</sub>
  > No, the amount of times the Lair Aspect gets to gather with that threshold is set at the start of the threshold. Nevertheless, the other two thresholds can increase this count, since they're resolved first.
- [ ] <a id="q-intensify-markers-and-repeats"></a>**Do the modifiers from Intensify carry over when Repeating a Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensify-markers-and-repeats))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Repeat, Action Modifiers, Element markers, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No, since they apply to actions (e.g., one use of a Power) rather than to Powers directly. However, since each new use of a Power is a new action, the marker limit resets for each subsequent use.
- [ ] <a id="q-deeps-damage-drowning-invaders"></a>**Does Damage from the Deeps Aspect drown Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/deeps-damage-drowning-invaders))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Damage, Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Drowning (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > No, only drown Invaders if the instructions use the word "Drown," as in the second threshold.
- [ ] <a id="q-what-is-granted-by-the-bonus-space-on-the-intensify-aspect"></a>**Does the Bonus space on the Intensify Aspect grant extra Element Markers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/what-is-granted-by-the-bonus-space-on-the-intensify-aspect))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Bonus Spaces, Elements, Element markers, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > No, that space works like any other Elements on a Presence Track: Shifting Memory of Ages adds those Elements to its total for the purpose of hitting Elemental thresholds and paying for Choice Events. However, the Moon element gives the Intensify Aspect reliable access to at least one Element Marker each turn via …
- [ ] <a id="q-does-the-deeps-aspect-change-which-lands-are-coastal"></a>**Does the Deeps Aspect change which lands are Coastal by sinking lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-the-deeps-aspect-change-which-lands-are-coastal))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp), Kingdom of Scotland, Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > Yes. Lands are Coastal if they're adjacent to any Ocean, even one on another board. This can be rather inconvenient when the Coastal lands Invader Card shows up or against the Kingdom of Scotland, so be careful!
- [ ] <a id="q-encircle-benefitting-from-its-own-range-bonus"></a>**Does the middle threshold of the Encircle Aspect benefit from that Aspect's Range boost?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/encircle-benefitting-from-its-own-range-bonus))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Range Boosts, Encircle Aspect (Sharp Fangs Behind the Leaves)</sub>
  > No, since that threshold doesn't target anything.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-many-minor-powers-gained-by-nourishing-when-gaining-multiple-major-powers"></a>**How many Minor Powers does the Nourishing Aspect gain if Vital Strength of the Earth gains multiple Major Powers at once (e.g., from Entwined Power in solo)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-many-minor-powers-gained-by-nourishing-when-gaining-multiple-major-powers))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Triggered Actions, Gaining Power Cards, Nourishing Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Just one. As a triggered action, Flourish with Nature's Strength can only gain 1 Minor Power per action, no matter how many Major Powers are gained during that action.
- [ ] <a id="q-dark-fire-and-each-board-presence-destruction"></a>**How many Presence can the Dark Fire save from Blight Cards like Downward Spiral or Blighted Island Events like Voracious Consumption?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dark-fire-and-each-board-presence-destruction))

If, on the other hand, Presence is Destroyed “on each board” (e.g., "Blight Corrodes the Spirit"), then "Frightful Shadows Elude Destruction" can save 1 Presence per board. A single Presence can …
- [ ] <a id="q-intensify-aspect-affecting-multiple-lands"></a>**If an action affects multiple lands, can the Intensify Aspect spend prepared Element markers to affect multiple lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensify-aspect-affecting-multiple-lands))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Action Modifiers, Bloodwrack Plague, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Yes, but it must spend separately for each land, and is still limited to one marker of each type for the whole action. It's thus uncommon to usefully increase the effect in multiple lands, but, e.g., Bloodwrack Plague could be boosted with an Animal marker to add an additional Disease in target land, an Earth marker …
- [ ] <a id="q-affecting-extra-pieces-with-effects-that-scale-with-health"></a>**If an Action bases its effects around Invaders with a specific amount of Health (e.g., 3 Health worth of Invaders), what does it mean for that Action to affect +1 piece (e.g., for the Intensify Aspect)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/affecting-extra-pieces-with-effects-that-scale-with-health))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Health, Action Modifiers, Dream of the Untouched Land, Radiant and Hallowed Grove (Towering Roots of the Jungle), Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Inspire the Release of Stolen Lands</sub>
  > Also affect one additional piece that meets the original requirement. In the "3 Health worth of Invaders" example, this means affecting any one extra Invader with 3 Health or less. Health bonuses and penalties change which pieces can be affected, but Damage does not.
- [ ] <a id="q-intensifying-call-of-the-dahan-ways"></a>**If the Intensify Aspect spends a Moon marker on "Call of the Dahan Ways" with threshold, which pieces can be replaced?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensifying-call-of-the-dahan-ways))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Action Modifiers, Element markers, Call of the Dahan Ways, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > You can replace 2 Explorers by intensifying the base effect or 2 Towns by intensifying the threshold effect; you can't mix and match.
- [ ] <a id="q-moving-the-lair-incarna-to-a-coastal-land"></a>**If the Lair Aspect finds a way to move their Incarna without counting it as Presence, can you it move to a Coastal land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/moving-the-lair-incarna-to-a-coastal-land))

Any attempt to move it to a Coastal land fails – it and all Invaders, Dahan, Beasts, and Presence that would move with it instead stay where they are. (There wasn't space to fit this extra restriction on the …
- [ ] <a id="q-number-of-locus-repeats"></a>**If the Locus Aspect lets a Spirit Repeat a Power Card at its Incarna, what happens if that Spirit uses the Power Card again (e.g., via Powerstorm)? Can they Repeat the Power Card at the Incarna again?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/number-of-locus-repeats))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Repeat, Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Yes, but only if the Spirit has enough absorbed Presence. Each Repeat granted by Strength of the Waking Island counts against the total number of Repeats it grants that Spirit that turn.
- [ ] <a id="q-mentor-gifting-powers-with-unusual-elements"></a>**If the Mentor Aspect gives another Spirit Elemental Boon, which Element Markers can they pick from?   <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Element markers, Elemental Boon, Gift of Nature's Connection, Mentor Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > While not in play, Elemental Boon counts has having whichever Elements on it are most convenient, so Mentor can get 2 Element Markers of their choice when gifting that Minor Power. Gift of Nature's Connection, on the other hand, has no Elements printed on it, so Mentor gets only 1 Element Marker when gifting it.
- [ ] <a id="q-range-arrows-with-element-icons"></a>**On the Lair Aspect, what does the Range arrow with the Air icon on it mean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/range-arrows-with-element-icons))

  > Range equal to the amount of Air you have, measured from the Lair Incarna itself.
