- [ ] <a id="q-mentor-aspect-and-entwined-power"></a>**What happens when the Mentor Aspect uses Entwined Power on another Spirit? How about if another Spirit targets Mentor?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/mentor-aspect-and-entwined-power))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Gaining Power Cards, Entwined Power, Mentor Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > If Mentor targets another Spirit, then that Spirit gains a Power Card normally (drawing four and picking one) and Mentor gains two of the remaining three Power Cards. If another Spirit targets Mentor, then Mentor draws two Power Cards (4−2), gains two (1+1), and the other Spirit has no cards left to gain from.
- [ ] <a id="q-does-dark-fire-count-moon-as-fire-or-as-both-moon-and-fire"></a>**When Dark Fire counts Moon as Fire (or vice versa), is that in addition to still being Moon?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-dark-fire-count-moon-as-fire-or-as-both-moon-and-fire))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Elements, Dark Fire Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > No. A Moon that counts as Fire no longer counts as Moon for that action (but may count as Moon again for a future action). Dark Fire essentially tracks only seven Elements: Sun, Darkfire, Air, Water, Earth, Plant, and Animal. As far as it's concerned, a Power in play with both Moon and Fire on it has 2 Darkfire …
- [ ] <a id="q-dark-fire-gaining-moon-or-fire-for-a-single-action"></a>**When Dark Fire gains a Moon/Fire for only a single action (e.g., when playing Elemental Invocation), can it treat that Element as the other?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dark-fire-gaining-moon-or-fire-for-a-single-action))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Elements, Element markers, Elemental Invocation, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Elemental Teachings (Shifting Memory of Ages), Dark Fire Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > Yes. That is an Element available to you, even if it's only available for a limited purpose. This also applies in the rare case that the Dark Fire Aspect is gifted "Elemental Teachings" and spends a prepared Element marker to have an Element for a single action. But it doesn't let the Dark Fire Aspect use Moon …
- [ ] <a id="q-intensify-marker-timing"></a>**When does the Intensify Aspect spend Element Markers? Does it have to have Element Markers handy when it plays a Power during Spirit Phase?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/intensify-marker-timing))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Timing, Element markers, Triggered Actions, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > When it's actually performing the instruction it wants to boost. In the case of Powers, this generally means when using the Power in either Fast Phase or Slow Phase. However, if a Power creates Triggered Actions, the markers are spent when the Triggered Action actually happens, which might be at a later time. As an …
- [ ] <a id="q-leftover-deeps-when-a-land-is-sunk-become-markers"></a>**When happens to the Deeps tokens left over by Reclaimed by the Deeps? Can they be used to sink other lands if they can somehow get moved (e.g., by Weave Together the Fabric of Place)? If they are moved, does that un-sink the land they were in?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/leftover-deeps-when-a-land-is-sunk-become-markers))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Pieces, Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > The remaining Deeps tokens become reminder markers, which are just helpful reminders to players of which areas of the board are part of the Ocean. Reminder markers don't interact with anything on the island and don't do anything as far as the rules are concerned. In particular, they can't be moved and aren't Deeps …
- [ ] <a id="q-deeps-tokens-with-no-adjacent-lands"></a>**When Reclaimed by the Deeps sinks a land that has no adjacent lands (not even the Ocean), what happens to the Deeps tokens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/deeps-tokens-with-no-adjacent-lands))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Push, Deeps Aspect (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > Since there are no lands to Push the Deeps Tokens to, those tokens stay in place. Since they're all "remaining Deeps," they get flipped and become reminder markers.
- [ ] <a id="q-dark-fire-using-moon-fire-cards-in-choice-events"></a>**When the Dark Fire Aspect discards a card with both Moon and Fire to pay for a choice event, how much Energy does that count towards the cost? How about if it forgets the power card isntead?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dark-fire-using-moon-fire-cards-in-choice-events))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Choice Events, Elements, Sacred Sites under Threat, New Species Spread, Dahan Trade with the Invaders, Dark Fire Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > 2 Energy and 4 Energy, respectively. The discard and forget options for Choice Events check if a card has a relevant Element, not how many of that Element is on the card.
- [ ] <a id="q-dark-fire-aspect-checking-which-elements-it-has-on-cards-it-owns"></a>**When the Dark Fire Aspect is playing a Scenario that cares about the Elements of cards you own, can it count Fire as Moon and vice versa?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/dark-fire-aspect-checking-which-elements-it-has-on-cards-it-owns))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Elements, Rituals of the Destroying Flame, Elemental Invocation, Powers Long Forgotten, Dark Fire Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > Yes. Though the Aspect only explicitly refers to Elements in play and to choice Events, it's also intended to work with scenarios like Rituals of the Destroying Flame and Elemental Invocation.
- [ ] <a id="q-boosting-replacements"></a>**When the Intensify Aspect spends a Moon Marker when replacing a piece of type A with a piece of type B (e.g., replacing 1 Explorer with 1 Dahan with Call of the Dahan Ways), how many piece of type B will there be?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/boosting-replacements))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Replace, Upgrade, Downgrade, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Gift of the Untamed Wild (Lure of the Deep Wilderness), Crops Wither and Fade (Shadows Flicker Like Flame)</sub>
  > One per piece of type A that was replaced. So in this case, if there were two Explorers present, there would then be two Dahan.
- [ ] <a id="q-adding-extra-destroyed-presence"></a>**When the Intensify Aspect uses a Plant marker to increase the amount of destroyed Presence added with an action, can it only add one extra instead of two extra?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/adding-extra-destroyed-presence))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Presence, Element markers, Action Modifiers, Destroyed Presence, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > Only if there's exactly one extra destroyed Presence to add. If there are two extra destroyed Presence, both must be added, even if you'd rather leave one of them destroyed.
- [ ] <a id="q-can-nourishing-forget-the-new-minor-for-the-new-major"></a>**When the Nourishing Aspect gains an extra Minor Power because it gained a Major Power, can it Forget the new Minor Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/can-nourishing-forget-the-new-minor-for-the-new-major))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Forgetting, Triggered Actions, Gaining Power Cards, Timing, Nourishing Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > No. Completely resolve the action that gained the Major Power, including Forgetting, before gaining the Minor Power.
- [ ] <a id="q-warrior-aspect-incarna-damage-timing"></a>**When the Warrior Aspect deals Damage by Dahan bringing its Incarna, can it deal the Damage before the move?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/warrior-aspect-incarna-damage-timing))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Timing, Warrior Aspect (Thunderspeaker), Gather the Warriors (Thunderspeaker), Call to Migrate</sub>
  > No, the Damage must be in the destination land. The Incarna hasn't been brought until it arrives in a land. However, if one Power results in the Incarna being brought along multiple times (e.g., Gather the Warriors moving Dahan twice), the Incarna can deal Damage in any one of the lands it arrives in, even if that …

## Event Cards

- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odef"></a>**Are the separate parts of an Event Card separate actions for the purpose of victory or defeat?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odef))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Events, Effect</sub>
  > Yes. A single event card usually has three separate events: a main event, a token event (Disease, Beasts, etc), and a Dahan event. They are all separate, applied in order. _(See the Jagged Earth Action rules for more precision on this.)
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6of00"></a>**Can Events on an Event Card be resolved in the order of your choice? (e.g., to get the benefit from "Canny Defense" during the Ravage cause by "New Cash Crops Take Hold" earlier on the card)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6of00))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Events, Ordering, New Cash Crops Take Hold, Canny Defense, A Strange Madness Among the Beasts, Beasts Find New Homes</sub>
  > No, the Events on a card are executed in order from top to bottom, so the extra Ravage takes place before you get the benefit of "Canny Defense." Likewise for "A Strange Madness Among the Beasts," the Beasts deal damage after the first part of the card (in which some of them may be removed).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gd7j"></a>**Does damage to the land from War Touches the Island's Shores carry over to future ravages?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gd7j))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Ravage, Blight, War Touches the Island's Shores</sub>
  > No, partial damage to the land is ignored, just as for multiple ravages (B&C rulebook p. 15, base rulebook p. 9)
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kbjr"></a>**For the Dahan Event "Retreat", are you being told to (on each board) Push 2 Dahan from a *single* land with a City, or could you Push 1 Dahan from 2 different lands with Cities? What happens if you can't meet the full requirements?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kbjr))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Dahan, Events, Retreat (Dahan Event)</sub>
  > You're being told to Push 2 Dahan from a single land with a City to a single land without a City. If that's impossible, but you can Push 1 Dahan following those constraints, do so. Otherwise, do nothing on that board. "On each board" cares about the land the Dahan are currently in; it's fine to Push them onto a …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od09"></a>**For the Event card Outpaced on the second option, can Spirits help each other pay the Energy cost?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od09))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Outpaced</sub>
  > No, each Spirit has to independently pay the cost (in Energy or Presence) for each Slow power they want to keep. The Event card is worded differently from other Choice Events, which is why it is different.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcfs"></a>**For the event War Touches the Island's Shores, does Defend reduce the damage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcfs))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · War Touches the Island's Shores</sub>
  > Yes, if you allow the attacks, Defend reduces the damage to both the land and the Invaders.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gang"></a>**For the token effect "New Diseases", you do something on half the boards. Are you allowed to choose boards with no valid lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gang))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Half the boards, New Species Spread, Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, New Diseases</sub>
  > Yes, you can choose boards with no lands that have both Invaders and Dahan.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ganh"></a>**How does a Stage II Coastal Lands card interact with the "Fortification" event *(After advancing Invader Cards: Invaders Build in one terrain not shown under any Invader Action)*?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ganh))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Terrain, Strange Tales Attract Explorers, Fortification, New Cash Crops Take Hold</sub>
  > It doesn't – it is treated as though the card were blank, since "Coastal Lands" is not a terrain type. Similarly, "Fortification" doesn't interact with any unique Invader Cards that don't correspond to the four terrains: Jungle, Mountain, Sands, and Wetland.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odrc"></a>**If Invaders do extra damage in a land where all Invaders have Strife, what happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odrc))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Strife, Damage, Changed rulings, Destroy the Unnatural!, Fearful Mobs, Mesmerized Tranquility</sub>
  > Changed answer (as of Jagged Earth): The damage boost does not apply. (JE, p. 7) Per-land damage boosts only apply if the Invaders manage to muster up at least 1 Damage prior to Defend, so empty lands which manage to Ravage, lands where all Invaders are Strifed, or lands full of Explorers that are affected by …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ofsl"></a>**If Sky Stretches to Shore has been used on you, and you reveal the "Outpaced" Event, can you change a Power's speed at that point so it interacts differently with Outpaced?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ofsl))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Speed (Fast/Slow), Sky Stretches to Shore, Outpaced</sub>
  > Yes. Making a Slow Power Fast will NOT let you resolve it immediately - the Fast phase is done - but will let you keep it in play (perhaps for its elements) without paying Outpaced. Making a Fast Power Slow would let you discard it to gain Energy regardless of whether you used it during Fast, but you could not …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gd7c"></a>**In the Beasts Attack Event, how is the damage done and which Beasts get removed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gd7c))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Beasts, Events, Beasts Attack (Beasts Event)</sub>
  > Each Beasts does damage in turn, distributed as you prefer as usual for damage to Invaders, and only Beasts that finish off a Town or City get removed. For instance, in a land with two Towns and two Beasts, the first Beasts could deal 1 damage to each Town, and then the second one could finish off both Towns. Only …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qv"></a>**On the Event card Sacred Sites Under Threat, what are the choices we are choosing from?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qv))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Choice Events, Sacred Sites under Threat</sub>
  > You are choosing between the two bold-faced headings: 'Let the Island's Strength Repulse them' and 'Guard them Yourself, for Well or Ill'. If you choose 'Guard them Yourself, for Well or Ill', then there is a further choice for each Sacred Site with Invaders. On the further choice, in each relevant land (no matter …
- [ ] <a id="q-distant-exploration-and-isolated-islets"></a>**Playing with an Archipelago, Distant Exploration comes up (Invaders Explore at +1 distance). There's an empty islet with an Isolated Ocean. Can Invaders Explore its Coastal lands from facing islet's Coastal lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/distant-exploration-and-isolated-islets))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Archipelago, Explore, Range, Isolate, Distant Exploration</sub>
  > Only if you want to permit it. The "range 2" that Coastal lands are away from each other does need to measure through both board's Oceans, even though "2" isn't the additive number of steps to do so.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8nl"></a>**What are 'Hills' in the Coming of Age section of the Event card Strange Tales Attract Explorers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nl))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Terrain, Strange Tales Attract Explorers, Coming of Age</sub>
  > That should be 'Mountain'. That section should read "On Each Board: Add 1 Dahan to a Mountain or Sands with Dahan". This is leftover from a late terminology switch.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfr7"></a>**What are the restrictions on where Towns can go in Invaders Surge Inland?`** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfr7))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Invaders Surge Inland</sub>
  > You move the Town from a Coastal land once or twice, moving further inland (farther from the coast) each time. Sometimes, after you have moved a Town once, there is no way to move it a second time; this happens, for instance, on Board A if you move from land 3 to land 4. You cannot then move it to land 5, since land …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kavi"></a>**What does the Distant Exploration event card do?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kavi))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Distant Exploration</sub>
  > In the upcoming Explore, it lets the Invaders explore further from an existing base than normal. Precisely, for each Explore action this turn they Explore in a land (of the correct type) if there is a Town, City, or Ocean within Range 2.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pk"></a>**When an Event says, "On each board: Either destroy 2 presence (total) OR ...", what does "total" mean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pk))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Events, Total, On Each Board, Presence, Devastated Shores, Widening Destruction</sub>
  > That you only need to destroy 2 Presence on that board, not 2 per Spirit. So in a 4-player game, if you choose the "destroy Presence" option for every board, you'll destroy 8 Presence. If a given board doesn't have 2 Presence on it to destroy, you must choose the other option if possible.
- [ ] <a id="q-removing-disease-that-does-not-prevent-builds"></a>**When an Event stops Disease from preventing Builds, is that Disease still removed when Invaders Build?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/removing-disease-that-does-not-prevent-builds))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw, Nature Incarnate · Disease, Lingering Plagues, Irregular Outbreaks</sub>
  > No, Disease is only removed by a Build Action as part of skipping that action.
- [ ] <a id="q-do-disease-events-damage-dahan"></a>**When Disease events do damage to Dahan, is the damage reduced by Defend powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/do-disease-events-damage-dahan))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Damage, Dahan, Disease, Defend, Grim Toll, New Diseases</sub>
  > No, since it is not the Invaders doing damage.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qu"></a>**When Event cards ask you to discard the top major or minor power and do something on each board, do you discard just one card overall or one per board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qu))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Missionaries Arrive, New Species Spread, War Touches the Island's Shores, Years of Little Rain, Life's Balance Tilts, Lesser Spirits Imperiled</sub>
  > You discard one card for each board and follow the instructions on that board. This affects 'Missionaries Arrive', 'New Species Spread', 'War Touches the Island's Shores', and 'Years of Little Rain'.
- [ ] <a id="q-war-touches-the-islands-shores-in-a-rising-heat-sacred-site"></a>**When War Touches the Island’s Shores does Damage in a land where Rising Heat of Stone and Sand has exactly 2 Presence, can you take advantage of the Health penalty from Blistering Heat to Destroy Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/war-touches-the-islands-shores-in-a-rising-heat-sacred-site))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Nature Incarnate · Blight, Timing, Health, Durable, War Touches the Island's Shores, Blistering Heat (Rising Heat of Stone and Sand)</sub>
  > Yes. There is no point at which Rising Heat's Presence has already been destroyed by the Blight, but Invaders haven't yet been checked for taking enough Damage to destroy them. This also applies to Far-Off Wars Touch the Island against Durable Invaders.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odbi"></a>**When you resolve a Choice Event, do you have to resolve one of the two options?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odbi))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Choice Events</sub>
  > Yes.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gat6"></a>**When you return an Invader or Fear card to the box, can you look at it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gat6))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Rising Interest in the Island</sub>
  > No.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9i"></a>**Which elements does Elemental Boon have for the purpose of Events?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9i))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Discarding Powers as a randomizer, Choice Events, Elemental Boon</sub>
  > It is your choice (Branch & Claw rulebook, p. 16). This is true whether you are discarding the power as a randomizer, or discarding/forgetting it to get +2/+4 Energy for Choice Events.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6odej"></a>**With Promising Farmland, can you choose to add the Town in a land that wouldn't be explored anyway *(e.g., due to Wilds)*?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6odej))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Promising Farmland (Event)</sub>
  > No. You have to add a Town instead of an Explorer that would actually be placed. You can only avoid the Town if no Explorers would actually be placed on a board.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oeen"></a>**With the Dahan event Rouse the Spirits, when do you check if you meet the condition *(3+ Dahan in your lands)*? Can another Spirit help you meet the condition?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oeen))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Conditions, Rouse the Spirits (Dahan Event)</sub>
  > No, you check which Spirits meet the condition first, and then resolve the Powers. The general rule is that when the game tells you "For each [thing that meets conditions], do [some action]", you first note which things meet the conditions, and then do the action on each of those things (in an order of your …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedi"></a>**Can "Tell the Old Stories" restore Powers forgotten or replaced prior to the start of play? (e.g, Boon of Vigor with the Sunshine Aspect)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedi))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Forgetting, Tell the Old Stories, Sunshine Aspect (River Surges in Sunlight), Solidify Echoes of Majesty Past, Violence Aspect (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Locus Aspect (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > No.
- [ ] <a id="q-Ethereal-Conjunction-and-effects-that-modify-forgetting"></a>**Can Shifting Memory of Ages use Long Ages of Knowledge and Forgetfulness to avoid Forgetting the Power Card it uses via Ethereal Conjunction?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Ethereal-Conjunction-and-effects-that-modify-forgetting))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Forgetting, Shifting Memory of Ages, Long Ages of Knowledge and Forgetfulness (Shifting Memory of Ages), Ethereal Conjunction</sub>
  > No, even if the Power Card was in hand at the start of the Event, it's in play by the time Memory has to Forget it.
- [ ] <a id="q-bureaucrats-adjust-funding-in-lands-without-invaders"></a>**Can the "3 or fewer Towns/Cities" effect of "Bureaucrats Adjust Funding" cause a Build Action in a land without Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/bureaucrats-adjust-funding-in-lands-without-invaders))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Build Cards, Matches, Bureaucrats Adjust Funding, Pour Time Sideways (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Thriving Crops</sub>
  > Yes, and the resulting Build Action will usually Build a Town in that land. "Bureaucrats Adjust Funding" only checks if the land matches the Build Card, not if it contains Invaders. This is different from "Pour Time Sideways," which checks where Invader Cards are already creating Invader Actions.
- [ ] <a id="q-counting-participating-invaders"></a>**Do Invaders that are not participating in Ravage count towards making a land Ravage with "No Bravery Without Numbers" or getting a damage bonus from "Fearful Mobs"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/counting-participating-invaders))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Do not participate in Ravage, Courage of the Crowd, No Bravery Without Numbers, Fearful Mobs</sub>
  > No, only count the Invaders that could actually participate.
- [ ] <a id="q-numinous-crisis-energy"></a>**Do Spirits gain Energy from Numinous Crisis if that Event doesn't flip the Blight Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/numinous-crisis-energy))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Numinous Crisis</sub>
  > Yes, the Spirits gain 3 Energy for each Blight Numinous Crisis removes from the Blight Card, not just the extra Blight removed to force a Blight Card flip.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gegf"></a>**Exactly where does Fortune-Seekers cause exploration?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gegf))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Matches, Fortune-Seekers</sub>
  > The Explore card will be considered to match every land without Towns/Cities. It does not override the usual rules about sources of Explorers. So (if nothing else happens) it will explore every land that has no Towns or Cities and is either Coastal or adjacent to a Town or City. Note also that the subsequent Build …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfsd"></a>**For Bureaucrats Adjust Funding, when do you check how many Towns or Cities there are on the boards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfsd))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Bureaucrats Adjust Funding, Mapmakers Chart the Wild</sub>
  > At the time you draw the Event (not at the time when you Build), when you perform the "On Each Board" instruction.
- [ ] <a id="q-foreign-dignitaries-visit-in-lands-that-are-not-ravaging"></a>**For the "Foreign Dignitaries Visit" event, what happens if the lowest-numbered matching land on that board has no Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/foreign-dignitaries-visit-in-lands-that-are-not-ravaging))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Matches, Foreign Dignitaries Visit, Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > Then no Ravage on that board gets the Damage bonus. The event just checks which lands match the Invader Card (usually having the same terrain), not if that land will actually Ravage. Similarly, if an Invader Card causes a mix of Ravage Actions and non-Ravage Actions (e.g., Build Cards causing both Build Actions and …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedh"></a>**For the Event "Dissolution Threatens", whose Presence gets destroyed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedh))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Choice Events, Dissolution Threatens</sub>
  > The Presence of the Spirit making the choice.
- [ ] <a id="q-on-each-board-event-actions-with-bulleted-lists"></a>**How many actions is the "Act Cautiously in the Background" option of "Hard-Working Settlers," and what order do those actions resolve in?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/on-each-board-event-actions-with-bulleted-lists))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Actions, Waning Support, Hard-Working Settlers</sub>
  > It's three actions per board. Resolve all of the actions from the first bullet (Pushing Dahan) before moving onto the actions from the second bullet (adding Towns), before concluding with the actions in the third bullet (Ravaging). In particular, all Dahan will have moved before any Ravages happen. Similarly, "Waning …
- [ ] <a id="q-separate-ravage-damage-modifiers-for-the-land-and-the-Dahan"></a>**If the "Xenophobia" Event stops each individual Invader from dealing Damage to the land (but leaves them dealing Damage to Dahan), do Ravage Damage bonuses increase Damage dealt to the land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/separate-ravage-damage-modifiers-for-the-land-and-the-Dahan))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Ravage, Xenophobia, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony)</sub>
  > No. In order for per-land Damage bonuses to apply, at least one Invader must be dealing more than 0 damage. (See the Ravage Timing sidebar on page 7 of the Jagged Earth rulebook.) This is checked separately for "damage to the land" and "damage to Dahan". Damage to the land and to the Dahan are tallied separately. The …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geaw"></a>**If the Event Beasts Prey on the Injured happens in a game with Many Minds Move as One, do Many Minds' Sacred Sites count as a Badlands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geaw))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Counts As, Badlands, Beasts, Many Minds Move as One, Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, Beasts Prey on the Injured (Beasts Event)</sub>
  > Yes. The Sacred Site counts as a Beasts due to Many Mind's special rule, then counts as a Badlands due to Beasts Prey on the Injured. Note that this isn't relevant for Settle Into Hunting-Grounds, since the Presence of a Spirit affected by it already counts as Badlands - see this FAQ.
- [ ] <a id="q-foreign-dignitaries-visit-with-multiple-invader-cards"></a>**If there are multiple Ravage Cards, which lands get a Ravage Damage bonus from "Foreign Dignitaries Visit"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/foreign-dignitaries-visit-with-multiple-invader-cards))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Matches, Ravage, Foreign Dignitaries Visit, Tsardom of Russia, Habsburg Mining Expedition, Surges of Colonization</sub>
  > Each Invader Card grants the bonus to the lowest-numbered land it matches, only for the Ravage it creates in that land (if any). For example, if the first Ravage Card is Wetlands and the second Ravage Card is Coastal lands, then if land #2 is a Coastal Wetlands, it'll get the +2 Damage in the Ravage caused by the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefa"></a>**If you use Weave Together the Fabric of Place to join a land to the Ocean and the Event Relentless Optimism *(no Fear for destroying Towns/Cities)* comes out, can you get Fear when the effects wear off?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefa))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Time Passes, Timing, Relentless Optimism, Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Well-Prepared Explorers, Tight-Knit Communities</sub>
  > Yes. All the effects wear off at the same time during Time Passes, so if you put Towns in the Ocean when Weave Together expires, Relentless Optimism is no longer active. Similar comments apply for other reasons Invaders might die during Time Passes (Health bonuses wearing off, etc.)
- [ ] <a id="q-voracious-consumption-prevention-timing"></a>**When do Spirits decide whether or not to destroy Presence for the Voracious Consumption event?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/voracious-consumption-prevention-timing))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Timing, Voracious Consumption</sub>
  > When the effect triggers on that particular board after the Ravage Step. The payment is to prevent the addition of Blight, not to prevent the creation of the "after the Ravage Step,..." triggered action when the Event Card is initially resolved.
- [ ] <a id="q-choice-events-with-energy-loss-that-is-not-a-cost"></a>**Can a Spirit pick the top option for Visions Out of Time even if they don’t have 1 Energy or a Power Card that the Event can replace?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/choice-events-with-energy-loss-that-is-not-a-cost))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Choice Events, Visions Out of Time, Ethereal Conjunction</sub>
  > Yes. Those are both effects of the choice, not costs, so just do as much as you can.
- [ ] <a id="q-ethereal-conjunction-and-costs"></a>**Does a Spirit have to pay for the Power Card played via Ethereal Conjunction?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/ethereal-conjunction-and-costs))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Cost To Use, Resolving Powers, Ethereal Conjunction, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), The Past Returns Again (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Pour Time Sideways (Fractured Days Split the Sky)</sub>
  > No, since the card is put directly into play without needing to pay its cost. However, the Spirit still needs to pay any costs associated with using the Power, whether that's a mandatory "Cost to Use" (e.g., for Absolute Stasis) or a cost associated with a particular choice (e.g., for Hazards Spread Across the …
- [ ] <a id="q-exactly-hitting-the-limit-of-wavering-resolve"></a>**For the "Wavering Resolve" Event, if you have N Power Cards that add Fear, do you have to choose to Remove a set of Invaders that have exactly N Health between them?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/exactly-hitting-the-limit-of-wavering-resolve))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Health, Remove, Wavering Resolve</sub>
  > In most cases, yes. Keep picking more Invaders to remove until it's impossible to pick another without going over N Health. This will usually just be picking the ones you actually want off the island and then using miscellaneous Explorers to get all the way to N. In the rare case that it's impossible to exactly hit N …
- [ ] <a id="q-unusual-omen-situations-in-visions-out-of-time"></a>**In the "Visions Out of Time" Event, what happens if multiple Omens end up in one land? How about if an Omen is temporarily out of the game due to being under Absolute Stasis?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/unusual-omen-situations-in-visions-out-of-time))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Visions Out of Time, Absolute Stasis (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Weave Together the Fabric of Place, Bargain of Coursing Paths</sub>
  > On the turn after "Visions Out of Time" is drawn, in each land that's in the game and has Omens, gain 2 Fear, Ravage once, and Remove 1 Omen after the Ravage Step. Any leftover Omens (whether due to multiple Omens stacking up in one land or the Omens not being in a land at the relevant time) can be removed from the …
- [ ] <a id="q-unchosen-power-cards-in-visions-out-of-time"></a>**When replacing a Power Card with Visions Out of Time, what happens to the 3 unchosen Power Cards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/unchosen-power-cards-in-visions-out-of-time))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Visions Out of Time</sub>
  > They are discarded to that deck's discard pile.
- [ ] <a id="q-picking-an-impending-card-with-ethereal-conjunction"></a>**When selecting Power Cards for Ethereal Conjunction, can you choose a Power Card made impending by Dances Up Earthquakes?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/picking-an-impending-card-with-ethereal-conjunction))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Begin a Dance of Decades (Dances Up Earthquakes), Dances Up Earthquakes, Ethereal Conjunction</sub>
  > Yes. The list of places on Ethereal Conjunction that you can choose Power Cards from is a reminder of where your Power Cards will usually be, not the complete list. Impending Cards are still Dances' Power Cards, so they can be chosen for this Event.
- [ ] <a id="q-Ethereal-Conjunction-and-thresholds"></a>**When using a Power Card via Ethereal Conjunction, can you hit its thresholds? Do its own Elements contribute to those thresholds?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Ethereal-Conjunction-and-thresholds))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Elements, Ethereal Conjunction</sub>
  > Yes and yes. You can use any Elements you have when you use the Minor/Unique Power, including the Elements on the Power Card itself. However, the Power Card will be Forgotten before you have a chance to use any Powers in Slow Phase, so it won’t help you hit the Elemental thresholds of those Slow Powers.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geek"></a>**For an Event that does something "for each Beasts", is it one Action for the whole board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geek))
  <br><sub>Saved From Destruction, Actions, Prey on the Unwary (Beasts Event), Beasts Attack (Beasts Event), Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > No, it is one Action per land with Beasts (JE rulebook, "Each" exception sidebar).

## Invader Actions

- [ ] <a id="q-Stage-II-default-timing"></a>**Do Stage II Escalations happen before or after the Explore?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Stage-II-default-timing))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo 2, Jagged Earth · Stage II escalation, Kingdom of Brandenburg-Prussia, Kingdom of England, Kingdom of Scotland, Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > If it is not specified, it happens before the Explore. This means that, e.g., Brandenburg-Prussia can add a Town that then acts as a source for the Exploration that happens immediately afterwards.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kc4h"></a>**Does a damaged Invader or damage Dahan deal less damage than normal?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kc4h))
  <br><sub>Core game · Damage, Invaders, Health, Ravage</sub>
  > No. The damage a piece deals doesn't depend on how much damage it has taken - it's 1 per Explorer, 2 per Town, 3 per City, and 2 per Dahan, unless explicitly modified. (Rulebook, p. 9)
- [x] <a id="q-7w4g8nu"></a>**Does Defend guard both the land and Dahan? Eg, if the Invaders attack for 3 Damage in a land with Defend 2, does the Defend 2 block damage to both the land and the Dahan?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nu))
  <br><sub>Core game · Defend, Ravage</sub>
  > Yes. The Defend 2 will reduce the 3 damage to 1, which is then applied to both the land and the Dahan. (And is not enough to add Blight / destroy a Dahan.) A few Powers treat damage to Dahan and to the land differently: Concealing Shadows will only protect the Dahan, for instance.
- [ ] <a id="q-does-order-of-builds-affect-what-builds"></a>**Does the order of Builds matter to determine what Builds for Kingdom of England at Level 1+ or Kingdom of Scotland Level 3+?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-order-of-builds-affect-what-builds))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth, Promo 2 · Build, Ordering, Kingdom of England, Kingdom of Scotland</sub>
  > No. You look at the board position before the Build Card. (The England Adversary card explains this.) Thus Towns or Cities added do not count for further Builds coming from the same Build card. Similarly, in the much less likely case that a Town or City is destroyed, replaced, or removed while resolving a Build Card, …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8px"></a>**During Explore, do you still add an Explorer if there is already an Explorer, Town, or City in that land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8px))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explore, Explorer</sub>
  > Yes, if there is a source of invaders, you add one Explorer, whether or not there is anything there already. A Town or City in the land itself does count as a source of Invaders. (See Explore Example A on p. 11.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gec4"></a>**I'm confused - when do restrictions like "there must be Invaders" for Build and "there must be a source of Explorers" for Explore apply?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gec4))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Build, Explore, Ravage, Events, Matches, Fortification</sub>
  > Those apply when resolving Invader Cards – specifically, when making a list of what lands are affected by the Invader Card (JE rulebook p. 27) If an Event tells you to perform a single Build action – e.g., "On Each Board: Build in a land with Dahan" – don't check whether that land has Invaders. But if it tells you to …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8r8"></a>**If a Ravage is prevented (by, e.g., A Year of Perfect Stillness or Choke the Land with Green) do the Dahan still fight back?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8r8))
  <br><sub>Core game · Ravage, Dahan, Choke the Land with Green (A Spread of Rampant Green), A Year of Perfect Stillness (Vital Strength of the Earth), Paralyzing Fright</sub>
  > No. See 'Ravage' on p. 9 of the rulebook. There are many other ways the Ravage can be prevented entirely. Note that Defense by itself does not prevent the Ravage, just reduces the damage.
- [x] <a id="q-7w4g8qa"></a>**If a region is adjacent to or contains more than one source of Invaders, do you add more than one Explorer?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qa))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explore</sub>
  > No, you only add one Explorer if there is a source of Invaders, regardless of how many Cities, Towns, or Oceans are around or in it. (See p. 10 and Explore Examples C and D on p. 11)
- [ ] <a id="q-number-of-counterattacks-when-dahan-counterattack-early"></a>**If an effect changes when Dahan deal Damage in Ravage (e.g., Words of Warning), is that Damage in addition to or instead of the normal Damage after Invaders?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/number-of-counterattacks-when-dahan-counterattack-early))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Ravage, Timing, Action Modifiers, Engage on Their Own Terms, Words of Warning (Thunderspeaker), Warn of Impending Conflict (Hearth-Vigil)</sub>
  > Instead of. Each Dahan only deals Damage once.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gec1"></a>**Under “Ravage Timing” on p. 7 of the Jagged Earth rulebook, it mentions “modifiers to individual Invader Damage” happen first, and “per-land modifiers” only happen afterwards. Why is this relevant? What are some examples of individual vs. land modifiers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gec1))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Strife, Damage, Ravage, Mesmerized Tranquility, Cooperation Among Towns, Civic Engagement</sub>
  > It's relevant when every Invader in a land has been rendered harmless (via Strife, or reducing its individual Damage to 0) — drawing an Event Card like Destroy the Unnatural (which does +3 total Damage in certain lands) won't increase the Damage from 0 to 3. Individual modifiers are things like “Cities deal +3 …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g9as"></a>**What does the "Coastal" invader card do? Is it worse than the other Stage II cards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g9as))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explore, Flag Effect, Stage II escalation, Coastal Lands invader card</sub>
  > It means that you Explore (and later Build and Ravage) in all Coastal lands on each board, 3 lands per board on the standard size. If you are playing against an Adversary, the flag effect on the other Stage II cards is meant to make them approximately equal. Without an Adversary, this card is worse than the other …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8ps"></a>**What does the symbol on most of the Stage II Invader Cards mean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8ps))
  <br><sub>Core game · Stage II escalation</sub>
  > It is an Escalation symbol [p. 10 sidebar], with an effect depending on the Adversary. If you are playing without an Adversary, as in an introductory game, ignore it.
- [ ] <a id="q-pieces-added-or-moved-by-ravages-do-not-cause-more-ravages"></a>**When a Ravage changes which pieces are in other lands, can that cause additional Ravages from the current Ravage Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/pieces-added-or-moved-by-ravages-do-not-cause-more-ravages))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth · Ravage, Ordering, Do not participate in Ravage, Matches, Tsardom of Russia, Kingdom of Sweden</sub>
  > No. Determine which lands Ravage before resolving any of those Ravages. A land with no Invaders at this point won't Ravage even if Invaders are later added or moved there. However: - If a land already has Invaders participating in Ravage, the new Invaders also participate in that Ravage. Depending on how well the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qb"></a>**When an Invader card shows two terrain types, do they Explore (or Build, or Ravage) in every region of both terrains?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qb))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explore, Terrain</sub>
  > Yes, they will act in every terrain of the shown type, subject to the usual restrictions that the region be accessible for Explore or that invaders be present for Build or Ravage.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kaam"></a>**When doing an Invader action (Explore, Build, or Ravage), can you choose which lands they act in first?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kaam))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explore, Build, Ravage, Ordering</sub>
  > Yes. The card tells you which lands to act in, but then the players can choose which of those lands happen first. At Invader Stage III, you can do lands of the two shown terrains in any order; you don't need to fully resolve one terrain before resolving the other terrain. The order Explore Actions rarely matters, …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8ot"></a>**When Invaders Ravage, do they damage the land and Dahan simultaneously? Or one after the other?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8ot))
  <br><sub>Core game · Ravage, Earth's Vitality (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Simultaneously. If they do 3 damage, they do 3 damage to the Dahan and 3 damage to the land (adding a Blight), applied at the same time. This usually isn't relevant, but it can be if the Ravage is destroying a Presence that affects Dahan survival. (p. 9 in 2nd printing rulebook. 1st printing incorrectly omitted …
- [ ] <a id="q-pieces-arriving-in-the-middle-of-a-ravage-action"></a>**If a piece is added or moved to a land mid-Ravage (e.g., by a Blight Card that flips during the Ravage), does that piece participate in the Ravage?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/pieces-arriving-in-the-middle-of-a-ravage-action))

Dahan counterattack if added or moved to the land by a flipping Blight Card (or anything it triggers), and any Invaders added or moved to the land by that Blight Card (or anything it triggers) can be dealt Damage by the counterattack. (The Invaders arrive too late to deal any Damage – Blight …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcdu"></a>**If an effect *(e.g., "A Year of Perfect Stillness")* makes "Invaders skip all actions", what is affected? How is this different from "Skip all Invader actions"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcdu))
  <br><sub>All · Skipping Actions, A Year of Perfect Stillness (Vital Strength of the Earth), Paralyzing Fright, Indomitable Claim, Manifest Incarnation, New Cash Crops Take Hold</sub>
  > Both phrases mean the same thing: any time the Invaders would Ravage, Build or Explore in that land that turn, skip the Ravage/Build/Explore action in that land only. All such actions in that land are skipped, even if you'd rather let them happen. This includes any normal actions that come from the Invader cards, and …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4ghh6"></a>**Are the extra Builds from Bureaucrats Adjust Funding part of the normal Build for the purposes of Panicked by Wild Beasts?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4ghh6))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Normal Action, Bureaucrats Adjust Funding, Panicked by Wild Beasts, Pour Time Sideways (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Kingdom of England, Fortification</sub>
  > Yes, if the Build Card is in the Build Space, Panicked by Wild Beasts level 2 or 3 skips any additional Builds it causes via Bureaucrats Adjust Funding. Bureaucrats Adjust Funding modifies how the Build Card works, and thus any additional Build Actions it causes are normal if caused by a normal Build Card. (If the …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kc2j"></a>**Can the "next normal" Ravage or Build be on a future turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kc2j))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Ravage, Build, Events, Normal Action, Heavy Farming, Urban Development</sub>
  > Yes. If the Ravage or Build Space is empty, or if the Invader Card in that space has been changed into a different type of Invader Card (e.g., the next normal Ravage Card becoming a Build Card) leave the relevant Event Card in that space until the effect has a chance to apply.
- [x] <a id="q-7w4geew"></a>**If Invaders Ravage twice in the same round, how does Defend work? If each Ravage deals 1 damage to the land, do you add a Blight?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geew))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Ravage, Blight, Defend</sub>
  > Resolve the second Ravage just looking the current pieces on the board and ongoing effects; you don't need to remember if or how many times the Invaders previously Ravaged there that turn. In particular: - Defend applies each time the Invaders deal Damage in a land – it isn't "used up" in the first Ravage. - Each …
- [ ] <a id="q-What-is-a-normal-ravage"></a>**What counts as a single Ravage when "the next normal Ravage" is affected?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/What-is-a-normal-ravage))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Skipping Actions, Normal Action, Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, Heavy Farming, Urban Development, Rising Interest in the Island</sub>
  > A "normal" Ravage refers to a single Ravage Card in the Ravage Space printed on the board, resolving during the Ravage Step. Similarly the "normal" Build/Explore refers to a Build/Explore Card in the Build/Explore Space printed on the Invader Board, resolving during the Build/Explore Step. (See B&C p. 15 or JE p. 27) …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geeb"></a>**What does the 2nd choice in Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid modify: a Ravage Step, a Ravage Card, or Ravage Actions?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geeb))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Ravage, Build, Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, Kingdom of Scotland, Kingdom of England, Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > Read "the next normal Ravage becomes a Build" as "the next normal Ravage Card (even on a future turn) becomes a Build Card". The step is still the Ravage Step, so anything that specifically happens before, during, or after the Ravage Step will still happen at its normal time (but no Build Step effects will apply). …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kc2i"></a>**What happens if one effect modifies "the next normal Ravage" and another effect skips it?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kc2i))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Ravage, Skipping Actions, Events, Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, Urban Development, Rising Interest in the Island</sub>
  > If the next normal Ravage is skipped, then the effect that modifies the Ravage remains in effect until a Ravage actually occurs. This can happen, eg, if the event Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid (the next normal Ravage becomes a Build) occurs on a turn when there is no card in the Ravage slot, and on the next turn the …
- [ ] <a id="q-invavder-cards-that-do-not-advance"></a>**What happens when a specific Invader Card doesn't advance (e.g., due to "Immigration Slows")?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/invavder-cards-that-do-not-advance))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Branch & Claw · Invader deck, Immigration Slows, Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > When advancing Invader Cards, leave the card in the space it's already in, but advance all of the other Invader Cards. There will then usually be two (or more!) Invader Cards in that space for the following Invader Phase. On future turns, the card that didn't move will resolve before the newly-arrived card, and both …
- [ ] <a id="q-multiple-invader-cards-in-a-space"></a>**What happens when multiple Invader Cards are in the same space on the Invader Board? (e.g., due to Explorers are Reluctant)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/multiple-invader-cards-in-a-space))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Invader deck, Matches, Tsardom of Russia, Surges of Colonization, Explorers are Reluctant, Immigration Slows</sub>
  > In the corresponding step of the Invader Phase, resolve each card one at a time, starting with the oldest card – the one closest to the Invader Board. When Invader Cards advance at the end of the Invader Phase, the cards move together. [B&C p. 14, JE p. 11] Each card separately determines where it creates Invader …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gecz"></a>**When an Explore Card adds new sources of Invaders (e.g., adding a Towns via Kingdom of Scotland Level 1 or the Event "Promising Farmland") are the Invaders able to immediately Explore from that new source?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gecz))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Explore, Ordering, Kingdom of Scotland, Promising Farmland (Event), Explorers are Reluctant, Surges of Colonization</sub>
  > No. When resolving an Explore Card, first you note which lands have nearby sources, then you resolve all Explore Actions in those lands – in an order of your choice, if relevant. Nevertheless, if there is a second Explore Card (e.g., due to the "Explorers are Reluctant" Fear Card), the new sources matter for that …
- [ ] <a id="q-invader-stage-while-resolving-multiple-explore-cards"></a>**When resolving a 2nd (or 3rd!) Explore Card in a single Invader Phase, what's the Invader Stage while resolving the later card(s)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/invader-stage-while-resolving-multiple-explore-cards))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Invader deck, Ward the Shores, Explorers are Reluctant, Surges of Colonization</sub>
  > Whatever if would be if they were on top of the deck (usually the Phase printed on them). Most of the time, this distinction doesn't matter – almost all checks of the Invader Stage happen earlier in the Invader Phase, when resolving an Event Card. However, when resolving an Explore Card during the "Ward the Shores" …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gedo"></a>**If a land matches an Invader Card multiple ways – eg, if the Stage III Jungle + Wetland comes up and "Weave Together the Fabric of Place" has been used to merge a Jungle with a Wetland – does it perform that action multiple times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gedo))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Invader deck, Land Types, Matches, Weave Together the Fabric of Place</sub>
  > No. Invader Cards check "what lands match the land type(s) printed on me and are affected by the rules for the current action?", then perform that action once in each matching land.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geej"></a>**When a Stage II card shows up at an unusual time, when exactly do you resolve the Escalation effect?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geej))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Stage II escalation, Tsardom of Russia, Explorers are Reluctant</sub>
  > Unless otherwise specified, when the Invader card is resolved for the first time (JE rulebook, p. 27). For instance, Russia Level 5 will put Invader cards directly in the Build space. When that card is resolved for the first time (usually Building on the next Invader phase), it will also trigger the Escalation …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og24"></a>**When Invader Damage Dahan, and it is boosted by Badlands, does the boost need to be applied efficiently?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og24))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Badlands, Ravage, Dahan</sub>
  > Yes. In general, Badlands damage boosts follow the restrictions of the damage they are boosting.

## Definitions

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8ph"></a>**Are Invader components unlimited?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8ph))
  <br><sub>Core game · Invaders, Pieces</sub>
  > Yes. Invaders are not limited by the number of figures provided with the game. If you run out of figures, you can mark multiples by putting the Invaders on top of Energy tokens (Invaders, p. 15) You can use 1s instead of 3s if that's more convenient to your board situation.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfr0"></a>**Can you "move" pieces from a land to that same land (e.g., with "Wrap in Wings of Sunlight")? If so, is it considered a move?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfr0))
  <br><sub>Core game · Move, Push, Gather, Wrap in Wings of Sunlight, Bargain of Coursing Paths, Paths Tied by Nature (Finder of Paths Unseen)</sub>
  > Yes and yes, assuming the instructions don't restrict where you're allowed to move, the way that Push and Gather do. ("Wrap in Wings of Sunlight" doesn't.) Thematically, pieces moved to the land they're already in are moving around within that land.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kaai"></a>**If an effect tells you to, eg, "remove 2 Invaders" (Twisted Flowers Murmur Ultimatums), can you just remove 2 Cities?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kaai))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Core game · Invaders, Remove, Health, Isolation (Fear card), Twisted Flowers Murmur Ultimatums</sub>
  > Yes. "An Invader" may refer to an Explorer, Town, or City. (p. 30) Some Fear Cards remove Invaders based on Health rather than quantity. Those consider all modifiers to current Health (from, e.g., England Level 5, or an Event/Fear card) - but not Damage, as Damage does not reduce Health. (Health is simply how much …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od0k"></a>**There's an effect that applies when "(Dahan) outnumber (Town)/(City)". Can I use it in a land with no Towns or Cities?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od0k))
  <br><sub>Core game · Urban Development, Distant Exploration, Outpaced, Avoid the Dahan</sub>
  > Yes, you can. See the glossary entry for "outnumber".
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oc8z"></a>**Under a Spirit's Play Style, there's a Summary of Powers with bars in 5 categories. What do those categories mean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oc8z))
  <br><sub>Core game · Play Style</sub>
  > - Offense = Damaging/destroying Invaders - Control = Moving pieces on the board - Fear = Generating fear - Defense = Defend, Blight removal, and preventing Invader actions - Utility = Everything else "Fear" doesn't include Fear generated by destroying buildings. Powers affecting other Spirits count based on what they …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qw"></a>**What are all the terrains on the thematic map of the island?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qw))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo, Jagged Earth · Thematic map, Terrain</sub>
  > Here's the list. (As usual, J = Jungle, M = Mountain, S = Sands, W = Wetland.) - Northwest: 1S 2S 3W 4J 5M 6S 7W 8M 9M - Northeast: 1S 2J 3J 4W 5J 6M 7M 8S 9W - West: 1J 2S 3S 4S 5J 6M 7W 8J 9S 10M - East: 1J 2W 3W 4W 5S 6M 7M 8M 9W 10W - Southwest: 1S 2W 3S 4J 5J 6J 7M 8M 9S 10J - Southeast: 1J 2J 3W 4M 5J 6W 7S 8M …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kavt"></a>**What does the slash mean in effects like "Push up to 3 (Explorer)/(Town)"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kavt))
  <br><sub>Core game · Explorer, Town, City, Wash Away (River Surges in Sunlight), Drought, Harbingers of the Lightning (Lightning's Swift Strike)</sub>
  > It means "pieces of either or both types" - you are Pushing up to 3 pieces; those pieces may be Explorers and/or Towns. Similarly, "1 Damage to each [Town]/[City]" means "1 Damage to each piece that is either a Town or a City". (Or, in theory, both - though this would require some bizarre new piece considered both a …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pu"></a>**What's the difference between an Invader and an Explorer (the stick figure)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pu))
  <br><sub>Core game · Invaders, Explorer, Isolation (Fear card)</sub>
  > Explorer refers only to that type of piece, represented both on cards and on the board by a figure of a person. Invader is a generic term, referring to any of the types of pieces representing the settlers, i.e., an Explorer (stick figure), Town (two buildings), or City (three buildings). If a rule tells you to do …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oczl"></a>**When an effect reduces the Health of a piece (or it loses a bonus to Health), and it has already taken damage equal to its new health, is it destroyed immediately?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oczl))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Core game · Health, Damage, Farmers Seek the Dahan for Aid, Years of Little Rain, Panic, Dahan Threaten</sub>
  > Yes. (Branch & Claw rulebook p. 16) Invaders and Dahan have Health and take Damage. As soon as they have taken as much Damage as their Health, they are destroyed, whether that comes from taking more Damage or having their Health reduced. Unlike in some other games, doing Damage normally doesn't reduce the Health of …
- [ ] <a id="q-identifying-power-card-type"></a>**How can you determine the type of a Power Card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/identifying-power-card-type))
  <br><sub>All · Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages), Immense Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike), Wind Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike), Tell the Old Stories, Solidify Echoes of Majesty Past</sub>
  > The most obvious signs are on the back of the Power Card: - Major Powers have a "Major Power" banner across the top and feature the Major Power icon on a blue background. - Minor Powers have a "Minor Power" banner across the top and feature the Minor Power icon on a brown background. - Unique Powers feature art of …
- [ ] <a id="q-definition-of-rulings-under-examination"></a>**What does it mean for a ruling to "be under examination"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/definition-of-rulings-under-examination))
  <br><sub>All · Rulings Under Examination</sub>
  > The ruling is preliminary and might be changed later. Sometimes there's only one good example of an uncommon interaction, so it's hard to extrapolate how that sort of interaction ought to work in general. What seems right in the moment for that particular case might turn out to be a poor fit when the design space is …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pv"></a>**What does it mean when a Power's effects or Target Land shows (Terrain A)(Terrain B)? A or B? A next to B?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pv))
  <br><sub>All · Targeting, Terrain, Iconography, Uncanny Melting, Savage Mawbeasts, Devouring Ants</sub>
  > It means "Terrain A OR Terrain B." (Back page, Targeting section.) The division between them is supposed to be a sort of slash, but that's not especially clear.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8oh"></a>**When counting how many Invaders are in a land, are you counting pieces? Or do Towns count as 2 and Cities as 3?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8oh))
  <br><sub>All · Invaders, Health, Town, City, Powers Long Forgotten, Kingdom of Sweden</sub>
  > You're counting pieces. "Invader" in the Glossary is defined as "A City, Town, or Explorer", so a land with three Towns and a City has four Invaders in it. (A few effects instead key off of "N Health worth of Invaders", and in those cases Towns and Cities generally count for 2 and 3, respectively.) Similarly if …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p1"></a>**When does destroying a Town or City cause Fear?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p1))
  <br><sub>All · Destroy, Fear, Dahan, Ravage, Remove, Replace</sub>
  > Always, regardless of how it's destroyed: directly via a Power that says to destroy one, from damage done by a Power, from damage done by Dahan counterattacking, due to a Spirit's special rules, from a Fear or Event card, etc. [p. 18] Fear from destruction is independent of any explicit Fear the Power does – e.g., …
- [ ] <a id="q-does-damage-change-health"></a>**When you "Remove 3 Health worth of Invaders," can you Remove 3 damaged Towns?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/does-damage-change-health))
  <br><sub>All · Damage, Health, Seek Safety, Belief Takes Root, Flee the Pestilent Land, Depart the Dangerous Land</sub>
  > No, the Health of an Invader doesn't change when it takes Damage. Damaged Towns still count for 2 Health each. A few effects (e.g., Kingdom of England Level 5, or some Event/Fear cards) modify Health, which does affect how many Invaders you can Remove. But Damage does not reduce Health. (Health is simply the total …
- [ ] <a id="q-definition-of-discarding-a-card"></a>**When instructed to discard a card, which cards can you choose from? Where do those cards go?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/definition-of-discarding-a-card))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Discarding Powers as a randomizer, Choice Events, Turmoil's Touch (Wandering Voice Keens Delirium), Wandering Voice Keens Delirium, Overenthusiastic Arson (Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble), Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble</sub>
  > "Discard a card" without any other qualifiers means "put a Power Card from your hand into your discard pile." This most commonly occurs as part of paying for a Choice Event, but can also appear as a cost or detrimental effect. Some effects (most notably, the standard effects of Time Passes) discard Power Cards from …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfq5"></a>**If Oceans are in play, and you Weave Together an Ocean with a land, is the resulting joined land Coastal?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfq5))

A strict reading of the rules would say "only for Powers and Blight" (Ocean's Hungry Grasp) or "only for Blight removal/movement" (Scotland 5), because the joined land is not adjacent to itself, and "Coastal" means "adjacent to the Ocean". But that's super-wrong thematically, so it gets an …
- [ ] <a id="q-end-of-phase-multiple-triggered-actions"></a>**If the end of a Step or Phase triggers multiple Actions, are those Triggered Actions part of the same Action Tree?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/end-of-phase-multiple-triggered-actions))

  > No, they each start their own Action Tree.
