For instance, the Triggered Actions from Russia Level 6 each start their own Action Tree, each …
- [ ] <a id="q-what-are-your-actions"></a>**What are "your Actions"? (e.g., for "Responsibilities to the Dead" or "Spread Tumult and Delusion")** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/what-are-your-actions))
  <br><sub>Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth · Actions, Presence track, Triggered Actions, Responsibilities to the Dead (Finder of Paths Unseen), Finder of Paths Unseen, Intensify Aspect (Shifting Memory of Ages)</sub>
  > As a general guideline, anything done by your Spirit Panel or Power Cards, including any consequences directly following from the game rules (e.g., destroying Invaders/Dahan that have taken Damage equal to or greater than their Health, earning Fear from destroying Towns/Cities, destroying Presence / cascading when …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2g"></a>**What does it mean that a land "matches" an Invader card? Most places, it seems to mean "has the land type shown on the card" and nothing else, but the Notes on England (p. 27 JE) use it differently.** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2g))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Invader deck, Matches, Build, Kingdom of England</sub>
  > The p. 27 note on England (first JE printing) is incorrect – all "match" means is "matches the land type shown on the card". Limitations from the Invader Action are separate. The England example should instead say, "Invader Build Cards affect matching lands without Invaders if they are adjacent to at least 2 …

## Blighted Island cards

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqt"></a>**If there's only 1 Blight on the Healthy Island card, and you need to add 2 at once (eg: Sweden 1), what do you do?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqt))
  <br><sub>Core game · Blighted Island, Nested Actions, Blight, Timing, Kingdom of Sweden</sub>
  > Immediately flip the card; any Blight that was on it is added to the newly created Blight pool. If the Blight Card has any immediate effects, resolve them. Then add the 2 Blight to the board. Immediate effects of the flipped Blight card are nested actions - you resolve them completely (including anything they …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8r7"></a>**When the Blight card is flipped to the Blighted Island side, how much Blight gets added?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8r7))
  <br><sub>Core game · Blight, Blighted Island</sub>
  > If the new side says N Blight per player, you add N Blight per player more. The total amount of Blight on both sides will then be N+2 Blight per player, plus one Blight more (following this erratum). The amount of Blight on the base board is roughly balanced with the Blight cards - your total Blight pool is smaller, …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef6"></a>**Can you forget the card you gain from Aid to Lesser Spirits when you gain a Major Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef6))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Aid from Lesser Spirits</sub>
  > No. The card is "used as if played", but is not your card. (You cannot Forget it for any other reason, either.)
- [ ] <a id="q-repeating-aid-from-lesser-spirits-cards"></a>**Can you Repeat the Power Card granted by Aid from Lesser Spirits?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/repeating-aid-from-lesser-spirits-cards))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Repeat, Aid from Lesser Spirits, Powerstorm, Gift of Twinned Days, Gift of Abundance (Downpour Drenches the World), Gift of Strength (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Yes, but with some restrictions. - Anything that checks the cost of the Power Card checks its printed cost, which may be 1 Energy. - It's not one of your Power Cards in play, so you can't Repeat it with Gift of Twinned Days (but it doesn't stop you from repeating a more expensive Power Card). Triggered Repeats, like …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9c"></a>**How often do you apply the Blighted Island effects from Branch & Claw?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9c))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Blighted Island</sub>
  > If the Blight card says "Immediately", you do it once, as soon as the card is flipped. If it says "Every Spirit Phase", you do it every turn, during the Spirit Phase. If it says "At the start of each Invader Phase" or "Each Invader Phase," you do it every turn, during the Blighted Island part of the Invader Phase. …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oc6k"></a>**If the Blight card is Aid from Lesser Spirits and Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares is playing, is the Minor Power it gets subject to To Dream a Thousand Deaths?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oc6k))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Playing Power Cards, To Dream a Thousand Deaths (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Aid from Lesser Spirits</sub>
  > Yes. It says "as if played", so it obeys the Special Rules of the Spirit using it. Thematically, the minor spirits are working through Bringer of Dreams & Nightmares - it's why the Power targets through Bringer's Presence and why Bringer gains the Elements from it. (An alternate version which could target anywhere …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8re"></a>**The Blighted Island card Disintegrating Ecosystem adds Blight to the board. Where does that come from?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8re))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Disintegrating Ecosystem</sub>
  > The Blight comes from the Blight card, from the pool of 5 Blight per player that is created when the card flips. Unless explicitly stated otherwise, Blight is always added from the Blight card (not the box), and is returned to the Blight card when removed from the board.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pl"></a>**The Blighted Island card Disintegrating Ecosystem reads: "Immediately, on each board destroy 1 Beast and add 1 Blight to a land with a Town/City." Are both effects to the same land, or can the destroyed Beast come from anywhere on the board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pl))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Blighted Island, Disintegrating Ecosystem</sub>
  > The destroyed Beast can come from anywhere. (There should be a comma at the linebreak.)
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef5"></a>**When counting the number of power cards you have in play or have played (e.g., for "Draw Towards a Consuming Void"), do you count the extra Power Card from "Aid from Lesser Spirits"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef5))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw, Nature Incarnate · Non-elemental thresholds, Draw Towards a Consuming Void, Aid from Lesser Spirits, Boon of Vigor (River Surges in Sunlight), Gift of Seismic Energy (Dances Up Earthquakes), Earth Shudders, Buildings Fall (Dances Up Earthquakes)</sub>
  > No. The card is "used as if played," but is not in play. So with the extra card, you: - Can use the threshold of "Draw Towards a Consuming Void" if you have no other Power Cards in play; - Can't count the card towards extra Energy with "Boon of Vigor" or "Gift of Seismic Energy"; and - Can't count the card towards …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kblb"></a>**When exactly do you get the benefits from *Aid from Lesser Spirits*?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kblb))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Speed (Fast/Slow), Elements, Aid from Lesser Spirits</sub>
  > The new power card enters play as soon as the Blight card is flipped and the Spirits choose who gets which Minor Power. You gain the elements on it immediately, and can use it for thresholds that same turn. You can also use the Power that turn, although if it is a Fast power and the Blight card flipped after the Fast …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gefd"></a>**With the Event Outpaced, do you need to pay for a card from Aid from Lesser Spirits?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gefd))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Changed rulings, Outpaced, Aid from Lesser Spirits</sub>
  > No, you cannot pay for it to be sped up, nor can you discard it for Energy. It is "used as if played", but is not a card you played.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb7"></a>**How does All Things Weaken interact with Heart of the Wildfire? When Wildfire places Presence, does it then destroy its own Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb7))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Promo, Feather & Flame · Actions, Blight, Blazing Presence (Heart of the Wildfire), All Things Weaken, Destructive Nature (Heart of the Wildfire), Volcano Looming High</sub>
  > No. All Things Weaken modifies how Presence destruction from Blight works, but doesn't change the source of the Blight, so Wildfire is still protected from Blight added due Spirit actions. This also applies to the portion which reads "...and 1 Presence in an adjacent land": if you choose Wildfire's Presence as the 1 …
- [ ] <a id="q-why-remove-with-burn-brightest-befoe-the-end"></a>**Why does Burn Brighted Before the End have the option to remove Presence instead of adding Presence? Isn't adding Presence always better?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/why-remove-with-burn-brightest-befoe-the-end))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Promo, Feather & Flame · Blight, Blighted Island, Burn Brightest Before the End, Blazing Presence (Heart of the Wildfire), Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > In most instances, yes, adding Presence will be better. However, Heart of the Wildfire is often in a position where adding Presence to one of its lands will not only add more Blight, but also cascade – rather punishing for a Blighted Island card with so little Blight. Hence, there's an option to remove Presence from …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geao"></a>**Can you do the effects on a Blighted Island card in a different order than listed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geao))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Blighted Island, Unnatural Proliferation</sub>
  > No, do them in the order listed. For instance, with Unnatural Proliferation, you must add Presence (e.g., from Heart of the Wildfire) before adding Cities.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geb9"></a>**How does All Things Weaken interact with Stone's Unyielding Defiance's ability to protect Presence from destruction?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geb9))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Blight, Bestow the Endurance of Bedrock (Stone's Unyielding Defiance), All Things Weaken</sub>
  > If the conditions of Bestow the Endurance of Bedrock are met, no Presence is destroyed, either in that land or in an adjacent land. (All Things Weaken modifies how much and where Presence is destroyed, but Bestow the Endurance of Bedrock says "Presence is not destroyed", which makes those modifications irrelevant.) …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geba"></a>**If All Things Weaken is out, can the third level of Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will (Stone's Unyielding Defiance) save adjacent Presence from destruction?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geba))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Blight, Adding Presence, Hold the Island Fast with a Bulwark of Will (Stone's Unyielding Defiance), All Things Weaken</sub>
  > No. That innate threshold specifies "When a ... Blight card directly destroys Presence". All Things Weaken changes the rules for how Blight destroys Presence, but does not directly destroy Presence itself, it's adding Blight which does so.

## Fear and Fear Cards

- [ ] <a id="q-scapegoats-action"></a>**At Terror Level 2 and 3, how many actions is Scapegoats? It's very relevant against, eg, Russia Lvl 2.** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/scapegoats-action))
  <br><sub>Core game · Actions, Scapegoats, Tsardom of Russia, Observe the Ever-Changing World (Shifting Memory of Ages), Tall Tales of Savagery</sub>
  > One action per land with Towns/Cities - treat it as if it said: - Terror 2: In each land with (Town)/(City): Destroy 1 (Explorer) per (Town) and 2 (Explorer) per (City). - Terror 3: In each land with (Town)/(City): Destroy all (Explorer). Destroy 1 (Town) per (City). A strict reading of the Terror 2 text would be …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ocpc"></a>**At Terror Level III, can the Fear card Trade Suffers be used to replace a City with a Town in any land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ocpc))
  <br><sub>Core game · Trade Suffers</sub>
  > No, the modifier "in a Coastal land" applies whether you are downgrading a City or a Town.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9n"></a>**Can the same piece be moved multiple times with the same Event or Fear card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9n))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw · Events, Fear, Gather, Push, Retreat!, Dahan Enheartened</sub>
  > Yes, as long as any restrictions on the card are obeyed. For instance, the same Explorer can move multiple times with the Fear card 'Retreat!', and the same Beast can be moved multiple time with 'A Strange Madness among the Beasts'. If the card says players must make different choices, this must be obeyed.
- [ ] <a id="q-do-island-wide-effects-check-land-contents-at-resolution"></a>**Does Dahan on their Guard *(In each land, 1 Defense per Dahan)* provide Defense based on the number of Dahan at the time the Fear card was revealed or when the Ravage happens?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/do-island-wide-effects-check-land-contents-at-resolution))
  <br><sub>Core game · Defend, Dahan on their Guard, Belief Takes Root, Canny Defense, Avoid the Dahan, Trade Suffers</sub>
  > For island-wide effects like Dahan on their Guard, you check the number of Dahan there when the Invaders do damage (usually in the Ravage). This applies to all Event / Fear cards that affect all lands with (some contents) - you treat it as a game-wide rule for the turn, so you don't have to keep track of where pieces …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8ra"></a>**For the Dahan Raid and Dahan Enheartened fear cards, is the damage done to each Invader?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8ra))
  <br><sub>Core game · Dahan Raid, Dahan Enheartened</sub>
  > No, the damage listed is the total damage done. For instance, Dahan Raid at Terror Level 1 does 1 damage to 1 Invader. This is clarified in the second printing.
- [ ] <a id="q-overseas-trade-seems-safer-and-build-modifiers"></a>**How does Overseas Trade Seems Safer at Terror 2 ("Invaders do not Build Cities in Coastal Lands this turn") interact with effects that change what gets Built?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/overseas-trade-seems-safer-and-build-modifiers))
  <br><sub>Core game · Skipping Actions, Action Modifiers, Overseas Trade Seems Safer, Fathomless Mud of the Swamp</sub>
  > Check if a City would be Built before any modifiers to how the Build action would work. Overseas Trade Seems Safer skips the Build entirely, and you resolve all "skip this Action" effects before any "modify this Action" effects.
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28aus"></a>**If the effects of a Fear Card earn you another Fear Card, does the new card resolve during that Invader Phase or the next one?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28aus))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear, Fear Cards</sub>
  > It's resolved that Invader Phase. Add it to the bottom of the stack of cards you're resolving. (p. 9) If earning the card causes you to reach a new Terror Level, subsequent Fear Cards immediately start using the new Terror Level.
- [ ] <a id="q-fear-cards-checking-for-presence"></a>**When a Fear Card lets each player do something in a land with Presence / a Sacred Site, must it be the Presence / Sacred Site of the Spirit they are playing?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/fear-cards-checking-for-presence))
  <br><sub>Core game, Horizons of Spirit Island, Jagged Earth · Presence, Fear Cards, Theological Strife, Belief Takes Root, Fear of the Unseen</sub>
  > No, those Fear Cards check for any Presence / Sacred Site. When resolving the effects of a Fear Card, you're not making choices for your Spirit, which has no control over Fear effects. Rather, each player gets a choice so that the effects of the Fear Card scale properly with the number of players in the game. …
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28aut"></a>**When are earned Fear Cards revealed?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28aut))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear, Fear Cards</sub>
  > When resolved, during the next Invader Phase. (p. 9) At that time, you pick up the stack of earned Fear Cards, flip it over (which means you're reading them in the order earned), and resolve them one at a time, without looking ahead. If you earn more Fear Cards while so doing, they go on the bottom of the stack.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8q1"></a>**When Fear cards say "Each player may..." do the player choices have to happen in a space where they have presence or can they affect any space on the island?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8q1))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear</sub>
  > Any space on the island. Thematically, Fear effects are what the Invaders do as a result of their rising terror. (Or, sometimes, what the Dahan do when enheartened by the Invaders' faltering resolve.) While the players make the decisions, it's not the Spirits actually taking action.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p5"></a>**When you resolve a Fear card, do you resolve only the current level?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p5))
  <br><sub>Core game · Fear, Terror Level, Fear Cards</sub>
  > Yes, you only resolve the effect corresponding to the current Terror Level, not any previous levels. (p. 9) You resolve it at the current Terror Level, regardless of whether the Terror Level was lower when the card was earned.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qr"></a>**With the Fear card Overseas Trade Seems Safer at Terror level 2, what happens if a City would be built in a Coastal land?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qr))
  <br><sub>Core game · Overseas Trade Seems Safer</sub>
  > Nothing is built: no City, and also no Town. If a Town would normally be built, it is built as normal.
- [ ] <a id="q-how-mandatory-are-fear-cards"></a>**Are the effects of Fear Cards mandatory?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/how-mandatory-are-fear-cards))
  <br><sub>All, Horizons of Spirit Island · Fear Cards</sub>
  > Yes, the effects of Fear Cards are not under the control of the Spirits, and players only get to break ties between equally-effective ways of resolving the card. That being said, some effects give the players a bit more control: - If a choice is explicitly granted to the a player (e.g., "Each player chooses a …
- [ ] <a id="q-overseas-trade-seems-safer-and-disease"></a>**How does Overseas Trade Seems Safer at Terror 2 ("Invaders do not Build Cities in Coastal Lands this turn") interact with Disease?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/overseas-trade-seems-safer-and-disease))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Disease, Overseas Trade Seems Safer</sub>
  > Treat Overseas Trade Seems Safer as skipping the Build if a City would get built (ie, Towns &gt; Cities). So for Disease, you can choose to apply the Overseas Trade skip first, preserving the Disease token.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gbg5"></a>**If a Fear cards tells you to add Strife and then do something to pieces with Strife, does that affect just the newly-added Strife?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gbg5))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Strife, Unrest, Dahan Threaten, Discord, Panic, Slave Rebellion</sub>
  > No, it affects all pieces with Strife anywhere on the board, whether or not you put a new token on it. Remember that Strife, unlike the other tokens, is always assigned to a particular Invader.
- [ ] <a id="q-ravage-damage-modifiers-that-check-for-strife"></a>**Does Beset by Many Troubles count Strife being removed in the current Ravage Action?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/ravage-damage-modifiers-that-check-for-strife))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Strife, Ravage, Ordering, Beset by Many Troubles, Cooperation Among Towns</sub>
  > Yes. While this doesn't strictly follow from the rules (p. 7 Jagged Earth), it's both thematically correct and how most players intuitively resolve the Fear Card. (If similar things come up in the future, we may tweak the Ravage sequence, but for 1 card it's less disruptive to just say "yes, you get this benefit".) …

## Setup

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kaak"></a>**Are some Island tiles better than others for your first games?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kaak))
  <br><sub>Core game · Island setup</sub>
  > As long as you are using the balanced (non-thematic) side, the Island tiles are all pretty equivalent, and you should just pick them randomly.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8nh"></a>**Can every Spirit be played in a solo game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nh))
  <br><sub>Core game · Solo games, Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares</sub>
  > Yes, it is possible to play and win with each Spirit in a solo game. It is possible to win even with Spirits with special restrictions like Ocean's Hungry Grasp or Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares. Some Spirits will be harder to win with than others (particularly against certain Adversaries), but the difficult …
- [ ] <a id="q-Can-one-person-play-more-than-one-Spirit"></a>**Can one person play more than one Spirit?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Can-one-person-play-more-than-one-Spirit))
  <br><sub>Promo 2, Core game, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Player count, Solo games, A Diversity of Spirits, Destiny Unfolds</sub>
  > Practically speaking, sure. Because the game doesn’t usually have secret information that only one player knows, one person can play as many Spirits as they’d like. However, that one person is taking on the roles of multiple Players, e.g., simultaneously making choices for the Red Player, the Blue Player, and the …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8oz"></a>**Can you play 5-6 players with two copies of the base game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8oz))
  <br><sub>Core game · Player count, Island setup</sub>
  > Yes, with some caveats. You will need Presence disks and reminder tokens in different player colors. We also strongly recommend your first play or two be with 4 or fewer players per game. 5-6p games are fine when everyone involved knows the game well, but with newer players there's just an overwhelming amount of …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka5b"></a>**Does Blight added during Setup come from the box or from the Blight Pool?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka5b))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo · Blight, Kingdom of Sweden, Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > All Blight added during Setup comes from the box. Unless specifically stated otherwise, the Blight Pool does not start out depleted. This applies to Blight added due to printed icons on the board (p. 6), an Invader's setup (like Sweden level 6) or a Spirit's setup (like Heart of the Wildfire).
- [ ] <a id="q-Spirit-with-multiple-ways-to-set-up"></a>**If a Spirit has multiple legal ways to set up on the island, can it choose the one it likes best?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/Spirit-with-multiple-ways-to-set-up))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Spirit setup, Thunderspeaker, A Spread of Rampant Green, Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Sharp Fangs behind the Leaves, Stone's Unyielding Defiance</sub>
  > Yes, when there are multiple choices that equally meet the setup conditions, the player gets to choose the one they like. For some Spirits, this will only happen on the thematic side or against specific Adversaries/Scenarios.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8pg"></a>**What are the options for making the map for a 2-player game?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8pg))
  <br><sub>Core game · 2-player games, Island setup, Guard the Isle's Heart</sub>
  > On the balanced maps, the standard layout given in the rulebook might be called "bottom to bottom", with one ocean in the upper-left and one in the bottom-right. But you can put together the maps any way they fit, with some variation in the game dynamics and (slightly) overall difficulty. Some examples: if you put …
- [ ] <a id="q-what-is-during-play"></a>**What counts as the end of Setup for “During Play” Adversary/Scenario effects? And how are they different from “After Setup” effects?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/what-is-during-play))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Guard the Isle's Heart, Kingdom of France, Varied Terrains, Despicable Theft, Heart of the Wildfire, Kingdom of Scotland</sub>
  > During Play effects do not affect Setup, but do affect normal gameplay. Setup normally ends with the initial Explore, with normal gameplay starting with the first Spirit Phase, but some Adversaries and Scenarios have Setup instructions that happen after the initial Explore and before the game starts. There is no …
- [ ] <a id="q-two-setup-instructions-placing-presence-in-the-same-land"></a>**Can two Presence setup instructions place Presence into the same land? (e.g., Shadows Flicker Like Flames putting 2 Presence in the highest-numbered Jungle and 1 Presence in land #5 when land #5 is the highest-numbered Jungle)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/two-setup-instructions-placing-presence-in-the-same-land))
  <br><sub>All · Island setup, Presence, Shadows Flicker Like Flame, Sharp Fangs behind the Leaves, Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble, Vengeance as a Burning Plague</sub>
  > Yes. There is no requirement that multiple Presence-placement instructions happen in different lands. In some cases (like with Shadows Flicker Like Flames), this can set up more than two Presence in one land. This extra Presence is generally not immediately useful, but gives some buffer against losing that Sacred …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcwa"></a>**Is the initial Explore considered part of Setup *(for, e.g., France Level 1)*?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcwa))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw · Explore, Kingdom of France</sub>
  > Yes, the initial Explore is the very last part of setup (unless otherwise modified). France Level 6 (Persistent Explorers) applies, as does anything else that keys off of resolving "an Explore Card". (It is not 'the Explore Step', which is a structural part of the Invader Phase.)
- [ ] <a id="q-impossible-setup-on-the-thematic-map"></a>**When setting up a Spirit, what happens if the setup instructions are impossible (e.g., adding Presence to a land with Blight when that board has no Blight).** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/impossible-setup-on-the-thematic-map))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Nature Incarnate · Thematic map, Spirit setup, Vengeance as a Burning Plague, Starlight Seeks Its Form, Stone's Unyielding Defiance, Fractured Days Split the Sky</sub>
  > Either don't start that Spirit on that board or follow the instructions as best as you can, choosing freely when no lands meet the criteria given. [Jagged Earth Rulebook p. 6] When choosing freely, use theme as your guide — if you're in this situation, you're likely playing on the thematic map, after all!
- [ ] <a id="q-checking-for-adjacent-lands-in-setup"></a>**If a Spirit's setup instructions reference the distance to other lands (e.g., Ember-Eyed Behemoth looking for a Wetland adjacent to a Jungle), does this check for lands on other boards?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/checking-for-adjacent-lands-in-setup))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Spirit setup, Ember-Eyed Behemoth</sub>
  > Yes. Ember-Eyed Behemoth's highest-numbered Wetlands adjacent to a Jungle could be adjacent to a Jungle on another board (e.g., Wetlands F7 adjacent to Jungle D6 in the standard three-board layout).

## Spirit Phase

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g7z1"></a>**Can I wait for the results of another Spirit's Growth before making my own Growth choice? E.g., see if they draw into a Power Card which handles a bad situation before making my own choices?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g7z1))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Promo · Growth, Timing, Gaining Power Cards, Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds, Heart of the Wildfire</sub>
  > Yes. The only rules constraints are that each individual Spirit must Grow / Gain Energy / Play Power Cards in that order, and that all Spirits must complete the Spirit Phase before anyone starts resolving Fast Powers. [p. 8] Other than that, players play simultaneously and may confer as they wish, so for instance, …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8qz"></a>**Can you use Energy you gain during the Fast Power Phase (e.g., from Boon of Vigor) to pay for cards you play that turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8qz))
  <br><sub>Core game · Energy, Playing Power Cards, Boon of Vigor (River Surges in Sunlight)</sub>
  > No, you cannot. You need to pay for all Powers when you play them, during the 'Play and pay for Power Cards' part of the Spirit Phase at the beginning of the turn. The Spirit Phase needs to be entirely completed before you start the Fast Power Phase, when you would gain the energy from cards like Boon of Vigor. …
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28aul"></a>**Do I have to do everything shown in a Growth option I pick?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28aul))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo, Feather & Flame, Promo 2, Nature Incarnate · Growth, Repositioning Presence, Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Heart of the Wildfire, Downpour Drenches the World, Ember-Eyed Behemoth</sub>
  > Yes. You must do everything shown if possible, though you can do it in any order. Usually, there's no downside: Reclaiming, gaining Energy/Power Cards, and adding Presence are almost strictly good. (You can always reposition a Presence already on the island when adding Presence [p. 14], so "running out" isn't a …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8oq"></a>**How does one move Presence during Growth?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8oq))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo · Growth, Presence, Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island</sub>
  > When a Growth choice says "Move Presence" (with a curved arrow), move one of your Presence up to the shown number of lands away from where it currently lies. When the Growth option says "Add Presence", but instead of adding from your Presence track you choose to add one already on the board (per the p. 14 sidebar), …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p3"></a>**If you have a Growth option which lets place two Presence, can you chain them, placing the second one within range of the first one?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p3))
  <br><sub>Core game · Presence, Growth, Thunderspeaker, Lightning's Swift Strike, River Surges in Sunlight</sub>
  > Yes, you can do this. In general, after you choose a Growth option, you do everything under that option, in any order you wish. (p. 8)
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od08"></a>**When you reposition Presence on the board rather than adding new Presence, where can you measure the range from?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od08))
  <br><sub>Core game · Growth, Repositioning Presence, Presence, Gift of Proliferation (A Spread of Rampant Green), Overgrow in a Night (A Spread of Rampant Green), Indomitable Claim</sub>
  > You can measure the range from any Presence, including the one you are about to reposition. The sequence of events for adding Presence during growth, you - Choose a land to add Presence to. This must be measured from your Presence on the board at that moment. - Add a Presence to that land. Normally this is picked up …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8p4"></a>**When your Presence track gives you "Any" element, when do you have to choose what element that is? Can you change what it is during the turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8p4))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Promo, Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Elements, "Any" element, Timing, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island, Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble</sub>
  > You can wait until the middle of your turn to choose what it is, but once you've set it for a turn, it can't change that turn [base rulebook p. 14, last item under Presence Tracks] This holds true even if you're restricted on which Elements you can choose (e.g., Wounded Waters Bleeding having a "Water / Animal" space …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6oegz"></a>**With the growth of A Spread of Rampant Green, can I gain a power card and then choose where to use the first (non-optional) growth option?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6oegz))
  <br><sub>Core game · Growth, Ordering, A Spread of Rampant Green, Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > Yes, you can choose where to place your Jungle/Wetlands presence based on a power card you gain that turn. This is more explicit in the Branch & Claw rulebook, where there are more spirits with multiple growth choices.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od05"></a>**With Spirits that choose more than 1 Growth option, can I see the results of one choice before picking the next?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od05))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Promo, Feather & Flame, Jagged Earth · Growth, Sharp Fangs behind the Leaves, Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island, Grinning Trickster Stirs Up Trouble, Starlight Seeks Its Form</sub>
  > Yes — you can completely finish resolving your first Growth choice before making your second choice.
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od06"></a>**With Spirits that get to choose multiple Growth options, if more than one option has "Gain Power Card", can I gain a Power Card *then* pick which Growth choice I was using?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od06))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Growth, Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds, Sharp Fangs behind the Leaves, Starlight Seeks Its Form</sub>
  > No. You choose a set of things to do, then do them all. If you have multiple Growth choices, you then do this again, and cannot choose one you already picked this turn.
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9p"></a>**What happens if Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island is at its Presence limit and attempts to add Presence?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9p))
  <br><sub>Promo · Growth, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island, Unrelenting Growth, Gift of Proliferation (A Spread of Rampant Green)</sub>
  > It cannot add more Presence, by its special rule, so nothing happens. (True for both Growth and Presence-adding Powers.) It may choose to instead reposition a Presence already on the island (sidebar, p. 14).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebe"></a>**Can you use a Presence track action in the middle of Growth?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebe))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Growth, Presence track, Actions, Downpour Drenches the World, Shroud of Silent Mist, Many Minds Move as One</sub>
  > Yes, so long it's in between actions (or before choosing a Growth option). Generally, each icon is one Action(†). For instance, Downpour Drenches the World could choose its second growth option (which has 2 "Add a Presence" icons), add 1 Presence to uncover a "Move Presence 1" space, use the Move 1 to shift a …

## Elements and Elemental Thresholds

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8rg"></a>**Can I use an Innate Power more than once a turn, if I meet the threshold multiple times?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8rg))
  <br><sub>Core game · Elements</sub>
  > In general, no. Usually a power can only be used once a turn. Some powers (e.g. Powerstorm) allow you to repeat a power card. Other powers let you repeat themselves, via threshold effect (e.g. Creepers Tear into Mortar, the innate power of Spread of Rampant Green). But having extra elements doesn't let you do …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6od0c"></a>**Can the different thresholds of an innate power be used separately?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6od0c))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Core game, Promo, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Elements, Lightning's Swift Strike, A Spread of Rampant Green, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island, Shadows Flicker Like Flame, Thunderspeaker</sub>
  > No, the thresholds are all part of a single power. All effects must be used on the same target land (or same target Spirit), and you use the effects of every threshold you meet{1}, in the order they're printed{2}. For example, Lightning's Swift Strike's innate power of Thundering Destruction used with 5 Fire, 4 Air, …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kcmx"></a>**If I gain an Element in the middle of resolving a Power, may I use that Element to gain a later threshold?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kcmx))
  <br><sub>Core game, Branch & Claw, Promo 2, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · Elements, Unrelenting Growth, Indomitable Claim, Slip the Flow of Time (Fractured Days Split the Sky), Fractured Days Split the Sky, Serpent Wakes in Power (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Yes, you may. This can happen, e.g., if the first part of Indomitable Claim reveals an Element on a Presence track, or if the land gets Blighted and the card is Aid from Lesser Spirits. Likewise if you lose Elements in the middle of resolving the Power, you may lose further thresholds. Note that any parts of the …
- [ ] <a id="q-choosing-flexible-elements"></a>**If you gain a flexible Element (e.g., an "Any" Element, or multiple Elements separated by slashes) from something other than a Presence Track, when do you choose which Element to gain?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/choosing-flexible-elements))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Promo, Feather & Flame, Nature Incarnate · "Any" element, Elements, Growth, Presence track, Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island</sub>
  > When directly gaining flexible Elements from an Action mid-turn, like from "Absorb Essence" or Lure of the Deep Wilderness’ 3rd Growth option, you must choose a specific Element immediately. Passive Elements on Presence Tracks or in Bonus Spaces can be chosen at any time. [Core rulebook, p. 14, last item under …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8rf"></a>**If you gain presence during the turn (after Growth) and uncover the "any" or another element, can you then use it for other powers?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8rf))
  <br><sub>Core game · "Any" element, Elements, Adding Presence, Indomitable Claim, Growth Through Sacrifice, Unrelenting Growth</sub>
  > Yes, you can start using elements as soon as you gain them. (p. 14) Similarly, if you are able to play a card mid-turn, you get the effects of its Elements immediately. (And if some effect were to cover an Element by returning Presence to your track, or destroy a Power card in play, you'd immediately lose access to …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kavs"></a>**When I Repeat a Major Power, if I got the threshold effect the first time, do I get it on the repetition as well?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kavs))
  <br><sub>Core game · Elements, Repeat, Powerstorm, Gift of Strength (Vital Strength of the Earth)</sub>
  > Yes, usually. Elements are not "used up" when you reach a threshold, so you still have the elements on the repetition. The only exception would be if you lose the elements before the repetition (e.g., you Forgot a card in play granting the elements).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8o1"></a>**Are elements personal, or can you use elements from other spirits?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8o1))
  <br><sub>All · Elements, Elemental Boon, Gift of Nature's Connection, Spirits May Yet Dream (Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares), Bringer of Dreams and Nightmares, Absorb Essence (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island)</sub>
  > Elements can only be used by the spirit that played them; they can't be shared unless an effect explicitly says so. For example, if you are playing River Surges in Sunlight, you cannot use Water generated by cards played by Ocean's Hungry Grasp. There are a few power cards that grant Elements to other Spirits, like …
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5ka9h"></a>**Can you use the threshold effects of Innate Powers, even if you can't use the effects above them?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5ka9h))
  <br><sub>All · Elements, Sparking Aspect (Lightning's Swift Strike), Haven Aspect (River Surges in Sunlight), Foreboding Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Might Aspect (Vital Strength of the Earth), Gather the Warriors (Thunderspeaker)</sub>
  > Yes, each line is evaluated independently, depending on the Elements you activate the power with. (You are allowed to pretend you have fewer Elements than you actually have, but need to be consistent for the whole power.)
- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kavq"></a>**When you hit the threshold of a Major Power, is the effect in addition to the base effect?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kavq))
  <br><sub>All · Elements, The Jungle Hungers, Tsunami, Vigor of the Breaking Dawn, Entwined Power, Tigers Hunting</sub>
  > Yes, unless the card says "instead", threshold effects are in addition to the base effect [p. 17].
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geag"></a>**Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power can let you use a Major with all elemental thresholds. Does this let you use the threshold on Draw Towards a Consuming Void?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geag))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw · Non-elemental thresholds, Unlock the Gates of Deepest Power, Draw Towards a Consuming Void</sub>
  > No, because the threshold checks something that isn't an element (how many other Power Cards you have in play).
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6og2h"></a>**If you gain Elements from a Growth option, do they go away at the end of the turn?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6og2h))
  <br><sub>Promo 2, Jagged Earth · Elements, Growth, Fractured Days Split the Sky, Lure of the Deep Wilderness, Downpour Drenches the World</sub>
  > Yes. (Core rulebook, p. 10)

## Range and Targeting

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gef8"></a>**Can you place Presence during Growth based on range from your Presence in the Ocean?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gef8))
  <br><sub>Core game · Ocean's Hungry Grasp, Ocean In Play (Ocean's Hungry Grasp)</sub>
  > Yes, even though the Oceans are not a land at that time (since Growth is not a Power or Blight). Once Presence is in the Ocean, it is treated normally until/unless the Ocean is no longer in play (because Ocean's Hungry Grasp loses all Presence on that board).
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebo"></a>**Can you target a power on a land where you know it won't do anything? For instance, target a land without Explorers with a "Push 1 Explorer" Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebo))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Branch & Claw, Core game · Targeting, Scream Disease Into the Wind, Mists Shift and Flow (Shroud of Silent Mist), Shroud of Silent Mist, Blitz</sub>
  > Yes. You can target a power on any land, as long as it satisfies the targeting and range restrictions. ("Do as much as you can" only refers to resolving a Power, not how you use it in the first place.) Obvious reasons you might want to do this include Gathering Presence (if you're Shroud of Silent Mist) or placing …
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28aqg"></a>**Do Range buffs apply to Powers that affect "a land with your Presence" or "one of your lands"?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28aqg))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo, Branch & Claw · Range, Presence, Targeting, Reaching Grasp, Sky Stretches to Shore, Flow Like Water, Reach Like Air</sub>
  > No. "A land with your Presence" specifies a requirement for the land you're affecting (similar to, eg, "a land with Dahan" or "a land with Blight"), not a numeric Range. While it is usually the same in practice as "a land at Range 0", when using effects that modify Range the distinction matters. For powers like …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8nq"></a>**Does Shadows of the Dahan ("...you may pay 1 Energy to target a land with Dahan regardless of the Power's Range") let you ignore other targeting requirements?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nq))
  <br><sub>Core game, Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Range, Targeting, Shadows of the Dahan (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Finder of Paths Unseen, Reach Aspect (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine)</sub>
  > No. Only Range - the number of lands distant - is ignored. All other targeting requirements still apply. This includes source restrictions, so if, e.g., a Power must be targeted from a Sacred Site and you have none, Shadows of the Dahan won't let you use it, or if it needs to be targeted from a Jungle, you need …
- [ ] <a id="q-is-a-land-adjacent-to-or-within-range-of-itself"></a>**Is a land adjacent to itself? Is it within Range 1 of itself? Can it be the nearest land to itself that meets a particular condition? Does Isolate change that?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/is-a-land-adjacent-to-or-within-range-of-itself))
  <br><sub>Core game, Jagged Earth, Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Isolate, Push, Gather, Range, Hazards Spread Across the Island, Sunset's Fire Flows Across the Land</sub>
  > No, yes, yes, and no. In particular: - A land is never adjacent to itself, not even in particularly unusual cases. - A land is always at exactly Range 0 of itself, and therefore "within Range 1," "within Range 2," etc. - If a land meets a particular condition (e.g., "with Dahan"), it's its own "nearest land" meeting …
- [ ] <a id="q-special-rule-range-bonuses-to-targeting"></a>**When a special rule grants a Range boost specifically for targeting lands with specific traits/contents, like "Shadows of the Dahan," does that apply to Range arrows within a Power's text?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/special-rule-range-bonuses-to-targeting))
  <br><sub>Core game, Nature Incarnate, Branch & Claw · Range Boosts, Shadows of the Dahan (Shadows Flicker Like Flame), Shadows Flicker Like Flame, Encircle Aspect (Sharp Fangs Behind the Leaves), Shadow-Touched Realm (Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine), Breath of Darkness Down Your Spine</sub>
  > No, that bonus is specifically for choosing the target of a land-targeting Power. Use the Power as though the number in the "Range" box were increased or completely ignored (depending on the Range boost in question) and the extra target requirement were included in the "Target Land" box. In the case of Shadows of the …
- [ ] <a id="q-breaking-range-or-target-requirements-in-the-middle-of-a-power"></a>**Does a Power stop resolving if you can no longer meet the Range/Target requirements in the middle of using it? (e.g., because it moves Presence out of the origin land)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/breaking-range-or-target-requirements-in-the-middle-of-a-power))
  <br><sub>All · Range, Targeting, Timing, Ordering, Focus the Sun’s Rays (Relentless Gaze of the Sun), Relentless Gaze of the Sun</sub>
  > No. Range and target requirements are only checked as part of targeting the Power, before resolving any of its text effects.
- [ ] <a id="q-timing-of-checking-range-and-target-for-powers-that-add-ongoing-effects"></a>**If a Spirit's Power adds an ongoing effect in a land (e.g., Defend), does that effect go away if the Spirit can no longer meet the Range & Target requirements, or any conditional requirements in the Power?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/timing-of-checking-range-and-target-for-powers-that-add-ongoing-effects))
  <br><sub>All · Range, Targeting, Defend, Timing</sub>
  > No, Range and Target requirements are only checked at the start of using a Power, and conditional requirements are checked when you reach that line in the effect text. Once the Power has added the ongoing effect in a specific land, that effect remains until Time Passes. Powers that alter an entire class of things, …
- [ ] <a id="q-range-in-text-of-powers-that-target-spirits"></a>**Where is Range in the text of a Spirit-targeting Power measured from?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/range-in-text-of-powers-that-target-spirits))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth, Feather & Flame, Promo · Range, Unleash a Torrent of the Self's Own Essence, Serpent Wakes in Power (Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island), Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island, Blazing Renewal, Unleash a Torrent of the Self's Own Essence</sub>
  > From the presence of the Spirit following the instruction (usually target Spirit) unless the Power explicitly says otherwise. Use the Range bonuses (if any) of the Spirit using the Power, which may not be the Spirit measuring Range. To cover specific cases: - For "Serpent Wakes in Power," each Spirit adds Presence …
- [ ] <a id="q-range-checks-in-the-text-of-land-targeting-powers-measure-from-target-land"></a>**When choosing Beasts to move with Flocking Red-Talons, which land do you measure Range from?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/range-checks-in-the-text-of-land-targeting-powers-measure-from-target-land))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Jagged Earth, Feather & Flame · Range, Flocking Red-Talons, Inspire the Release of Stolen Lands, Ravaged Undergrowth Slithers Back to Life, Utter a Curse of Dread and Bone, Finder of Paths Unseen</sub>
  > The target land. All text in land-targeting Powers is resolved in relation to the target land unless otherwise specified.

## Play Options

- [ ] <a id="q-9v5kfqz"></a>**Playing without Events - when Commanding Beasts, can you bounce back and forth between lands?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.9v5kfqz))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Playing Without Events, Actions</sub>
  > Formally: no, you have to command all Beasts in a single land before moving on to the next. (Everything done by Beasts in a single land is a single Event Action.) Practically, this only matters in unusual cases (like vs. Russia), so if it aids ease of play it's usually OK to let each player handle their board …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gecx"></a>**When combining Adversaries, how do you handle Escalation on Stage 3 Invader Cards when the Escalation cares about terrain? (France, Sweden)** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gecx))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Combining Adversaries, Stage II escalation, Kingdom of France, Kingdom of Sweden</sub>
  > Choose one of the two land types randomly. (JE p. 19) Don't use "top terrain" or "bottom terrain"; they not equally distributed. If there's no other convenient way, spin the card and use the terrain that's closest to pointing at the Adversary panel. If you just apply the Escalation to both Explored terrains, Sweden's …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4gebv"></a>**When playing on an Archipelago, can Many Minds Move as one Gather/Push Beasts from a coastal land on one board to a "Range 2 away" coastal land on another board?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4gebv))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Range, Archipelago, Fly Fast As Thought (Many Minds Move as One)</sub>
  > Yes.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geax"></a>**When playing without Events, can you use the Stage II "Command Beasts" card during Invader Stage III?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geax))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Playing Without Events, Beasts, Kingdom of Scotland, Tsardom of Russia</sub>
  > Yes, you can use the Stage II Commanding Beasts any time after it is first unlocked. Likewise, for an Invader that switches back and forth between Stage II and Stage III (e.g., the Tsardom of Russia at Level 4+), you can use the Stage III "Command Beasts" card anytime after you first reach Stage III.
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4geat"></a>**When playing without Events, what type of Action is Commanding Beasts? Can you use your special rules to modify what the Beasts do?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4geat))
  <br><sub>Jagged Earth · Actions, Playing Without Events, Beasts, Sharp Fangs behind the Leaves, Ally of the Beasts (Sharp Fangs Behind the Leaves), Many Minds Move as One</sub>
  > It is a separate Event Action for each land with Beasts (JE p. 20). A Spirit's special rules generally don't apply, since they usually only affect their Powers in particular or their Spirit Actions in general. For example, Sharp Fangs Behind the Leaves can move their Presence with the commanded Beasts, since their …
- [ ] <a id="q-coastal-lands-card-with-kingdom-of-scotland-and-habsburg-mining-expedition"></a>**Is it possible for "Untapped Salt Deposits" (Habsburg Mining Expedition Level 4) to combine with "Seize Opportunity" (The Kingdom of Scotland Level 2), since they both alter what happens to the “Coastal Lands” card?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/coastal-lands-card-with-kingdom-of-scotland-and-habsburg-mining-expedition))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Combining Adversaries, Kingdom of Scotland, Coastal Lands invader card, Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>
  > Yes, just set up the “Coastal Lands” card according to "Seize Opportunity" (as the third Stage II card). Both Adversary effects remove the "Coastal Lands” card before randomizing the Stage II Invader Cards – "Untapped Salt Deposits" just doesn’t do anything special with it afterwards, while "Seize Opportunity" puts …
- [ ] <a id="q-gathering-when-combining-habsburgs"></a>**When combining the two Habsburg Adversaries, when does Migratory Herders (Livestock Colony Level 1) Gather check which lands match the Salt Deposits card (Mining Expedition Level 4)?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/gathering-when-combining-habsburgs))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate · Combining Adversaries, Matches, Timing, Habsburg Monarchy (Livestock Colony), Habsburg Mining Expedition</sub>

Builds in the non-Mining Lands might turn them into Mining Lands before Towns are Gathered, reducing the number of lands where Migratory Herders does anything. The list of lands that will be Gathered into is fixed at …

## Presence and Sacred Sites

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8nw"></a>**Can cascading Blight kill Presence twice?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nw))
  <br><sub>Core game · Blight, Presence</sub>
  > Yes, it can. Each time you add one or more Blight to a given land - including cascades - destroy 1 Presence from every Spirit there. (p. 15) So if you had a hideously bad cascade that added Blight to 4 different lands, in each of those 4 lands you'd destroy a Presence from each Spirit. In the very odd case that you …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8nv"></a>**Can Presence tokens destroyed by Blight be added to the island?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8nv))
  <br><sub>Core game · Presence, Growth, Destroyed Presence</sub>
  > Only if an effect explicitly says to add "destroyed Presence". Very few effects currently let you add destroyed Presence, like the "Steady Regeneration" special rule for A Spread of Rampant Green or the major power "Blazing Renewal."
- [ ] <a id="q-3y28auk"></a>**Can you add or move Presence into lands with Blight?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.3y28auk))
  <br><sub>Core game · Presence, Blight, Growth</sub>
  > Yes. Blight destroys Presence only when the Blight is added. If a Spirit is so sensitive to Blight that it has trouble adding Presence to lands having any, its Growth choices will explicitly reflect that. (e.g., Keeper of the Forbidden Wilds, Serpent Slumbering Beneath the Island) To draw a parallel: if you're in a …
- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8oc"></a>**If all my Presence from my Spirit Panel is placed on the board, how do I add more?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8oc))
  <br><sub>Core game · Presence, Growth, Destroyed Presence</sub>
  > Whenever adding Presence, instead of taking from your Presence Tracks you can use Presence (of yours) already on the island instead. [sidebar, p. 14]. You can take it out of any land, including the land you're measuring Range from. While this option is most often relevant if your Presence tracks are empty, it's not a …
- [ ] <a id="q-removing-and-replacing-presence"></a>**What happens to Presence when it gets removed or replaced?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/removing-and-replacing-presence))
  <br><sub>Nature Incarnate, Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth, Promo 2, Feather & Flame · Replace, Presence, Remove, Bargain, Destroyed Presence, Transform to a Murderous Darkness</sub>
  > If it is removed or replaced during play, it goes to the destroyed Presence pile in the supply unless the particular effect says to put it elsewhere. From there, it can be restored by any effect that returns destroyed Presence to the island. Anything removed or replaced during setup is out of the game. As of the …
- [ ] <a id="q-bu6ocb8"></a>**When Blight is moved into a land, does it destroy Presence or cause cascades?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.bu6ocb8))
  <br><sub>Branch & Claw, Jagged Earth · Presence, Blight, Absorb Corruption, Flow Like Water, Reach Like Air, Flow Downriver, Blow Downwind, Skies Herald the Season of Return</sub>
  > No, Blight only destroys Presence when it is added to the board, not when it is moved from one land to another. Likewise, moving Blight does not cause cascades. [B&C p. 15, JE p. 28]

## ~ World and Lore

- [ ] <a id="q-7w4g8rs"></a>**How do you pronounce "Dahan", anyway?** ([FAQ](https://querki.net/raw/darker/spirit-island-faq/.7w4g8rs))
  <br><sub>Core game · Dahan</sub>
  > da-HAN, with long As. (The closest English equivalent is the "a" in "father".) There is slightly more stress on the second syllable.
